App.LocalPlayer = App.Services.Players.LocalPlayer
function App.stopAntiGuard()
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiGuardState
    if not state then
        return
    end
    state.Installed = false
    if state.Thread and task and type(task.cancel) == "function" then
        pcall(task.cancel, state.Thread)
    end
    state.Thread = nil
    for _, connection in pairs(state.Connections or {}) do
        pcall(function()
            connection:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    state.Connections = {}
    for _, connection in pairs(state.CharacterConnections or {}) do
        pcall(function()
            connection:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    state.CharacterConnections = {}
    for instance, collisionGroup in pairs(state.OriginalCollisionGroups or {}) do
        if instance and instance.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                instance.CollisionGroup = collisionGroup
            end)
        end
    end
    state.OriginalCollisionGroups = {}
    App.stopGuardHitboxProtection()
    local module = App.Runtime.AntiGuardState.RagdollModule
    if module then
        for methodName, original in pairs(state.OriginalRagdollMethods or {}) do
            pcall(function()
                module[methodName] = original
            end)
        end
    end
    state.OriginalRagdollMethods = {}
    if state.Network and state.OriginalNetworkFire then
        pcall(function()
            state.Network.Fire = state.OriginalNetworkFire
        end)
    end
    if state.Network and state.OriginalNetworkInvoke then
        pcall(function()
            state.Network.Invoke = state.OriginalNetworkInvoke
        end)
    end
    if state.EggCmds and state.OriginalRequestDropHeldAreaEgg then
        pcall(function()
            state.EggCmds.RequestDropHeldAreaEgg = state.OriginalRequestDropHeldAreaEgg
        end)
    end
    state.OriginalNetworkFire = nil
    state.OriginalNetworkInvoke = nil
    state.OriginalRequestDropHeldAreaEgg = nil
    state.Network = nil
    state.EggCmds = nil
    state.RagdollModule = nil
end

function App.restoreAntiHitCharacter(character)
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiHitState
    if not state then return end
    for instance, backup in pairs(state.OriginalCollision or {}) do
        if instance and instance.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                instance.CanTouch = backup.CanTouch
                instance.CanQuery = backup.CanQuery
            end)
        end
        state.OriginalCollision[instance] = nil
    end
    for humanoid, backup in pairs(state.OriginalHumanoidStates or {}) do
        if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
            for stateType, enabled in pairs(backup) do
                pcall(function() humanoid:SetStateEnabled(stateType, enabled) end)
            end
        end
        state.OriginalHumanoidStates[humanoid] = nil
    end
end
function App.applyAntiHitCharacter(character)
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiHitState
    if not state or not character then return end
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
            if state.OriginalCollision[descendant] == nil then
                state.OriginalCollision[descendant] = {
                    CanTouch = descendant.CanTouch,
                    CanQuery = descendant.CanQuery,
                }
            end
            descendant.CanTouch = false
            descendant.CanQuery = false
        end
    end
    if humanoid then
        if state.OriginalHumanoidStates[humanoid] == nil then
            state.OriginalHumanoidStates[humanoid] = {
                [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics] = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics),
                [Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding] = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding),
                [Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown] = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown),
                [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll] = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll),
                [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated] = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated),
            }
        end
        pcall(function()
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, false)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
            if humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
            then
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
        end)
    end
end
function App.stopAntiHitProtection()
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiHitState
    if not state then return end
    state.Enabled = false
    App.Runtime.AntiHitProtection = false
    if state.Thread and task and type(task.cancel) == "function" then
        pcall(task.cancel, state.Thread)
    end
    state.Thread = nil
    if state.CharacterConnection then pcall(function() state.CharacterConnection:Disconnect() end) end
    if state.SteppedConnection then pcall(function() state.SteppedConnection:Disconnect() end) end
    state.CharacterConnection = nil
    state.SteppedConnection = nil
    App.restoreAntiHitCharacter(App.LocalPlayer.Character)
    local module = state.RagdollModule
    if module then
        for methodName, original in pairs(state.OriginalRagdollMethods or {}) do
            pcall(function() module[methodName] = original end)
        end
    end
    state.RagdollModule = nil
    state.OriginalRagdollMethods = {}
end
function App.startAntiHitProtection()
    App.stopAntiHitProtection()
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiHitState
    state.Enabled = true
    App.Runtime.AntiHitProtection = true
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    if character then App.applyAntiHitCharacter(character) end
    state.CharacterConnection = App.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
        if state.Enabled then
            task.wait(0.2)
            if state.Enabled then App.applyAntiHitCharacter(newCharacter) end
        end
    end)
    state.SteppedConnection = App.Services.RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not state.Enabled then return end
        local currentCharacter = App.LocalPlayer.Character
        if currentCharacter then App.applyAntiHitCharacter(currentCharacter) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local module = require(App.Services.ReplicatedStorage.Library.Modules.Ragdoll)
        state.RagdollModule = module
        for _, methodName in ipairs({"ApplyClientRagdoll", "Ragdoll", "TimedRagdoll", "TimedRagdollAsync"}) do
            local original = module[methodName]
            if type(original) == "function" then
                state.OriginalRagdollMethods[methodName] = original
                module[methodName] = function(targetCharacter, ...)
                    if state.Enabled and targetCharacter == App.LocalPlayer.Character then
                        return
                    end
                    return original(targetCharacter, ...)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    state.Thread = task.spawn(function()
        while state.Enabled and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
            local currentCharacter = App.LocalPlayer.Character
            if currentCharacter then App.applyAntiHitCharacter(currentCharacter) end
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
    end)
end

function App.restoreAntiRagdollCharacter(character)
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiRagdollState
    if not state then return end
    for humanoid, backup in pairs(state.OriginalHumanoidStates or {}) do
        if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
            for stateType, enabled in pairs(backup) do
                pcall(function() humanoid:SetStateEnabled(stateType, enabled) end)
            end
        end
        state.OriginalHumanoidStates[humanoid] = nil
    end
    for motor, enabled in pairs(state.OriginalMotors or {}) do
        if motor and motor.Parent then
            pcall(function() motor.Enabled = enabled end)
        end
        state.OriginalMotors[motor] = nil
    end
    for constraint, enabled in pairs(state.OriginalConstraints or {}) do
        if constraint and constraint.Parent then
            pcall(function() constraint.Enabled = enabled end)
        end
        state.OriginalConstraints[constraint] = nil
    end
end

function App.applyAntiRagdollCharacter(character)
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiRagdollState
    if not state or not state.Enabled or not character then return end
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        if state.OriginalHumanoidStates[humanoid] == nil then
            state.OriginalHumanoidStates[humanoid] = {
                [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics] = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics),
                [Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding] = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding),
                [Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown] = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown),
                [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll] = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll),
            }
        end
        pcall(function()
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, false)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
            local currentState = humanoid:GetState()
            if currentState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                or currentState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding
                or currentState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                or currentState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            then
                humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
        end)
    end
    for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
            if state.OriginalMotors[descendant] == nil then
                state.OriginalMotors[descendant] = descendant.Enabled
            end
            pcall(function() descendant.Enabled = true end)
        elseif (descendant:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or descendant:IsA("HingeConstraint"))
            and descendant:GetAttribute("RagdollConstraint") ~= nil
        then
            if state.OriginalConstraints[descendant] == nil then
                state.OriginalConstraints[descendant] = descendant.Enabled
            end
            pcall(function() descendant.Enabled = false end)
        end
    end
end

function App.stopAntiRagdoll()
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiRagdollState
    if not state then return end
    state.Enabled = false
    App.Runtime.AntiRagdoll = false
    if state.Thread and task and type(task.cancel) == "function" then
        pcall(task.cancel, state.Thread)
    end
    state.Thread = nil
    if state.CharacterConnection then
        pcall(function() state.CharacterConnection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    state.CharacterConnection = nil
    App.restoreAntiRagdollCharacter(App.LocalPlayer.Character)
end

function App.startAntiRagdoll()
    App.stopAntiRagdoll()
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiRagdollState
    state.Enabled = true
    App.Runtime.AntiRagdoll = true
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    if character then App.applyAntiRagdollCharacter(character) end
    state.CharacterConnection = App.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
        if state.Enabled then
            task.wait(0.2)
            if state.Enabled then App.applyAntiRagdollCharacter(newCharacter) end
        end
    end)
    state.Thread = task.spawn(function()
        while state.Enabled and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
            local currentCharacter = App.LocalPlayer.Character
            if currentCharacter then App.applyAntiRagdollCharacter(currentCharacter) end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end

function App.applyAntiFlingCharacter(character)
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiFlingState
    if not state or not state.Enabled or not character then return end
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root or not root:IsA("BasePart") then return end
    pcall(function()
        local linear = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local angular = root.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        if linear.Magnitude > 120 then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        if angular.Magnitude > 90 then
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end)
end

function App.stopAntiFling()
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiFlingState
    if not state then return end
    state.Enabled = false
    App.Runtime.AntiFling = false
    if state.Thread and task and type(task.cancel) == "function" then
        pcall(task.cancel, state.Thread)
    end
    state.Thread = nil
    if state.CharacterConnection then
        pcall(function() state.CharacterConnection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    state.CharacterConnection = nil
end

function App.startAntiFling()
    App.stopAntiFling()
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiFlingState
    state.Enabled = true
    App.Runtime.AntiFling = true
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    if character then App.applyAntiFlingCharacter(character) end
    state.CharacterConnection = App.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
        if state.Enabled then
            task.wait(0.2)
            if state.Enabled then App.applyAntiFlingCharacter(newCharacter) end
        end
    end)
    state.Thread = task.spawn(function()
        while state.Enabled and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
            local currentCharacter = App.LocalPlayer.Character
            if currentCharacter then App.applyAntiFlingCharacter(currentCharacter) end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end)
end

function App.setGuardNoCollide(character, enable)
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiGuardState
    if not character or not state then
        return
    end
    for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
            if enable then
                if state.OriginalCollisionGroups[descendant] == nil then
                    state.OriginalCollisionGroups[descendant] = descendant.CollisionGroup
                end
                pcall(function()
                    descendant.CollisionGroup = "GuardsNoCollide"
                end)
            else
                local original = state.OriginalCollisionGroups[descendant]
                if original then
                    pcall(function()
                        descendant.CollisionGroup = original
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function App.setGuardRagdollImmune(humanoid, enable)
    if not humanoid then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, not enable)
        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, not enable)
        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, not enable)
        humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
    end)
end

function App.setupAntiGuardHealth(humanoid)
    if not humanoid then
        return
    end
    local state = App.Runtime.AntiGuardState
    pcall(function()
        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
    end)
    local diedConnection = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
        local maxHealth = humanoid.MaxHealth
        local healthConnection
        healthConnection = App.Services.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not humanoid.Parent or not state.Installed then
                pcall(function()
                    healthConnection:Disconnect()
                end)
                return
            end
            humanoid.Health = maxHealth
        end)
        table.insert(state.Connections, healthConnection)
    end)
    table.insert(state.Connections, diedConnection)
    task.delay(0.05, function()
        if state.Installed and humanoid.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.Health = 0
            end)
        end
    end)
end

function App.installAntiGuard()

function App.setClientRenderedPetsHidden(hidden)
    hidden = hidden == true
    App.Runtime.HideClientRenderedPets = hidden
    local state = App.Runtime
    local connectionState = state.ClientRenderedAssetConnection
    if type(connectionState) == "table" then
        for _, connection in pairs(connectionState) do
            pcall(function()
                connection:Disconnect()
            end)
        end
    elseif connectionState then
        pcall(function()
            connectionState:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    state.ClientRenderedAssetConnection = nil

    local function restoreObject(object, backup)
        if not object or not object.Parent or not backup then
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            if backup.Kind == "BasePart" and object:IsA("BasePart") then
                object.LocalTransparencyModifier = backup.LocalTransparencyModifier
            elseif backup.Kind == "Transparency" and (object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture")) then
                object.Transparency = backup.Transparency
            elseif backup.Kind == "Enabled" and (object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
                or object:IsA("Beam")
                or object:IsA("Trail")
                or object:IsA("BillboardGui")
                or object:IsA("SurfaceGui")
            ) then
                object.Enabled = backup.Enabled
            end
        end)
    end

    if not hidden then
        for object, backup in pairs(state.ClientRenderedAssetBackup) do
            restoreObject(object, backup)
            state.ClientRenderedAssetBackup[object] = nil
        end
        return 0
    end

    local function hideObject(object)
        if not object or not object.Parent then
            return
        end
        if object:IsA("BasePart") then
            if state.ClientRenderedAssetBackup[object] == nil then
                state.ClientRenderedAssetBackup[object] = {
                    Kind = "BasePart",
                    LocalTransparencyModifier = object.LocalTransparencyModifier,
                }
            end
            object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
        elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
            if state.ClientRenderedAssetBackup[object] == nil then
                state.ClientRenderedAssetBackup[object] = {
                    Kind = "Transparency",
                    Transparency = object.Transparency,
                }
            end
            object.Transparency = 1
        elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
            or object:IsA("Beam")
            or object:IsA("Trail")
            or object:IsA("BillboardGui")
            or object:IsA("SurfaceGui")
        then
            if state.ClientRenderedAssetBackup[object] == nil then
                state.ClientRenderedAssetBackup[object] = {
                    Kind = "Enabled",
                    Enabled = object.Enabled,
                }
            end
            object.Enabled = false
        end
    end

    local folder = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("ClientRenderedAssets")
    if folder then
        for _, descendant in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
            hideObject(descendant)
        end
        state.ClientRenderedAssetConnection = {
            DescendantAdded = folder.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
                if state.HideClientRenderedPets then
                    hideObject(descendant)
                    for _, child in ipairs(descendant:GetDescendants()) do
                        hideObject(child)
                    end
                end
            end),
        }
    else
        state.ClientRenderedAssetConnection = {
            WorkspaceChildAdded = App.Services.Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if state.HideClientRenderedPets and child.Name == "ClientRenderedAssets" then
                    App.setClientRenderedPetsHidden(true)
                end
            end),
        }
    end
    return 1
end

    local state = App.Runtime.AntiGuardState
    if not state or state.Installed then
        return
    end
    state.Installed = true
    pcall(function()
        App.Services.StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Health, false)
    end)

    local function applyCharacter(character)
        if not state.Installed or not character then
            return
        end
        state.OriginalCollisionGroups = {}
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        App.setGuardNoCollide(character, true)
        App.setGuardRagdollImmune(humanoid, true)
        App.setupAntiGuardHealth(humanoid)
        local descendantConnection = character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            if not state.Installed or not App.Runtime.AntiGuardKnockback then
                return
            end
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                if state.OriginalCollisionGroups[descendant] == nil then
                    state.OriginalCollisionGroups[descendant] = descendant.CollisionGroup
                end
                pcall(function()
                    descendant.CollisionGroup = "GuardsNoCollide"
                end)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(state.CharacterConnections, descendantConnection)
    end

    local characterConnection = App.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(applyCharacter)
    table.insert(state.Connections, characterConnection)
    if App.LocalPlayer.Character then
        task.defer(applyCharacter, App.LocalPlayer.Character)
    end

    pcall(function()
        local replicatedStorage = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage
        local network = require(replicatedStorage.Library.Client.Network)
        local constants = require(replicatedStorage.Library.Globals.Constants)
        local guardsMap = constants.NETWORK_MAP.Guards
        local eggsMap = constants.NETWORK_MAP.Eggs
        local eggCmds = require(replicatedStorage.Library.Client.EggCmds)
        state.Network = network
        state.EggCmds = eggCmds
        state.OriginalNetworkFire = network.Fire
        state.OriginalNetworkInvoke = network.Invoke
        state.OriginalRequestDropHeldAreaEgg = eggCmds.RequestDropHeldAreaEgg
        network.Fire = function(eventName, ...)
            if App.Runtime.AntiGuardKnockback
                and (eventName == guardsMap.FOREST_HIT
                    or eventName == guardsMap.SPEED_HIT_OFFER
                    or eventName == guardsMap.SPEED_HIT_WARNING)
            then
                return
            end
            return state.OriginalNetworkFire(eventName, ...)
        end
        network.Invoke = function(endpoint, args, ...)
            if App.Runtime.AntiGuardKnockback and endpoint == eggsMap.REQUEST_AREA_EGG_DROP then
                local reason = typeof(args) == "table" and args.Reason or nil
                if reason ~= nil and reason ~= "PlayerRequest" then
                    return false
                end
            end
            return state.OriginalNetworkInvoke(endpoint, args, ...)
        end
        eggCmds.RequestDropHeldAreaEgg = function(reason)
            if App.Runtime.AntiGuardKnockback and reason ~= nil and reason ~= "PlayerRequest" then
                return false
            end
            return state.OriginalRequestDropHeldAreaEgg(reason)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local ragdoll = require(App.Services.ReplicatedStorage.Library.Modules.Ragdoll)
        state.RagdollModule = ragdoll
        for _, methodName in ipairs({"ApplyClientRagdoll", "Ragdoll", "TimedRagdoll", "TimedRagdollAsync"}) do
            local original = ragdoll[methodName]
            if type(original) == "function" then
                state.OriginalRagdollMethods[methodName] = original
                ragdoll[methodName] = function(character, ...)
                    if App.Runtime.AntiGuardKnockback and character == App.LocalPlayer.Character then
                        return
                    end
                    return original(character, ...)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local fallDownStates = {
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated] = true,
    }
    local steppedConnection = App.Services.RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not state.Installed or not App.Runtime.AntiGuardKnockback or App.Runtime.IsStealing then
            return
        end
        local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not character then
            return
        end
        local nowClock = os.clock()
        if nowClock - state.LastRagdollScanAt >= 0.2 then
            state.LastRagdollScanAt = nowClock
            for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
                    if not descendant.Enabled then
                        descendant.Enabled = true
                    end
                elseif descendant:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or descendant:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
                    if descendant:GetAttribute("RagdollConstraint") or descendant.Name:find("Ragdoll") then
                        pcall(function()
                            descendant:Destroy()
                        end)
                    end
                elseif descendant:IsA("Attachment") then
                    if descendant:GetAttribute("RagdollAttachment") or descendant.Name:find("Ragdoll") then
                        pcall(function()
                            descendant:Destroy()
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if humanoid then
            if humanoid.PlatformStand then
                humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            end
            if humanoid.Sit then
                humanoid.Sit = false
            end
            if fallDownStates[humanoid:GetState()] then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
    table.insert(state.Connections, steppedConnection)

    local heartbeatConnection = App.Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not state.Installed or not App.Runtime.AntiGuardKnockback or App.Runtime.IsStealing then
            return
        end
        local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then
            return
        end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local walkSpeed = humanoid and humanoid.WalkSpeed or 16
        local multiplier = tonumber(App.Runtime.TweenSpeedMultiplier) or 2
        local currentVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local allowedMagnitude = math.max(walkSpeed * multiplier * 1.5, 100)
        local suddenDelta = (currentVelocity - state.LastVelocity).Magnitude
        if currentVelocity.Magnitude > allowedMagnitude and suddenDelta > 40 then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            currentVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        state.LastVelocity = currentVelocity
    end)
    table.insert(state.Connections, heartbeatConnection)

    state.Thread = task.spawn(function()
        while state.Installed and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
            if App.Runtime.AntiGuardKnockback and not App.Runtime.IsStealing then
                local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
                if character then
                    App.setGuardNoCollide(character, true)
                    App.setGuardRagdollImmune(character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"), true)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end

App.installAntiGuard()

function App.triggerRejoin()
    if App.Runtime.Unloading or App.Runtime.RejoinTeleporting then
        return
    end
    App.Runtime.RejoinTeleporting = true
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        App.Services.TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(
            game.PlaceId,
            game.JobId,
            App.LocalPlayer
        )
    end)
    if not ok then
        App.Runtime.RejoinTeleporting = false
        warn("[Rejoin] Teleport failed:", err)
    end
end
function App.installRejoinMenu()
    if #App.Runtime.RejoinConnections > 0 then
        return
    end
    local coreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local guiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
    local userInputService = App.Services.UserInputService
    local function addRejoinButton()
        if App.Runtime.Unloading then
            return
        end
        task.defer(function()
            if App.Runtime.Unloading then
                return
            end
            local robloxGui = coreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
                or coreGui:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 5)
            local settingsShield = robloxGui
                and robloxGui:FindFirstChild("SettingsClippingShield")
            local settings = settingsShield
                and settingsShield:FindFirstChild("SettingsShield")
            local menuContainer = settings
                and settings:FindFirstChild("MenuContainer")
            local page = menuContainer and menuContainer:FindFirstChild("Page")
            local bottomFrame = page
                and page:FindFirstChild("BottomButtonFrame")
            bottomFrame = bottomFrame and bottomFrame:FindFirstChild("MenuButtons")
            if not bottomFrame then
                return
            end
            local existing = bottomFrame:FindFirstChild("IntegratedRejoinMenuButton")
            if existing then
                App.Runtime.RejoinButton = existing
                return
            end
            local template = bottomFrame:FindFirstChild("MenuButtonContainer1")
                or bottomFrame:FindFirstChild("MenuButtonContainer2")
            if not template then
                return
            end
            for _, child in ipairs(bottomFrame:GetChildren()) do
                if child ~= template
                    and string.match(child.Name, "^MenuButtonContainer")
                    and tonumber(child.LayoutOrder)
                    and child.LayoutOrder >= 3
                then
                    child.LayoutOrder = child.LayoutOrder + 1
                end
            end
            local rejoinButton = template:Clone()
            rejoinButton.Name = "IntegratedRejoinMenuButton"
            rejoinButton.LayoutOrder = 3
            local textLabel = rejoinButton:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
            if textLabel then
                textLabel.Text = "Rejoin"
            end
            local hint = rejoinButton:FindFirstChild("Hint", true)
            if hint and hint:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                hint.Image = "rbxassetid://109358602604497"
                hint.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.zero
                hint.ImageRectSize = Vector2.zero
            end
            local menuButton = rejoinButton:FindFirstChild("MenuButton", true)
            if menuButton then
                pcall(function() menuButton.Transparency = 0.88 end)
                if menuButton:IsA("GuiButton") then
                    menuButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                        pcall(function() menuButton.Transparency = 0.81 end)
                    end)
                    menuButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                        pcall(function() menuButton.Transparency = 0.88 end)
                    end)
                end
            end
            local clickableAction = rejoinButton:IsA("GuiButton")
                and rejoinButton
                or rejoinButton:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("GuiButton", true)
            if not clickableAction then
                rejoinButton:Destroy()
                return
            end
            clickableAction.Activated:Connect(App.triggerRejoin)
            rejoinButton.Parent = bottomFrame
            App.Runtime.RejoinButton = rejoinButton
        end)
    end
    table.insert(App.Runtime.RejoinConnections, guiService.MenuOpened:Connect(addRejoinButton))
    table.insert(App.Runtime.RejoinConnections, userInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
        if not processed
            and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.J
            and guiService.MenuIsOpen
        then
            App.triggerRejoin()
        end
    end))
    if guiService.MenuIsOpen then
        addRejoinButton()
    end
end
function App.stopRejoinMenu()
    for _, connection in ipairs(App.Runtime.RejoinConnections) do
        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    App.Runtime.RejoinConnections = {}
    if App.Runtime.RejoinButton then
        pcall(function() App.Runtime.RejoinButton:Destroy() end)
    end
    App.Runtime.RejoinButton = nil
    App.Runtime.RejoinTeleporting = false
end
for _, rarity in ipairs({"Rare", "Epic", "Legendary", "Mythic", "Divine", "Cosmic", "Eternal", "Secret"}) do
    App.Runtime.SelectedStealRarities[rarity] = true
end
function App.guard(callback)
    return function(...)
        local packed = table.pack(...)
        local ok, result = pcall(callback, table.unpack(packed, 1, packed.n))
        if not ok and not App.Runtime.Unloading then
            App.reportFeatureError("Runtime", result)
        end
        return result
    end
end
function App.spawnGuarded(callback, ...)
    return task.spawn(App.guard(callback), ...)
end
function App.deferGuarded(callback, ...)
    return task.defer(App.guard(callback), ...)
end
function App.delayGuarded(seconds, callback, ...)
    return task.delay(seconds, App.guard(callback), ...)
end
function App.bind(connection)
    table.insert(App.UI.connections, connection)
    return connection
end
function App.addGameConnection(connection)
    table.insert(App.Game.Connections, connection)
    return connection
end
function App.connectGuarded(signal, handler)
    if not signal or type(signal.Connect) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end
    return App.bind(signal:Connect(App.guard(handler)))
end
function App.withUiIdentity(callback)
    return callback()
end
function App.notify(title, message, duration)
    if Library and type(Library.Notify) == "function" then
        pcall(Library.Notify, Library, {Title = tostring(title), Description = tostring(message), Duration = duration or 3})
    end
end
function App.publicErrorText(message, fallback)
    local text = tostring(message or "")
    return text ~= "" and text or tostring(fallback or "The action could not be completed.")
end
function App.notifyError(feature, message)
    App.notify(tostring(feature or "Feature") .. " Error", App.publicErrorText(message), 4)
end
function App.notifySystemError(feature, message)
    App.notifyError(feature, message)
end
function App.setFeatureStatus(feature, status, isError)
    if feature == nil then return end
    if status == nil or status == "" or status == "Disabled" then
        App.Runtime.FeatureStatus[feature] = nil
    else
        App.Runtime.FeatureStatus[feature] = tostring(status)
    end
    App.Runtime.StatusFeature = feature
    App.Runtime.StatusIsError = isError == true
end
function App.setFeatureDescription(feature, status)
    App.setFeatureStatus(feature, status, false)
end
function App.featureAlive(feature, version)
    return not App.Runtime.Unloading and (feature == nil or (App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] == true and App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] == version))
end
function App.waitFeature(feature, version, duration)
    local deadline = os.clock() + math.max(0, tonumber(duration) or 0)
    repeat
        if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then return false end
        task.wait(math.min(0.1, math.max(0, deadline - os.clock())))
    until os.clock() >= deadline
    return App.featureAlive(feature, version)
end
function App.countMap(value)
    local count = 0
    if type(value) == "table" then for _ in pairs(value) do count = count + 1 end end
    return count
end
function App.copyTrueMap(source)
    local copy = {}
    if type(source) == "table" then for key, value in pairs(source) do if value == true then copy[tostring(key)] = true end end end
    return copy
end
function App.trueMapsEqual(left, right)
    left = type(left) == "table" and left or {}
    right = type(right) == "table" and right or {}
    for key, value in pairs(left) do if value == true and right[key] ~= true then return false end end
    for key, value in pairs(right) do if value == true and left[key] ~= true then return false end end
    return true
end

function App.waitUntil(feature, version, timeout, predicate)
    local deadline = os.clock() + math.max(0, tonumber(timeout) or 0)
    repeat
        if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
            return false
        end
        local predicateOk, result = pcall(predicate)
        if predicateOk and result == true then
            return true
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    until os.clock() >= deadline
    return false
end

function App.reportFeatureError(feature, message)
    local streak = (App.Runtime.FeatureErrorStreak[feature] or 0) + 1
    App.Runtime.FeatureErrorStreak[feature] = streak
    local text = App.publicErrorText(
        message,
        "This feature is temporarily unavailable"
    )
    local previous = App.Runtime.LastErrors[feature]
    local now = os.clock()
    App.setFeatureStatus(feature, text, true)
    if previous
        and previous.Message == text
        and now - previous.At < 8
        and streak < (tonumber(App.Config.SafetyMaxFeatureErrors) or 5)
    then
        return
    end
    App.Runtime.LastErrors[feature] = {
        Message = text,
        At = now,
    }
    App.notifyError(feature, text)
    if streak >= (tonumber(App.Config.SafetyMaxFeatureErrors) or 5) then
        App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] = false
        App.Runtime.PendingStarts[feature] = nil
        App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] = (App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] or 0) + 1
        App.setFeatureStatus(feature, "Stopped after repeated errors", true)
        App.notifyError(feature, "Stopped safely after repeated failures")
    end
end

function App.waitForPath(root, path, timeout)
    local current = root
    local deadline = os.clock() + (tonumber(timeout) or App.Config.ModuleLoadTimeout)
    for _, childName in ipairs(path) do
        if not current then
            return nil
        end
        local remaining = deadline - os.clock()
        if remaining <= 0 then
            return nil
        end
        current = current:WaitForChild(childName, remaining)
    end
    return current
end

function App.requirePath(path, label)
    local moduleScript = App.waitForPath(
        App.Services.ReplicatedStorage,
        path,
        App.Config.ModuleLoadTimeout
    )
    if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
        return false, tostring(label) .. " module was not found"
    end
    local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
    if not ok then
        return false, tostring(label) .. " failed to load: " .. tostring(result)
    end
    return true, result
end

function App.addGameConnection(connection)
    if connection then
        table.insert(App.Game.Connections, connection)
    end
    return connection
end

function App.initGame()
    if App.Runtime.Unloading then
        return false, "Script is unloading"
    end
    if App.Game.Ready then
        return true
    end
    if App.Game.Initializing then
        local deadline = os.clock() + App.Config.ModuleLoadTimeout
        repeat
            task.wait(0.05)
        until App.Game.Ready
            or not App.Game.Initializing
            or App.Runtime.Unloading
            or os.clock() >= deadline
        return App.Game.Ready, App.Game.InitError
    end

    App.Game.Initializing = true
    App.Game.InitError = nil
    local specs = {
        { "Network", { "Library", "Client", "Network" } },
        { "Signal", { "Library", "Signal" } },
        { "Save", { "Library", "Client", "Save" } },
        { "EggCmds", { "Library", "Client", "EggCmds" } },
        { "PlotCmds", { "Library", "Client", "PlotCmds" } },
        { "BaseUpgradeClient", { "Library", "Client", "BaseUpgradeClient" } },
        { "TabController", { "Library", "Client", "TabController" } },
        { "Constants", { "Library", "Globals", "Constants" } },
        { "Assets", { "Directory", "Assets" } },
        { "Rarity", { "Directory", "Rarity" } },
        { "Areas", { "Directory", "Areas" } },
        { "Guards", { "Directory", "Guards" } },
        { "Trails", { "Directory", "Trails" } },
        { "Treadmills", { "Directory", "Treadmills" } },
        { "Bases", { "Directory", "Bases" } },
        { "Rebirths", { "Directory", "Rebirths" } },
        { "WeatherSchedule", { "Library", "Weather", "Schedule" } },
        { "Sakura", { "Directory", "Sakura" } },
        {
            "GuardEscapePrediction",
            { "Library", "Modules", "GuardAreas", "GuardEscapePrediction" },
        },
        {
            "GuardChasePolicy",
            { "Library", "Modules", "GuardAreas", "GuardChasePolicy" },
        },
        {
            "ResolveGuardSpeedRequirement",
            { "Library", "Functions", "ResolveGuardSpeedRequirement" },
        },
        {
            "AreaEggSlotIdentity",
            { "Library", "Util", "AreaEggSlotIdentity" },
        },
        {
            "AreaEggResetWall",
            { "Library", "Client", "AreaEggResetWall" },
        },
        { 
            "AreaEggResetTimeUtil",
            { "Library", "Util", "AreaEggResetTimeUtil" },
        },
    }

    for _, spec in ipairs(specs) do
        if App.Runtime.Unloading then
            App.Game.InitError = "Script is unloading"
            App.Game.Initializing = false
            return false, App.Game.InitError
        end
        local ok, result = App.requirePath(spec[2], spec[1])
        if not ok then
            App.Game.InitError = result
            App.Game.Initializing = false
            return false, result
        end
        App.Game.Modules[spec[1]] = result
    end

    if App.Runtime.Unloading then
        App.Game.InitError = "Script is unloading"
        App.Game.Initializing = false
        return false, App.Game.InitError
    end
    App.Game.Constants = App.Game.Modules.Constants
    if type(App.Game.Constants) ~= "table"
        or type(App.Game.Constants.NETWORK_MAP) ~= "table"
    then
        App.Game.InitError = "The game's network map is unavailable"
        App.Game.Initializing = false
        return false, App.Game.InitError
    end

    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    if eggCmds
        and eggCmds.AreaEggCarryStateChanged
        and type(eggCmds.AreaEggCarryStateChanged.Connect) == "function"
    then
        App.addGameConnection(eggCmds.AreaEggCarryStateChanged:Connect(App.guard(function(state)
            if type(state) ~= "table" then
                return
            end
            local wasCarrying = App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying == true
            local previousUid = App.Runtime.CarryState.Uid
            local previousAreaId = App.Runtime.CarryState.AreaId
            local previousNestId = App.Runtime.CarryState.NestId
            local previousAssetCategory = App.Runtime.CarryState.AssetCategory
            App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying = state.IsCarrying == true
            App.Runtime.CarryState.Uid = state.Uid
            App.Runtime.CarryState.AreaId = state.AreaId
            App.Runtime.CarryState.NestId = state.NestId
            App.Runtime.CarryState.AssetCategory = state.AssetCategory
            if wasCarrying
                and not App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying
                and App.Runtime.AutoRecarryDroppedEgg
                and previousUid
            then
                local recarry = App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState
                recarry.Uid = previousUid
                recarry.AreaId = previousAreaId
                recarry.NestId = previousNestId
                recarry.AssetCategory = previousAssetCategory
                recarry.LastAttemptAt = 0
                recarry.LastSeenCarried = false
            end
        end)))
    end
    if eggCmds
        and eggCmds.AreaEggUpdated
        and type(eggCmds.AreaEggUpdated.Connect) == "function"
    then
        App.addGameConnection(eggCmds.AreaEggUpdated:Connect(App.guard(function(record)
            if type(record) ~= "table" or record.Uid == nil then return end
            local uid = tostring(record.Uid)
            local carry = App.Runtime.CarryState
            local recarry = App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState
            local sameHeldUid = carry.Uid ~= nil and tostring(carry.Uid) == uid
            local samePendingUid = recarry.Uid ~= nil and tostring(recarry.Uid) == uid
            if App.Runtime.AutoRecarryDroppedEgg
                and record.State == "Dropped"
                and (sameHeldUid or samePendingUid)
            then
                recarry.Uid = uid
                recarry.AreaId = record.AreaId or carry.AreaId
                recarry.NestId = record.NestId or carry.NestId
                recarry.AssetCategory = record.AssetCategory or carry.AssetCategory
                recarry.LastAttemptAt = 0
                recarry.LastSeenCarried = false
            end
        end)))
    end
    if eggCmds
        and eggCmds.AreaEggRemoved
        and type(eggCmds.AreaEggRemoved.Connect) == "function"
    then
        App.addGameConnection(eggCmds.AreaEggRemoved:Connect(App.guard(function(uid)
            local recarry = App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState
            if App.Runtime.AutoRecarryDroppedEgg
                and recarry.Uid ~= nil
                and tostring(recarry.Uid) == tostring(uid)
            then
                recarry.LastAttemptAt = 0
            end
        end)))
    end
    if eggCmds
        and eggCmds.AreaEggSnapshotUpdated
        and type(eggCmds.AreaEggSnapshotUpdated.Connect) == "function"
    then
        App.addGameConnection(eggCmds.AreaEggSnapshotUpdated:Connect(App.guard(function(snapshot)
            if type(snapshot) ~= "table" or type(snapshot.Records) ~= "table" then return end
            local recarry = App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState
            if not App.Runtime.AutoRecarryDroppedEgg or recarry.Uid == nil then return end
            for _, record in ipairs(snapshot.Records) do
                if type(record) == "table" and tostring(record.Uid) == tostring(recarry.Uid)
                    and record.State == "Dropped"
                then
                    recarry.AreaId = record.AreaId or recarry.AreaId
                    recarry.NestId = record.NestId or recarry.NestId
                    recarry.AssetCategory = record.AssetCategory or recarry.AssetCategory
                    recarry.LastAttemptAt = 0
                    recarry.LastSeenCarried = false
                    break
                end
            end
        end)))
    end
    if eggCmds
        and eggCmds.AreaEggClaimed
        and type(eggCmds.AreaEggClaimed.Connect) == "function"
    then
        App.addGameConnection(eggCmds.AreaEggClaimed:Connect(App.guard(function()
            App.Runtime.ClaimSerial = App.Runtime.ClaimSerial + 1
        end)))
    end

    App.trackTreadmillSession()

    App.Game.Ready = true
    App.Game.Initializing = false
    return true
end

function App.getSaveData()
    if not App.Game.Ready then
        local ready, errorMessage = App.initGame()
        if not ready then
            return nil, errorMessage
        end
    end
    local save = App.Game.Modules.Save
    local ok, data = pcall(save.Get)
    if not ok then
        return nil, data
    end
    if type(data) ~= "table" then
        return nil, "Save data is not loaded yet"
    end
    return data
end

function App.invokeNetwork(remoteName, ...)
    if type(remoteName) ~= "string" or remoteName == "" then
        return false, "This action is unavailable"
    end
    local network = App.Game.Modules.Network
    if type(network) ~= "table" or type(network.Invoke) ~= "function" then
        return false, "This action is unavailable"
    end
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    local lastCallAt = App.Runtime.NetworkLastCallAt[remoteName] or 0
    local minimumInterval = math.max(0, tonumber(App.Config.SafeNetworkMinInterval) or 0.06)
    local waitTime = minimumInterval - (nowClock - lastCallAt)
    if waitTime > 0 then
        task.wait(math.min(waitTime, 0.25))
    end
    App.Runtime.NetworkLastCallAt[remoteName] = os.clock()
    local results = { pcall(network.Invoke, remoteName, ...) }
    if results[1] ~= true then
        return false, results[2]
    end
    return true, results[2], results[3], results[4]
end

function App.getNetworkGroup(groupName)
    local constants = App.Game.Constants
    local map = type(constants) == "table" and constants.NETWORK_MAP or nil
    if type(map) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local group = map[groupName]
    if type(group) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    return group
end

function App.discoverSprinklers()
    local options = {}
    local lookup = {}
    local directoryModule = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Directory")
    local gearsModule = directoryModule and directoryModule:FindFirstChild("Gears")
    local ok, gears = false, nil
    if gearsModule then
        ok, gears = pcall(require, gearsModule)
    end
    if ok and type(gears) == "table" then
        local directory = type(gears.Directory) == "table" and gears.Directory or gears
        for id, data in pairs(directory) do
            if type(data) == "table" and data.IsSprinkler == true then
                local label = tostring(data.DisplayName or data.Name or id)
                if lookup[label] == nil then
                    lookup[label] = tostring(id)
                    options[#options + 1] = label
                end
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(options)
    App.Runtime.SprinklerTypes = lookup
    if App.Runtime.SelectedSprinklerType == nil or lookup[App.Runtime.SelectedSprinklerType] == nil then
        App.Runtime.SelectedSprinklerType = options[1]
    end
    return options
end

function App.getSprinklerPlacementEndpoint()
    local constants = App.Game.Constants
    local map = type(constants) == "table" and constants.NETWORK_MAP or nil
    if type(map) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    for groupName, group in pairs(map) do
        if type(group) == "table" then
            local groupText = string.lower(tostring(groupName))
            for key, endpoint in pairs(group) do
                if type(endpoint) == "string" then
                    local text = string.lower(tostring(key) .. " " .. endpoint)
                    local isSprinkler = string.find(groupText, "sprink", 1, true)
                        or string.find(text, "sprink", 1, true)
                    local isPlacement = string.find(text, "place", 1, true)
                        or string.find(text, "deploy", 1, true)
                    if isSprinkler and isPlacement then
                        return endpoint
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function App.getSprinklerPlacementCFrame()
    local position = App.getPlotReturnPosition()
    if typeof(position) == "Vector3" then
        return CFrame.new(position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
    end
    local _, _, root = App.getCharacterParts()
    return root and root.CFrame or nil
end

function App.stepAutoPlaceSprinklers(feature, version)
    local options = App.discoverSprinklers()
    local selectedLabel = App.Runtime.SelectedSprinklerType
    local sprinklerId = selectedLabel and App.Runtime.SprinklerTypes[selectedLabel]
    if #options == 0 or type(sprinklerId) ~= "string" then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "No sprinkler types found")
        return 3
    end
    local endpoint = App.getSprinklerPlacementEndpoint()
    local placementCFrame = App.getSprinklerPlacementCFrame()
    if type(endpoint) ~= "string" or typeof(placementCFrame) ~= "CFrame" then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Sprinkler placement endpoint unavailable")
        return 3
    end
    local placed, result = App.invokeNetwork(endpoint, sprinklerId, placementCFrame)
    if not placed then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Sprinkler placement failed")
        return 2
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Placing " .. tostring(selectedLabel))
    return 3
end




function App.trackTreadmillSession()
    if App.Runtime.TreadmillTracked then
        return
    end
    local treadmills = App.getNetworkGroup("Treadmills")
    local network = App.Game.Modules.Network
    if not treadmills
        or type(treadmills.ACTIVE_TREADMILL_EVENT) ~= "string"
        or type(network) ~= "table"
        or type(network.Fired) ~= "function"
    then
        return
    end
    local signalOk, signal = pcall(network.Fired, treadmills.ACTIVE_TREADMILL_EVENT)
    if not signalOk or type(signal) ~= "userdata" and type(signal) ~= "table" then
        return
    end
    local connectOk = pcall(function()
        App.addGameConnection(signal:Connect(App.guard(function(treadmillName)
            App.Runtime.ActiveTreadmill = treadmillName
            App.Runtime.TreadmillKnown = true
        end)))
        App.addGameConnection(
            App.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(App.guard(function()
                App.Runtime.ActiveTreadmill = nil
                App.Runtime.TreadmillKnown = false
            end))
        )
    end)
    if connectOk then
        App.Runtime.TreadmillTracked = true
    end
end

function App.requestTreadmillRelease(endpoint)
    local requestOk, released = App.invokeNetwork(endpoint)
    if not requestOk then
        return false, "Could not get off the treadmill"
    end
    App.Runtime.TreadmillKnown = true
    if released ~= true then
        App.Runtime.ActiveTreadmill = nil
        return true
    end
    local deadline = os.clock() + App.Config.TreadmillReleaseTimeout
    while App.Runtime.ActiveTreadmill ~= nil and os.clock() < deadline do
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    App.Runtime.ActiveTreadmill = nil
    return true
end




function App.stepOffTreadmillPad()
    local home = App.getPlotReturnPosition()
    if typeof(home) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false
    end
    return App.travelTo(nil, nil, home, true) == true
end

function App.leaveTreadmill(allowStepOff)
    local treadmills = App.getNetworkGroup("Treadmills")
    if not treadmills or type(treadmills.REQUEST_UNEQUIP) ~= "string" then
        return true
    end



    if App.Runtime.TreadmillTracked
        and App.Runtime.TreadmillKnown
        and App.Runtime.ActiveTreadmill == nil
    then
        return true
    end

    for attempt = 1, App.Config.TreadmillReleaseAttempts do
        local releaseOk, releaseError = App.requestTreadmillRelease(
            treadmills.REQUEST_UNEQUIP
        )
        if not releaseOk then
            return false, releaseError
        end
        task.wait(App.Config.TreadmillReleaseSettle)
        if App.Runtime.ActiveTreadmill == nil then
            return true
        end
        if not allowStepOff or attempt >= App.Config.TreadmillReleaseAttempts then
            break
        end
        App.stepOffTreadmillPad()
    end

    if App.Runtime.ActiveTreadmill == nil then
        return true
    end
    return false, "Treadmill will not let go"
end




function App.findStealWallObjects()
    local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
    local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
    if not areas then
        return {}
    end
    local matches = {}
    for _, descendant in ipairs(areas:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant.Name == "WallStartCollision"
            or descendant.Name == "WallStartVisual"
        then
            table.insert(matches, descendant)
        end
    end
    return matches
end

function App.applyStealWallBypass()
    local changed = 0
    local function disablePart(object)
        if object:IsA("BasePart") then
            if App.Runtime.StealWallBackup[object] == nil then
                App.Runtime.StealWallBackup[object] = {
                    CanCollide = object.CanCollide,
                    CanTouch = object.CanTouch,
                    CanQuery = object.CanQuery,
                    LocalTransparencyModifier = object.LocalTransparencyModifier,
                }
                changed = changed + 1
            end
            object.CanCollide = false
            object.CanTouch = false
            object.CanQuery = false
            object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
        end
    end
    for _, object in ipairs(App.findStealWallObjects()) do
        disablePart(object)
        for _, descendant in ipairs(object:GetDescendants()) do
            disablePart(descendant)
        end
    end
    return changed
end

function App.setStealWallsDisabled(disabled)
    App.Runtime.StealWallsDisabled = disabled == true
    if disabled then
        local changed = App.applyStealWallBypass()
        if not App.Runtime.StealWallConnection then
            App.Runtime.StealWallConnection = task.spawn(function()
                while not App.Runtime.Unloading and App.Runtime.StealWallsDisabled do
                    App.applyStealWallBypass()
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
                if App.Runtime.StealWallConnection == coroutine.running() then
                    App.Runtime.StealWallConnection = nil
                end
            end)
        end
        return changed, changed > 0 and nil or "Steal wall objects were not found"
    end
    local connection = App.Runtime.StealWallConnection
    App.Runtime.StealWallConnection = nil
    if type(connection) == "thread" and task and type(task.cancel) == "function" then
        pcall(task.cancel, connection)
    elseif connection then
        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    local changed = 0
    for object, backup in pairs(App.Runtime.StealWallBackup) do
        if object and object.Parent and object:IsA("BasePart") then
            object.CanCollide = backup.CanCollide
            object.CanTouch = backup.CanTouch
            object.CanQuery = backup.CanQuery
            object.LocalTransparencyModifier = backup.LocalTransparencyModifier
        end
        App.Runtime.StealWallBackup[object] = nil
        changed = changed + 1
    end
    return changed
end

function App.isGameplayAreaBlocked()
    if App.Runtime.StealWallsDisabled then
        App.applyStealWallBypass()
        return false
    end
    local blocked = false
    local wall = App.Game.Modules.AreaEggResetWall
    local resolved = false
    if type(wall) == "table" and type(wall.IsClosed) == "function" then
        local closedOk, closed = pcall(wall.IsClosed)
        if closedOk then
            blocked = closed == true
            resolved = true
        end
    end
    if not resolved then
        local timeUtil = App.Game.Modules.AreaEggResetTimeUtil
        if type(timeUtil) == "table" and type(timeUtil.IsNight) == "function" then
            local nightOk, night = pcall(
                timeUtil.IsNight,
                App.Services.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
            )
            if nightOk then
                blocked = night == true
            end
        end
    end

    if blocked then
        App.Runtime.ResetWallOpenAt = nil
    elseif App.Runtime.ResetWallOpenAt == nil then
        App.Runtime.ResetWallOpenAt = os.clock()
    end
    return blocked
end



function App.isExpectedTripInterruption(message)
    if type(message) ~= "string" then
        return false
    end
    return message == "Reset wall closed mid-trip"
        or message == "Treadmill grabbed you, retrying"
end




function App.isResetSweepPending()
    if App.Runtime.StealWallsDisabled then
        return false
    end
    local openedAt = App.Runtime.ResetWallOpenAt
    if openedAt == nil then
        return false
    end
    return os.clock() - openedAt < App.Config.ResetWallOpenGrace
end

function App.getGameplayWindowSeconds()
    local timeUtil = App.Game.Modules.AreaEggResetTimeUtil
    if type(timeUtil) ~= "table"
        or type(timeUtil.GetPhaseTimeLeft) ~= "function"
    then
        return nil
    end
    local leftOk, left = pcall(
        timeUtil.GetPhaseTimeLeft,
        App.Services.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    )
    if not leftOk or type(left) ~= "number" then
        return nil
    end
    return left
end

function App.isSpecialEventActive()
    local roundState = App.LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("MurderMysteryRoundState")
        or "Waiting"
    if roundState ~= "Waiting"
        or App.LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("MurderMysteryRole") ~= nil
    then
        return true, "Murder Mystery"
    end

    local eventAttributes = {
        { App.Services.ReplicatedStorage, "BloodNightEvent" },
        { App.Services.ReplicatedStorage, "HourlyWeatherEvent" },
        { App.Services.ReplicatedStorage, "NyanCatsEvent" },
        { App.Services.Workspace, "BloodmoonEvent" },
        { App.Services.Workspace, "TrickOrTreatEvent" },
        { App.Services.Workspace, "GraveyardEvent" },
        { App.Services.Workspace, "MagmaWeather" },
        { App.Services.Workspace, "Starfall" },
    }
    for _, entry in ipairs(eventAttributes) do
        if entry[1]:GetAttribute(entry[2]) then
            return true, entry[2]
        end
    end
    return false, nil
end

function App.getCharacterParts()
    local player = App.LocalPlayer
    if not player then
        return nil, nil, nil, "LocalPlayer is unavailable"
    end

    local deadline = os.clock() + 8
    local lastCharacter = nil
    local lastHumanoid = nil
    local lastRoot = nil

    while os.clock() < deadline do
        if App.Runtime.Unloading then
            return nil, nil, nil, "Script is unloading"
        end

        local character = player.Character
        lastCharacter = character
        if character and character.Parent then
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not humanoid then
                humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 1)
            end

            local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not root then
                root = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 1)
            end

            lastHumanoid = humanoid
            lastRoot = root

            if character == player.Character
                and humanoid
                and humanoid.Parent
                and humanoid.Health > 0
                and root
                and root:IsA("BasePart")
                and root.Parent
            then
                return character, humanoid, root, nil
            end
        end

        task.wait(0.15)
    end

    if not lastCharacter or not lastCharacter.Parent then
        return nil, nil, nil, "Character is unavailable after respawn wait"
    end
    if not lastHumanoid or not lastHumanoid.Parent then
        return lastCharacter, nil, lastRoot, "Humanoid did not load"
    end
    if lastHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
        return lastCharacter, nil, lastRoot, "Humanoid is dead"
    end
    if not lastRoot or not lastRoot:IsA("BasePart") or not lastRoot.Parent then
        return lastCharacter, lastHumanoid, nil, "HumanoidRootPart did not load"
    end
    return lastCharacter, lastHumanoid, lastRoot, nil
end

function App.getGuardModel(areaId)
    local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
    local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
    local guardAreas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
    local area = guardAreas and guardAreas:FindFirstChild(tostring(areaId or ""))
    return area and area:FindFirstChild("Guard") or nil
end

function App.setGuardHitboxDisabled(guard, disabled)
    local state = App.Runtime
    if not guard or not state then return end
    for _, descendant in ipairs(guard:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
            if disabled then
                if state.GuardHitboxBackup[descendant] == nil then
                    state.GuardHitboxBackup[descendant] = {
                        CanCollide = descendant.CanCollide,
                        CanTouch = descendant.CanTouch,
                        CanQuery = descendant.CanQuery,
                    }
                end
                descendant.CanCollide = false
                descendant.CanTouch = false
                descendant.CanQuery = false
            else
                local backup = state.GuardHitboxBackup[descendant]
                if backup then
                    descendant.CanCollide = backup.CanCollide
                    descendant.CanTouch = backup.CanTouch
                    descendant.CanQuery = backup.CanQuery
                    state.GuardHitboxBackup[descendant] = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function App.stopGuardHitboxProtection()
    local state = App.Runtime
    state.DisableGuardHitbox = false
    if state.GuardHitboxThread and task and type(task.cancel) == "function" then
        pcall(task.cancel, state.GuardHitboxThread)
    end
    state.GuardHitboxThread = nil
    for instance, backup in pairs(state.GuardHitboxBackup or {}) do
        if instance and instance.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                instance.CanCollide = backup.CanCollide
                instance.CanTouch = backup.CanTouch
                instance.CanQuery = backup.CanQuery
            end)
        end
        state.GuardHitboxBackup[instance] = nil
    end
end

function App.startGuardHitboxProtection()
    App.stopGuardHitboxProtection()
    local state = App.Runtime
    state.DisableGuardHitbox = true
    state.GuardHitboxThread = task.spawn(function()
        while state.DisableGuardHitbox and not state.Unloading do
            local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
            local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
            local guardAreas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
            if guardAreas then
                for _, area in ipairs(guardAreas:GetChildren()) do
                    local guard = area:FindFirstChild("Guard")
                    if guard then
                        App.setGuardHitboxDisabled(guard, true)
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.35)
        end
    end)
end

function App.getGuardSpeedRequirement(areaId, guard)
    local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
    local areaRoot = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
    local guardAreas = areaRoot and areaRoot:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
    local area = guardAreas
        and guardAreas:FindFirstChild(tostring(areaId or ""))
    local separationLine = areaRoot and areaRoot:FindFirstChild("SeparationLine")
    local bounds = area and area:FindFirstChild("Bounds")
    local closestExit = area and area:FindFirstChild("ClosestExitPoint")
    if not area
        or not guard
        or not separationLine
        or not separationLine:IsA("BasePart")
        or not bounds
        or not bounds:IsA("BasePart")
        or not closestExit
        or not closestExit:IsA("BasePart")
    then
        return nil
    end

    local areas = App.Game.Modules.Areas
    local guards = App.Game.Modules.Guards
    local prediction = App.Game.Modules.GuardEscapePrediction
    local policy = App.Game.Modules.GuardChasePolicy
    local resolveRequirement = App.Game.Modules.ResolveGuardSpeedRequirement
    local areaConfigOk, areaConfig = pcall(function()
        return areas.Directory[area.Name]
    end)
    if not areaConfigOk or type(areaConfig) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local guardConfigOk, guardConfig = pcall(function()
        return guards.Directory[areaConfig.GuardId]
    end)
    if not guardConfigOk or type(guardConfig) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end

    local exitDirection = -separationLine.CFrame.LookVector
    local requirementOk, requirement = pcall(function()
        local input = {
            BaseGuardWalkSpeed = guardConfig.WalkSpeed,
            ExitDirection = exitDirection,
            ExitDistance = prediction.ResolveExitDistance(
                bounds.CFrame,
                bounds.Size,
                closestExit.Position,
                exitDirection
            ),
            FlatRadius = guardConfig.FlatRadius,
            GuardStartPosition = guard:GetPivot().Position,
            HitDistance = policy.ResolveHitDistance(guardConfig.HitDistance),
            PlayerStartPosition = closestExit.Position,
        }
        return resolveRequirement(input)
    end)
    if not requirementOk or type(requirement) ~= "number" then
        return nil
    end
    return requirement
end

function App.getActiveTrapPositions()
    local positions = {}
    for _, trap in ipairs(
        App.Services.CollectionService:GetTagged("PlacedTrap")
    ) do
        if trap.Parent and trap:GetAttribute("TrapActive") ~= true then
            local pivotOk, pivot = pcall(function()
                return trap:GetPivot()
            end)
            if pivotOk and typeof(pivot) == "CFrame" then
                table.insert(positions, pivot.Position)
            end
        end
    end
    return positions
end

function App.pointToSegmentDistance(point, segmentStart, segmentEnd)
    local segment = segmentEnd - segmentStart
    local lengthSquared = segment:Dot(segment)
    if lengthSquared <= 0.001 then
        return (point - segmentStart).Magnitude
    end
    local alpha = math.max(
        0,
        math.min(1, (point - segmentStart):Dot(segment) / lengthSquared)
    )
    return (point - (segmentStart + segment * alpha)).Magnitude
end

function App.adjustMovePointForTraps(startPosition, targetPosition)
    local direction = targetPosition - startPosition
    local flatDirection = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
    if flatDirection.Magnitude < 0.01 then
        return targetPosition
    end
    flatDirection = flatDirection.Unit
    local perpendicular = Vector3.new(-flatDirection.Z, 0, flatDirection.X)

    for _, trapPosition in ipairs(App.getActiveTrapPositions()) do
        local distance = App.pointToSegmentDistance(
            trapPosition,
            startPosition,
            targetPosition
        )
        if distance < App.Config.TrapAvoidRadius then
            local side = (trapPosition - startPosition):Dot(perpendicular) >= 0
                and -1
                or 1
            return Vector3.new(
                trapPosition.X,
                targetPosition.Y,
                trapPosition.Z
            ) + perpendicular * side * (App.Config.TrapAvoidRadius + 3)
        end
    end
    return targetPosition
end

function App.moveToWaypoint(feature, version, humanoid, root, targetPosition, extraCancel)
    local finished = false
    local reached = false
    local connection = humanoid.MoveToFinished:Connect(function(didReach)
        reached = didReach == true
        finished = true
    end)
    humanoid:MoveTo(targetPosition)
    local deadline = os.clock() + App.Config.MoveWaypointTimeout

    repeat
        if (extraCancel and extraCancel()) or not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
            pcall(humanoid.MoveTo, humanoid, root.Position)
            connection:Disconnect()
            return false, "cancelled"
        end
        if not humanoid.Parent
            or humanoid.Health <= 0
            or not root.Parent
        then
            if root.Parent then
                pcall(humanoid.MoveTo, humanoid, root.Position)
            end
            connection:Disconnect()
            return false, "Character reset mid-trip"
        end
        if (root.Position - targetPosition).Magnitude <= 4 then
            reached = true
            break
        end
        task.wait(0.08)
    until finished or os.clock() >= deadline

    connection:Disconnect()
    if reached then
        return true, nil
    end
    pcall(humanoid.MoveTo, humanoid, root.Position)
    return false, "Waypoint timed out"
end

function App.moveHumanoidTo(feature, version, destination, allowDuringEvent, extraCancel)
    if typeof(destination) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false, "Invalid destination"
    end

    for _ = 1, App.Config.MoveRepathAttempts do
        if (extraCancel and extraCancel()) or not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
            return false, "cancelled"
        end
        local character, humanoid, root, characterError = App.getCharacterParts()
        if not character or not humanoid or not root then
            return false, characterError
        end
        if character:GetAttribute("IsTrapped") == true then
            return false, "You got trapped"
        end
        if not allowDuringEvent then
            local eventActive, eventName = App.isSpecialEventActive()
            if eventActive then
                return false, tostring(eventName) .. " is active"
            end
            if App.isGameplayAreaBlocked() then
                return false, "Reset wall closed mid-trip"
            end
        end
        if (root.Position - destination).Magnitude
            <= App.Config.StealInteractDistance
        then
            return true
        end

        local path = App.Services.PathfindingService:CreatePath({
            AgentRadius = 2,
            AgentHeight = 5,
            AgentCanJump = true,
            AgentCanClimb = true,
            WaypointSpacing = 6,
        })
        local computeOk = pcall(function()
            path:ComputeAsync(root.Position, destination)
        end)
        local waypoints = nil
        if computeOk and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
            waypoints = path:GetWaypoints()
        else
            waypoints = {
                {
                    Position = destination,
                    Action = Enum.PathWaypointAction.Walk,
                },
            }
        end

        local routeFailed = false
        for index, waypoint in ipairs(waypoints) do
            if index > 1 or #waypoints == 1 then
                if not allowDuringEvent then
                    local eventActive, eventName = App.isSpecialEventActive()
                    if eventActive then
                        return false, tostring(eventName) .. " is active"
                    end
                end
                if character:GetAttribute("IsTrapped") == true then
                    return false, "You got trapped"
                end
                if waypoint.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then
                    humanoid.Jump = true
                end
                local adjustedPosition = App.adjustMovePointForTraps(
                    root.Position,
                    waypoint.Position
                )
                local moved, moveError = App.moveToWaypoint(
                    feature,
                    version,
                    humanoid,
                    root,
                    adjustedPosition,
                    extraCancel
                )
                if not moved then
                    routeFailed = true
                    if moveError == "cancelled" then
                        return false, moveError
                    end
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        local _, _, refreshedRoot = App.getCharacterParts()
        if refreshedRoot
            and (refreshedRoot.Position - destination).Magnitude
                <= App.Config.StealInteractDistance
        then
            return true
        end
        if not routeFailed then
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end
    return false, "No safe route on foot"
end

function App.getTravelSpeed()
    local requested = tonumber(App.Runtime.StealTravel.StudsPerSecond)
        or App.Config.TweenMinSpeed
    return math.clamp(
        requested,
        App.Config.TweenMinSpeed,
        App.Config.TweenMaxSpeed
    )
end






function App.getRelocator()
    return nil
end

function App.getRelocationLift(relocator)
    local heightOk, height = pcall(relocator.GetGroundedRootHeight)
    if heightOk and type(height) == "number" and height > 0 then
        return height
    end
    return App.Config.TweenLandingLift
end




function App.releaseRootAnchor(root)
    if root.Anchored then
        pcall(function()
            root.Anchored = false
        end)
    end
end

function App.relocateRootTo(relocator, goalCFrame)
    local relocateOk, relocated = pcall(relocator.Relocate, goalCFrame)
    if not relocateOk then
        return false, "Teleport was blocked"
    end
    if relocated == false then
        return false, "Character reset mid-trip"
    end
    return true
end

function App.tweenRootTo(
    feature,
    version,
    character,
    humanoid,
    root,
    goalPosition,
    allowDuringEvent
)
    local relocator = App.getRelocator()
    if not relocator then
        return false, App.RelocatorMissingError
    end

    App.releaseRootAnchor(root)
    local origin = root.Position
    local distance = (origin - goalPosition).Magnitude
    if distance <= 0.5 then
        return true
    end
    local heading = (goalPosition - origin).Unit

    local flat = Vector3.new(heading.X, 0, heading.Z)
    local facing = nil
    if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
        facing = flat.Unit
    else
        facing = root.CFrame.LookVector
    end

    local speed = App.getTravelSpeed()
    local deadline = os.clock()
        + math.min(distance / speed + 2, App.Config.TweenHopTimeout)
    local covered = 0
    while true do
        if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
            return false, "cancelled"
        end
        if not character.Parent
            or not humanoid.Parent
            or humanoid.Health <= 0
            or not root.Parent
        then
            return false, "Character reset mid-trip"
        end
        if character:GetAttribute("IsTrapped") == true then
            return false, "You got trapped"
        end
        if not allowDuringEvent then
            local eventActive, eventName = App.isSpecialEventActive()
            if eventActive then
