                return false, tostring(eventName) .. " is active"
            end
            if App.isGameplayAreaBlocked() then
                return false, "Reset wall closed mid-trip"
            end



            if App.Runtime.ActiveTreadmill ~= nil then
                return false, "Treadmill grabbed you, retrying"
            end
        end
        if os.clock() >= deadline then
            return false, "Tween timed out"
        end

        local step = App.Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        covered = math.min(
            distance,
            covered + speed * math.min(step, App.Config.TweenMaxStep)
        )
        local point = origin + heading * covered
        local moved, moveError = App.relocateRootTo(
            relocator,
            CFrame.lookAt(point, point + facing)
        )
        if not moved then
            return false, moveError
        end
        if covered >= distance then
            return true
        end
    end
end




function App.getTweenLift()
    local relocator = App.getRelocator()
    if relocator then
        return Vector3.new(0, App.getRelocationLift(relocator), 0)
    end
    return Vector3.new(0, App.Config.TweenLandingLift, 0)
end




function App.buildTweenPathGoals(startPosition, destination)
    local path = App.Services.PathfindingService:CreatePath({
        AgentRadius = 2,
        AgentHeight = 5,
        AgentCanJump = true,
        AgentCanClimb = true,
        WaypointSpacing = App.Config.TweenWaypointSpacing,
    })
    local computeOk = pcall(function()
        path:ComputeAsync(startPosition, destination)
    end)
    if not computeOk or path.Status ~= Enum.PathStatus.Success then
        return nil
    end

    local lift = App.getTweenLift()
    local waypoints = path:GetWaypoints()
    local goals = {}
    for index = 2, #waypoints do
        table.insert(goals, waypoints[index].Position + lift)
    end
    if #goals == 0 then
        return nil
    end
    return goals
end

function App.getTweenStagingPosition(destination)
    local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
    local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
    local guardAreas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
    if not guardAreas then
        return nil
    end

    local staging, stagingDistance = nil, math.huge
    for _, area in ipairs(guardAreas:GetChildren()) do
        local exitPoint = area:FindFirstChild("ClosestExitPoint")
        if exitPoint and exitPoint:IsA("BasePart") then
            local distance = (exitPoint.Position - destination).Magnitude
            if distance < stagingDistance then
                stagingDistance = distance
                staging = exitPoint.Position
            end
        end
    end
    return staging, stagingDistance
end

function App.buildTweenRoute(root, destination)
    local goals = App.buildTweenPathGoals(root.Position, destination)
    if goals then
        return goals
    end

    local lift = App.getTweenLift()
    local directDistance = (root.Position - destination).Magnitude



    local staging, stagingDistance = App.getTweenStagingPosition(destination)
    if staging and stagingDistance <= App.Config.TweenStagingReach then
        local stagedGoals = App.buildTweenPathGoals(root.Position, staging)
        if stagedGoals then
            table.insert(stagedGoals, destination + lift)
            return stagedGoals
        end
    end
    if directDistance <= App.Config.TweenDirectFallbackDistance then
        return { destination + lift }
    end
    return nil
end

function App.tweenTravelTo(feature, version, destination, allowDuringEvent)
    if typeof(destination) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false, "Invalid destination"
    end

    for _ = 1, App.Config.MoveRepathAttempts do
        if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
            return false, "cancelled"
        end
        local character, humanoid, root, characterError = App.getCharacterParts()
        if not character or not humanoid or not root then
            return false, characterError
        end
        if character:GetAttribute("IsTrapped") == true then
            return false, "You got trapped"
        end
        if not allowDuringEvent then
            local eventActive, eventName = App.isSpecialEventActive()
            if eventActive then
                return false, tostring(eventName) .. " is active"
            end
            if App.isGameplayAreaBlocked() then
                return false, "Reset wall closed mid-trip"
            end
        end
        if (root.Position - destination).Magnitude
            <= App.Config.StealInteractDistance
        then
            return true
        end

        local goals = App.buildTweenRoute(root, destination)
        if not goals then
            return false, "No walkable route to the egg"
        end

        local failure = nil
        for _, goal in ipairs(goals) do
            local hopped, hopError = App.tweenRootTo(
                feature,
                version,
                character,
                humanoid,
                root,
                goal,
                allowDuringEvent
            )
            if not hopped then
                failure = hopError
                break
            end
        end
        if failure == "cancelled" then
            return false, failure
        end

        task.wait(App.Config.TweenSettleDelay)
        local _, _, settledRoot = App.getCharacterParts()
        if settledRoot
            and (settledRoot.Position - destination).Magnitude
                <= App.Config.StealInteractDistance
        then
            return true
        end
        if failure then
            return false, failure
        end
    end
    return false, "Tween could not reach the egg"
end

local DEFAULT_TWEEN_SPEED_MULTIPLIER = 2
local MIN_TWEEN_DURATION = 0.15
local activeMoveTweens = {}

function App.directTravelTo(feature, version, destination, allowDuringEvent, isCancelledFn)
    if typeof(destination) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false, "Invalid destination"
    end
    if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
        return false, "cancelled"
    end
    if not allowDuringEvent then
        local eventActive, eventName = App.isSpecialEventActive()
        if eventActive then
            return false, tostring(eventName) .. " is active"
        end
        if App.isGameplayAreaBlocked() then
            return false, "Reset wall closed"
        end
    end

    local character, humanoid, root, characterError = App.getCharacterParts()
    if not character or not humanoid or not root then
        return false, characterError
    end
    if character:GetAttribute("IsTrapped") == true then
        return false, "You got trapped"
    end

    local function cancelled()
        if isCancelledFn and isCancelledFn() then
            return true
        end
        return not App.featureAlive(feature, version)
    end

    if cancelled() then
        return false, "cancelled"
    end

    App.releaseRootAnchor(root)
    local previousTween = activeMoveTweens[root]
    if previousTween then
        pcall(function()
            previousTween:Cancel()
        end)
        activeMoveTweens[root] = nil
    end

    local goal = destination + Vector3.new(0, App.Config.TweenLandingLift, 0)
    local facing = root.CFrame.LookVector
    local flat = Vector3.new(destination.X - root.Position.X, 0, destination.Z - root.Position.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
        facing = flat.Unit
    end
    local targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(goal, goal + facing)
    local distance = (targetCFrame.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
    if distance < 0.1 then
        root.CFrame = targetCFrame
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        return true
    end

    local speed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
    if not speed or speed <= 0 then
        speed = 16
    end
    speed = speed * DEFAULT_TWEEN_SPEED_MULTIPLIER

    local duration = math.max(distance / speed, MIN_TWEEN_DURATION)
    local tween = App.Services.TweenService:Create(
        root,
        TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
        { CFrame = targetCFrame }
    )
    activeMoveTweens[root] = tween
    tween:Play()

    while tween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing do
        if cancelled() then
            tween:Cancel()
            break
        end
        App.Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end

    if activeMoveTweens[root] == tween then
        activeMoveTweens[root] = nil
    end

    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

    if cancelled() then
        return false, "cancelled"
    end
    return true
end

function App.getAreaEggPrompt(record, position)
    local function promptPosition(prompt)
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
            return nil
        end
        local parent = prompt.Parent
        if parent:IsA("Attachment") then
            return parent.WorldPosition
        end
        if parent:IsA("BasePart") then
            return parent.Position
        end
        local part = parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if part then
            return part.Position
        end
        local model = prompt:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        if model then
            local pivotOk, pivot = pcall(function()
                return model:GetPivot()
            end)
            if pivotOk and typeof(pivot) == "CFrame" then
                return pivot.Position
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function isEggPrompt(prompt)
        if not prompt or not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or not prompt.Parent then
            return false
        end
        local promptName = tostring(prompt.Name):lower()
        local actionText = tostring(prompt.ActionText or ""):lower()
        local objectText = tostring(prompt.ObjectText or ""):lower()
        return promptName == "carryareaegg"
    end
    local function belongsToRecord(prompt)
        if type(record) ~= "table" or type(record.Uid) ~= "string" then
            return false
        end
        local current = prompt
        while current and current ~= App.Services.Workspace do
            if current.Name == record.Uid then
                return true
            end
            local uid = current:GetAttribute("UID") or current:GetAttribute("Uid")
            if tostring(uid or "") == record.Uid then
                return true
            end
            current = current.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local model = App.getAreaEggModel(record)
    if model then
        for _, descendant in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if isEggPrompt(descendant) then
                return descendant
            end
        end
        if isEggPrompt(model) then
            return model
        end
    end
    if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
        return nil
    end
    local nearest, nearestDistance = nil, math.huge
    local workspaceService = App.Services.Workspace
    local slots = workspaceService:FindFirstChild("AreaEggSlotsClient")
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    local function getCachedPrompts(cacheName, root, ttl)
        local cache = App.Runtime[cacheName]
        local untilTime = App.Runtime[cacheName .. "Until"] or 0
        if type(cache) ~= "table" or nowClock >= untilTime then
            cache = {}
            if root then
                for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                    if isEggPrompt(descendant) then
                        table.insert(cache, descendant)
                    end
                end
            end
            App.Runtime[cacheName] = cache
            App.Runtime[cacheName .. "Until"] = nowClock + ttl
        end
        return cache
    end
    local function findNearest(candidates)
        for _, descendant in ipairs(candidates) do
            if descendant.Parent and isEggPrompt(descendant) then
                local promptPositionValue = promptPosition(descendant)
                if promptPositionValue then
                    local distance = (promptPositionValue - position).Magnitude
                    local recordMatch = belongsToRecord(descendant)
                    if recordMatch and distance < nearestDistance and distance <= 150 then
                        nearest = descendant
                        nearestDistance = distance
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    findNearest(getCachedPrompts("AreaEggPromptCache", slots or workspaceService, slots and 0.5 or 1))
    if not nearest and slots then
        findNearest(getCachedPrompts("WorkspaceEggPromptCache", workspaceService, 1.5))
    end
    return nearest
end

local function getFireProximityPrompt()
    local environment = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv() or nil
    return (environment and (environment.fireProximityPrompt or environment.fireproximityprompt))
        or fireProximityPrompt
        or fireproximityprompt
end
function App.fireAreaEggPrompt(record, position)
    local nearest = App.getAreaEggPrompt(record, position)
    if not nearest or not nearest.Parent then
        return false, "Egg prompt was not found"
    end
    local uid = tostring(type(record) == "table" and record.Uid or "")
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    local lastFire = App.Runtime.PromptLastFireAt[uid] or 0
    local cooldown = math.max(0.1, tonumber(App.Config.SafePromptInterval) or 0.2)
    if nowClock - lastFire < cooldown then
        return false, "Prompt cooldown"
    end
    if nearest.Enabled ~= true then
        return false, "Egg prompt is disabled"
    end
    App.Runtime.PromptLastFireAt[uid] = nowClock
    local fired = false
    pcall(function()
        local firePrompt = getFireProximityPrompt()
        if type(firePrompt) == "function" then
            firePrompt(nearest)
        else
            nearest:InputHoldBegin()
            task.wait(math.max(0, tonumber(nearest.HoldDuration) or 0))
            nearest:InputHoldEnd()
        end
        fired = true
    end)
    return fired, fired and nil or "Egg prompt could not be activated"
end

function App.getBackupStealEggPrompt(record, position)
    if type(record) ~= "table" or typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
        return nil
    end
    local uid = tostring(record.Uid or "")
    if uid == "" then return nil end
    local function promptPosition(prompt)
        local parent = prompt and prompt.Parent
        if not parent then return nil end
        if parent:IsA("Attachment") then return parent.WorldPosition end
        if parent:IsA("BasePart") then return parent.Position end
        local part = parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if part then return part.Position end
        local model = prompt:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        if model then
            local ok, pivot = pcall(function() return model:GetPivot() end)
            if ok and typeof(pivot) == "CFrame" then return pivot.Position end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function belongsToRecord(prompt)
        local current = prompt
        while current and current ~= App.Services.Workspace do
            local currentUid = current:GetAttribute("Uid") or current:GetAttribute("UID")
            if current.Name == uid or tostring(currentUid or "") == uid then return true end
            current = current.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function isStealPrompt(prompt)
        if not prompt or not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or not prompt.Parent then return false end
        local promptName = string.lower(tostring(prompt.Name))
        local actionText = string.lower(tostring(prompt.ActionText or ""))
        local objectText = string.lower(tostring(prompt.ObjectText or ""))
        return promptName:find("stealegg", 1, true) ~= nil
            or promptName:find("steal", 1, true) ~= nil
            or actionText:find("steal", 1, true) ~= nil
            or objectText:find("steal", 1, true) ~= nil
    end
    local model = App.getAreaEggModel(record)
    if model then
        for _, descendant in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if isStealPrompt(descendant) then return descendant end
        end
        if isStealPrompt(model) then return model end
    end
    local now = os.clock()
    local cache = App.Runtime.StealEggPromptCache
    if type(cache) ~= "table" or now >= (App.Runtime.StealEggPromptCacheUntil or 0) then
        cache = {}
        local roots = {
            App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("AreaEggSlotsClient"),
            App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS"),
            App.Services.Workspace,
        }
        local seenRoots = {}
        for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
            if root and not seenRoots[root] then
                seenRoots[root] = true
                for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                    if isStealPrompt(descendant) then table.insert(cache, descendant) end
                end
            end
        end
        App.Runtime.StealEggPromptCache = cache
        App.Runtime.StealEggPromptCacheUntil = now + 0.5
    end
    local nearest, nearestDistance = nil, math.huge
    for _, prompt in ipairs(cache) do
        if prompt.Parent and isStealPrompt(prompt) and belongsToRecord(prompt) then
            local promptPositionValue = promptPosition(prompt)
            if promptPositionValue then
                local distance = (promptPositionValue - position).Magnitude
                if distance < nearestDistance and distance <= 80 then
                    nearest = prompt
                    nearestDistance = distance
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nearest
end

function App.fireBackupStealEggPrompt(record, position)
    local prompt = App.getBackupStealEggPrompt(record, position)
    if not prompt then return false, "Backup StealEgg prompt was not found" end
    local uid = tostring(type(record) == "table" and record.Uid or "") .. ":backup"
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    local lastFire = App.Runtime.PromptLastFireAt[uid] or 0
    local cooldown = math.max(0.1, tonumber(App.Config.SafePromptInterval) or 0.2)
    if nowClock - lastFire < cooldown then
        return false, "Backup prompt cooldown"
    end
    if prompt.Enabled ~= true then
        return false, "Backup StealEgg prompt is disabled"
    end
    App.Runtime.PromptLastFireAt[uid] = nowClock
    local fired = false
    pcall(function()
        local firePrompt = getFireProximityPrompt()
        if type(firePrompt) == "function" then
            firePrompt(prompt)
        else
            prompt:InputHoldBegin()
            task.wait(math.max(0, tonumber(prompt.HoldDuration) or 0))
            prompt:InputHoldEnd()
        end
        fired = true
    end)
    return fired, fired and nil or "Backup StealEgg prompt could not be activated"
end
function App.tryLinkedCarryFallback(eggCmds, record, firstAreaSlotKey, position)
    if type(record) ~= "table" or record.Uid == nil then return false, "Missing selected egg UID" end
    local uid = tostring(record.Uid)
    local function carriedByLocalPlayer()
        local ok, live = App.callModuleMethod(eggCmds, "GetAreaEggRecord", uid)
        return ok and type(live) == "table"
            and live.State == "Carried"
            and tostring(live.CarrierUserId) == tostring(App.LocalPlayer.UserId)
    end
    local requestInvokeOk, requestAccepted, requestError = App.callModuleMethod(
        eggCmds,
        "RequestCarryAreaEgg",
        uid,
        firstAreaSlotKey
    )
    if requestInvokeOk and requestAccepted == true then
        task.wait(0.1)
        if carriedByLocalPlayer() then return true end
    end
    local promptAttempts = {
        function() return App.getAreaEggPrompt(record, position) end,
        function() return App.getBackupStealEggPrompt(record, position) end,
    }
    for _, getPrompt in ipairs(promptAttempts) do
        local prompt = getPrompt()
        if prompt and prompt.Parent then
            local fired = false
            pcall(function()
                local duration = prompt.HoldDuration
                local enabled = prompt.Enabled
                local distance = prompt.MaxActivationDistance
                local lineOfSight = prompt.RequiresLineOfSight
                prompt.Enabled = true
                prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
                prompt.MaxActivationDistance = math.max(distance, 10000)
                prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                local firePrompt = getFireProximityPrompt()
                if type(firePrompt) == "function" then
                    firePrompt(prompt)
                else
                    prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                    prompt:InputHoldEnd()
                end
                fired = true
                prompt.HoldDuration = duration
                prompt.Enabled = enabled
                prompt.MaxActivationDistance = distance
                prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = lineOfSight
            end)
            if fired then
                task.wait(0.12)
                if carriedByLocalPlayer() then return true end
            end
        end
    end
    return false, requestError or "Linked carry fallback failed"
end

function App.travelTo(feature, version, destination, allowDuringEvent)
    if App.Runtime.StealTravel.UseTween ~= true then
        return App.moveHumanoidTo(
            feature,
            version,
            destination,
            allowDuringEvent
        )
    end


    if not App.getRelocator() then
        if not App.Runtime.RelocatorWarned then
            App.Runtime.RelocatorWarned = true
            App.notifyError(
                "Tween Teleport",
                "Fast travel is unavailable, so steal trips will run on foot."
            )
        end
        return App.moveHumanoidTo(
            feature,
            version,
            destination,
            allowDuringEvent
        )
    end
    return App.tweenTravelTo(feature, version, destination, allowDuringEvent)
end

function App.canonicalRarity(value)
    if type(value) == "table" then
        value = value._id or value.Id or value.Name or value.DisplayName
    end
    local text = tostring(value or "")
    local compact = text:lower():gsub("[%s_%-%.,]", "")
    for _, option in ipairs(App.Runtime.RarityOrder or {}) do
        local optionText = tostring(option)
        local optionCompact = optionText:lower():gsub("[%s_%-%.,]", "")
        if compact == optionCompact then return optionText end
    end
    return text
end
function App.getAssetRarity(assetCategory)
    local assets = App.Game.Modules.Assets
    local directory = assets and (assets.Directory or assets.Assets or assets)
    local configOk, config = pcall(function()
        return directory and directory[assetCategory]
    end)
    if not configOk or type(config) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local rarity = config.Rarity
    if rarity == nil then
        return nil
    end
    return App.canonicalRarity(rarity)
end

function App.findReadyPlacedEgg()
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    local selectedEgg = App.getSelectedAreaEggInfo()
    local recordsOk, records = pcall(
        eggCmds.GetOwnerRuntimeRecords,
        App.LocalPlayer.UserId
    )
    if not recordsOk or type(records) ~= "table" then
        return nil, records
    end
    local uids = {}
    for uid, record in pairs(records) do
        if type(uid) == "string"
            and type(record) == "table"
            and record.Placement ~= nil
            and App.Runtime.Hatching[uid] ~= true
                and (
                App.Runtime.HatchRetryAt[uid] == nil
                or os.clock() >= App.Runtime.HatchRetryAt[uid]
            )
            and App.recordMatchesAreaEgg(record, selectedEgg)
        then
            table.insert(uids, uid)
        end
    end
    table.sort(uids)
    for _, uid in ipairs(uids) do
        local readyOk, isReady = pcall(eggCmds.IsLocalEggReady, uid)
        if readyOk and isReady == true then
            return uid
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function App.stepAutoHatchEggs(feature, version)
    local uid, findError = App.findReadyPlacedEgg()
    if not uid then
        if findError then
            App.reportFeatureError(feature, findError)
        else
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "No eggs ready to hatch")
        end
        return App.Config.HatchLoopDelay
    end

    App.Runtime.Hatching[uid] = true
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Starting a hatch")
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    local requestOk, accepted, requestError = App.callModuleMethod(
        eggCmds,
        "RequestHatchEgg",
        uid
    )
    if not requestOk or accepted ~= true then
        App.Runtime.Hatching[uid] = nil
        App.Runtime.HatchRetryAt[uid] = os.clock() + 2
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            requestOk and (requestError or "Hatch was rejected")
                or accepted
        )
        return App.Config.HatchLoopDelay
    end

    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Hatching")
    if not App.waitFeature(
        nil,
        nil,
        App.Config.HatchCompleteDelay
    ) then
        App.Runtime.Hatching[uid] = nil
        return 0
    end

    local completeOk, completed, completeError, grantedUid = App.callModuleMethod(
        eggCmds,
        "RequestCompleteHatchEgg",
        uid
    )
    if not completeOk or completed ~= true then
        App.Runtime.Hatching[uid] = nil
        App.Runtime.HatchRetryAt[uid] = os.clock() + 5
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            completeOk and (completeError or "Hatch did not finish")
                or completed
        )
        return App.Config.HatchLoopDelay
    end

    local confirmed = App.waitUntil(
        nil,
        nil,
        App.Config.StateConfirmTimeout,
        function()
        local currentOk, current = App.callModuleMethod(
            eggCmds,
            "GetRuntimeRecord",
            App.LocalPlayer.UserId,
            uid
        )
        if currentOk and current == nil then
            return true
        end
        if type(grantedUid) == "string" then
            local data = App.getSaveData()
            return data
                and type(data.Inventory) == "table"
                and data.Inventory[grantedUid] ~= nil
        end
        return false
        end
    )
    App.Runtime.Hatching[uid] = nil
    if not confirmed then
        App.Runtime.HatchRetryAt[uid] = os.clock() + 10
        if App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] then
            App.reportFeatureError(
                feature,
                "Hatch is taking a while to show up"
            )
        end
        return App.Config.HatchLoopDelay
    end
    App.Runtime.HatchRetryAt[uid] = nil
    App.Runtime.Stats.Hatched = App.Runtime.Stats.Hatched + 1
    App.setFeatureDescription(
        feature,
        "Hatched " .. tostring(App.Runtime.Stats.Hatched)
    )
    return 0.25
end

function App.findAllReadyPlacedEggs()
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    local recordsOk, records = App.callModuleMethod(
        eggCmds,
        "GetOwnerRuntimeRecords",
        App.LocalPlayer.UserId
    )
    if not recordsOk or type(records) ~= "table" then
        return nil, records
    end
    local uids = {}
    for uid, record in pairs(records) do
        if type(uid) == "string"
            and type(record) == "table"
            and record.Placement ~= nil
            and App.Runtime.Hatching[uid] ~= true
        then
            local readyOk, ready = App.callModuleMethod(
                eggCmds,
                "IsLocalEggReady",
                uid
            )
            if readyOk and ready == true then
                table.insert(uids, uid)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(uids)
    return uids
end

function App.stepAutoHatchAllEggs(feature, version)
    local uids, findError = App.findAllReadyPlacedEggs()
    if not uids then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, findError or "Placed egg records are unavailable")
        return App.Config.HatchLoopDelay
    end
    if #uids == 0 then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "No ready eggs")
        return App.Config.HatchLoopDelay
    end
    local hatched = 0
    for _, uid in ipairs(uids) do
        if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then break end
        App.Runtime.Hatching[uid] = true
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Hatching " .. tostring(uid))
        local ok, result = App.hatchIndexEgg(feature, version, uid)
        App.Runtime.Hatching[uid] = nil
        if ok then
            hatched = hatched + 1
            App.Runtime.Stats.Hatched = (tonumber(App.Runtime.Stats.Hatched) or 0) + 1
        else
            App.Runtime.HatchRetryAt[uid] = os.clock() + 5
            App.reportFeatureError(feature, tostring(result or "Hatch failed for " .. tostring(uid)))
        end
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Hatched " .. tostring(hatched) .. " available eggs")
    return 0.25
end

function App.findUnplacedEgg()
    local selectedEgg = App.getSelectedAreaEggInfo()
    local preferredUid = App.Runtime.IndexSystem and App.Runtime.IndexSystem.PlacementUid
    local uid, findError = App.findAreaEggUid(selectedEgg, preferredUid, true)
    if not uid and findError then return nil, findError end
    return uid
end
function App.getExistingEggPositions(plotData)
    local positions = {}
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    local recordsOk, records = pcall(
        eggCmds.GetOwnerRuntimeRecords,
        App.LocalPlayer.UserId
    )
    if not recordsOk or type(records) ~= "table" then
        return positions
    end
    for _, record in pairs(records) do
        local placement = type(record) == "table" and record.Placement or nil
        local localCFrame = placement and placement.LocalCFrame or nil
        if typeof(localCFrame) == "CFrame" then
            table.insert(
                positions,
                (plotData.CenterPoint.CFrame * localCFrame).Position
            )
        end
    end
    return positions
end

function App.findOpenEggPlacement(plotData)
    local petArea = plotData.PetArea
    local margin = App.Config.PlaceMargin
    local spacing = App.Config.PlaceSpacing
    local halfX = math.max(0, petArea.Size.X * 0.5 - margin)
    local halfZ = math.max(0, petArea.Size.Z * 0.5 - margin)
    local occupied = App.getExistingEggPositions(plotData)

    local z = -halfZ
    while z <= halfZ + 0.01 do
        local x = -halfX
        while x <= halfX + 0.01 do
            local worldPosition = petArea.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(
                Vector3.new(x, petArea.Size.Y * 0.5, z)
            )
            local free = true
            for _, occupiedPosition in ipairs(occupied) do
                local flatDelta = Vector3.new(
                    occupiedPosition.X - worldPosition.X,
                    0,
                    occupiedPosition.Z - worldPosition.Z
                )
                if flatDelta.Magnitude < spacing * 0.8 then
                    free = false
                    break
                end
            end
            if free then
                return plotData.CenterPoint.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(
                    CFrame.new(worldPosition)
                )
            end
            x = x + spacing
        end
        z = z + spacing
    end
    return nil
end

function App.isPlotFullResponse(message)
    if type(message) ~= "string" then
        return false
    end
    return string.find(string.lower(message), "too many") ~= nil
end




function App.resetCharacterForPlacement(feature)
    if App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying
        or App.Runtime["StealTripPhase"] ~= nil
    then
        return false
    end
    local character, humanoid = App.getCharacterParts()
    if not character or not humanoid then
        return false
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Resetting so the game accepts placements")
    App.Runtime.PlaceResetAt = os.clock()
    App.Runtime.PlaceFailures = 0
    local spawned = false
    local connection
    connection = App.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        spawned = true
    end)
    pcall(function()
        humanoid.Health = 0
    end)
    local deadline = os.clock() + 10
    while not spawned and os.clock() < deadline do
        task.wait(0.2)
    end
    pcall(function()
        connection:Disconnect()
    end)
    task.wait(App.Config.PlaceResetSettle)
    return spawned
end

function App.reachPlotForPlacement(feature, version, plotData)
    local petArea = plotData and plotData.PetArea
    if not petArea or not petArea:IsA("BasePart") then
        return true
    end




    local steppedOff, treadmillError = App.leaveTreadmill(true)
    if not steppedOff then
        return false, treadmillError
    end

    local _, _, root = App.getCharacterParts()
    if not root then
        return true
    end
    if (root.Position - petArea.Position).Magnitude
        <= App.Config.PlaceReachDistance
    then
        return true
    end

    if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoStealEgg then
        App.setFeatureDescription(
            feature,
            "Waiting until you are back at your plot"
        )
        return false
    end

    local home = App.getPlotReturnPosition()
    if typeof(home) ~= "Vector3" then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Get closer to your plot")
        return false
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Heading to your plot")
    local moved, moveError = App.travelTo(feature, version, home, true)
    if not moved then
        if moveError == "cancelled" or App.isExpectedTripInterruption(moveError) then
            return false
        end
        return false, moveError
    end
    return true
end

function App.stepAutoPlaceEggs(feature, version)
    local uid, uidError = App.findUnplacedEgg()
    if not uid then
        if uidError then
            App.reportFeatureError(feature, uidError)
        else
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "No eggs to place")
        end
        return App.Config.PlaceLoopDelay
    end

    local plotCmds = App.Game.Modules.PlotCmds
    local plotOk, plotData = pcall(plotCmds.GetPlotData)
    if not plotOk or type(plotData) ~= "table" then
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            plotOk and "Your plot is not loaded" or plotData
        )
        return App.Config.PlaceLoopDelay
    end
    local localCFrame = App.findOpenEggPlacement(plotData)
    if not localCFrame then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "No open spots on your plot")
        return App.Config.PlaceLoopDelay
    end




    local reached, reachError = App.reachPlotForPlacement(
        feature,
        version,
        plotData
    )
    if not reached then
        if reachError then
            App.reportFeatureError(feature, reachError)
        end
        return App.Config.PlaceLoopDelay
    end

    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Placing an egg")
    local equipOk, equipped, equipError = App.callModuleMethod(
        eggCmds,
        "RequestEquipTool",
        uid
    )
    if not equipOk or equipped ~= true then
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            equipOk and (equipError or "Could not hold that egg") or equipped
        )
        return App.Config.PlaceLoopDelay
    end
    if not App.waitFeature(feature, version, 0.15) then
        return 0
    end




    local placeOk, placed, placeError
    local placeDeadline = os.clock() + App.Config.PlaceRetrySeconds
    repeat
        placeOk, placed, placeError = App.callModuleMethod(
            eggCmds,
            "RequestPlaceEgg",
            uid,
            localCFrame
        )
        if placeOk and placed == true then
            break
        end


        if placeOk and App.isPlotFullResponse(placeError) then
            pcall(eggCmds.RequestUnequipTool, uid)
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Your plot is full")
            return App.Config.PlaceFullDelay
        end
        if not App.waitFeature(feature, version, App.Config.PlaceRetryInterval) then
            pcall(eggCmds.RequestUnequipTool, uid)
            return 0
        end
    until os.clock() >= placeDeadline
    pcall(eggCmds.RequestUnequipTool, uid)
    if not placeOk or placed ~= true then
        App.Runtime.PlaceFailures = App.Runtime.PlaceFailures + 1


        if App.Runtime.PlaceResetsSinceSuccess >= App.Config.PlaceMaxResets then
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Your plot looks full")
            return App.Config.PlaceFullDelay
        end
        if App.Runtime.PlaceFailures >= App.Config.PlaceResetAfterFailures
            and os.clock() - App.Runtime.PlaceResetAt
                >= App.Config.PlaceResetCooldown
            and App.resetCharacterForPlacement(feature)
        then
            App.Runtime.PlaceResetsSinceSuccess =
                App.Runtime.PlaceResetsSinceSuccess + 1
            return 0.25
        end
        App.setFeatureDescription(
            feature,
            "The game will not take a placement yet"
        )
        return App.Config.PlaceLoopDelay
    end
    App.Runtime.PlaceFailures = 0
    App.Runtime.PlaceResetsSinceSuccess = 0

    local confirmed = App.waitUntil(
        feature,
        version,
        App.Config.StateConfirmTimeout,
        function()
            local record = eggCmds.GetRuntimeRecord(
                App.LocalPlayer.UserId,
                uid
            )
            return type(record) == "table" and record.Placement ~= nil
        end
    )
    if not confirmed then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Placement did not show up in time")
        return App.Config.PlaceLoopDelay
    end
    App.Runtime.Stats.Placed = App.Runtime.Stats.Placed + 1
    App.setFeatureDescription(
        feature,
        "Placed " .. tostring(App.Runtime.Stats.Placed)
    )
    return 0.25
end

function App.equippedSignature(equippedAssets)
    local values = {}
    for _, uid in ipairs(
        type(equippedAssets) == "table" and equippedAssets or {}
    ) do
        table.insert(values, tostring(uid))
    end
    table.sort(values)
    return table.concat(values, "|")
end

function App.stepAutoEquip(feature)
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, dataError)
        return App.Config.EquipLoopDelay
    end
    local before = App.equippedSignature(data.EquippedAssets)
    local backpack = App.Game.Constants.NETWORK_MAP.Backpack
    local callOk, accepted, requestError = App.invokeNetwork(
        backpack and backpack.EQUIP_BEST
    )
    if not callOk or accepted ~= true then
        local errorMessage = callOk and requestError or accepted
        if errorMessage == "DEBOUNCE" or errorMessage == "LOCKED" then
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for equip cooldown")
        else
            App.reportFeatureError(
                feature,
                errorMessage or "Could not equip your pets"
            )
        end
        return App.Config.EquipLoopDelay
    end

    task.wait(0.35)
    local refreshed = App.getSaveData()
    local after = refreshed
        and App.equippedSignature(refreshed.EquippedAssets)
        or before
    if before == after then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Best pets already equipped")
    else
        App.Runtime.Stats.Equipped = App.Runtime.Stats.Equipped + 1
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Equipped best pets")
    end
    return App.Config.EquipLoopDelay
end

function App.unequipAllNow()
    local removed = 0
    local assetCmds = App.Game.Modules.AssetCmds
    if not assetCmds then
        local ok, module = App.requirePath({"Library", "Client", "AssetCmds"}, "AssetCmds")
        if ok then
            assetCmds = module
            App.Game.Modules.AssetCmds = module
        end
    end
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    if not eggCmds then
        local ok, module = App.requirePath({"Library", "Client", "EggCmds"}, "EggCmds")
        if ok then
            eggCmds = module
            App.Game.Modules.EggCmds = module
        end
    end
    local containers = {
        App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"),
        App.LocalPlayer.Character,
    }
    local seen = {}
    for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
        if container then
            for _, object in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                local uid = object:GetAttribute("UID") or object:GetAttribute("Uid")
                if uid and not seen[tostring(uid)] then
                    seen[tostring(uid)] = true
                    local itemType = object:GetAttribute("ItemType") or object:GetAttribute("Category")
                    local ok = false
                    if itemType == "Egg" and eggCmds and type(eggCmds.RequestUnequipTool) == "function" then
                        ok = pcall(eggCmds.RequestUnequipTool, tostring(uid))
                    elseif assetCmds and type(assetCmds.RequestUnequipTool) == "function" then
                        ok = pcall(assetCmds.RequestUnequipTool, tostring(uid))
                    end
                    if ok then
                        removed = removed + 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local humanoid = App.getLocalHumanoid()
    if humanoid then
        pcall(humanoid.UnequipTools, humanoid)
    end
    return true, removed
end

function App.getRebirthButton()
    local playerGui = App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    local screen = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("Rebirth")
    local panel = screen and screen:FindFirstChild("Rebirth")
    local content = panel and panel:FindFirstChild("Content")
    local button = content and content:FindFirstChild("Rebirth")
    if button and button:IsA("GuiButton") then
        return button
    end
    return nil
end

function App.fireExistingSignal(button)
    if not button or not button:IsA("GuiButton") then
        return false, "Rebirth is unavailable right now"
    end
    local ok, result = pcall(button.Activate, button)
    return ok, result
end

function App.stepAutoRebirth(feature, version)
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, dataError)
        return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
    end
    local nextRebirth = (tonumber(data.Rebirth) or 0) + 1
    local config = App.Game.Modules.Rebirths[nextRebirth]
    if type(config) ~= "table" then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Maximum rebirth reached")
        return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
    end
    local requirements = config.Requirements or {}
    local requiredSpeed = tonumber(requirements.RequiredSpeedPower)
        or math.huge
    local currentSpeed = tonumber(data.SpeedPower) or 0
    if currentSpeed < requiredSpeed then
        App.setFeatureDescription(
            feature,
            "Waiting for " .. tostring(requiredSpeed) .. " speed"
        )
        return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
    end

    local rebirthMap = App.Game.Constants.NETWORK_MAP.Rebirth
    local networkFolder = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
    local requestRemote = networkFolder
        and rebirthMap
        and networkFolder:FindFirstChild(rebirthMap.REQUEST_REBIRTH)
    local commitRemote = networkFolder
        and rebirthMap
        and networkFolder:FindFirstChild(rebirthMap.REQUEST_REBIRTH_COMMIT)
    if not requestRemote or not commitRemote then
        App.setFeatureDescription(
            feature,
            "Waiting for rebirth to be available"
        )
        return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
    end

    local requestEndpoint = rebirthMap.REQUEST_REBIRTH
    local commitEndpoint = rebirthMap.REQUEST_REBIRTH_COMMIT
    local directRequestOk, directRequestResult = App.invokeNetwork(requestEndpoint)
    if directRequestOk and directRequestResult ~= false then
        task.wait(0.12)
        local directCommitOk, directCommitResult = App.invokeNetwork(commitEndpoint)
        if directCommitOk and directCommitResult ~= false then
            local directConfirmed = App.waitUntil(
                feature,
                version,
                App.Config.StateConfirmTimeout,
                function()
                    local refreshed = App.getSaveData()
                    return refreshed
                        and (tonumber(refreshed.Rebirth) or 0) >= nextRebirth
                end
            )
            if directConfirmed then
                App.Runtime.Stats.Rebirths = App.Runtime.Stats.Rebirths + 1
                App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Rebirth complete: " .. tostring(nextRebirth))
                return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
            end
        end
    end

    local button = App.getRebirthButton()
    if not button then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Rebirth is unavailable right now")
        return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Rebirthing to " .. nextRebirth)
    local tabController = App.Game.Modules.TabController
    local signal = App.Game.Modules.Signal
    local openOk, opened = pcall(tabController.OpenTab, "Rebirth")
    if not openOk or opened ~= true then
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            openOk and "Could not open the rebirth menu" or opened
        )
        return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
    end

    App.waitUntil(feature, version, 2, function()
        return tabController.IsOpen("Rebirth") == true
    end)
    local pressOk, pressError = pcall(
        signal.Fire,
        "Console: Pressed Button",
        button
    )
    task.wait(0.08)
    local releaseOk, releaseError = pcall(
        signal.Fire,
        "Console: Released Button",
        button
    )
    if not pressOk or not releaseOk then
        pcall(tabController.CloseTab, true)
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            pressOk and releaseError or pressError
        )
        return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
    end

    local confirmed = App.waitUntil(
        feature,
        version,
        2,
        function()
            local refreshed = App.getSaveData()
            return refreshed
                and (tonumber(refreshed.Rebirth) or 0) >= nextRebirth
        end
    )
    if not confirmed then
        App.fireExistingSignal(button)
        confirmed = App.waitUntil(
            feature,
            version,
            App.Config.StateConfirmTimeout,
            function()
                local refreshed = App.getSaveData()
                return refreshed
                    and (tonumber(refreshed.Rebirth) or 0) >= nextRebirth
            end
        )
    end
    pcall(tabController.CloseTab, true)
    if not confirmed then
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            "Rebirth did not go through"
        )
        return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
    end

    confirmed = App.waitUntil(
        feature,
        version,
        1,
        function()
            local refreshed = App.getSaveData()
            return refreshed
                and (tonumber(refreshed.Rebirth) or 0) >= nextRebirth
        end
    )
    if not confirmed then
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            "Rebirth did not stick"
        )
        return App.Config.RebirthLoopDelay
    end
    App.Runtime.Stats.Rebirths = App.Runtime.Stats.Rebirths + 1
    App.setFeatureDescription(
        feature,
        "Completed rebirth " .. tostring(nextRebirth)
    )
    return 0.5
end

function App.stepAutoUpgradeBasePlot(feature, version)
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, dataError)
        return App.Config.UpgradeLoopDelay
    end
    local nextLevel = (tonumber(data.BaseUpgradeLevel) or 0) + 1
    local bases = App.Game.Modules.Bases
    local config = bases and bases.BASES and bases.BASES[nextLevel]
    if type(config) ~= "table" then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Maximum base level reached")
        return App.Config.UpgradeLoopDelay
    end


    local slots = tonumber(config.MaxAssets)
    local slotSuffix = slots and (" for " .. slots .. " equip slots") or ""
    local cost = tonumber(config.Cost) or math.huge
    if (tonumber(data.Money) or 0) < cost then
        App.setFeatureDescription(
            feature,
            "Saving $" .. App.formatMoney(cost) .. slotSuffix
        )
        return App.Config.UpgradeLoopDelay
    end

    local upgradeClient = App.Game.Modules.BaseUpgradeClient
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Buying base level " .. nextLevel)
    local requestOk, requested = pcall(upgradeClient.RequestCashUpgrade)
    if not requestOk or requested ~= true then
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            requestOk and "Base upgrade was rejected" or requested
        )
        return App.Config.UpgradeLoopDelay
    end
    local confirmed = App.waitUntil(
        feature,
        version,
        App.Config.StateConfirmTimeout + 3,
        function()
            local refreshed = App.getSaveData()
            return refreshed
                and (tonumber(refreshed.BaseUpgradeLevel) or 0) >= nextLevel
        end
    )
    if not confirmed then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Base upgrade did not show up in time")
        return App.Config.UpgradeLoopDelay
    end
    App.Runtime.Stats.BaseUpgrades = App.Runtime.Stats.BaseUpgrades + 1
    App.setFeatureDescription(
        feature,
        "Base level " .. tostring(nextLevel) .. slotSuffix:gsub("^ for", ",")
    )
    return 0.4
end



function App.getNextTreadmill(data)
    local treadmills = App.Game.Modules.Treadmills
    if type(treadmills) ~= "table"
        or type(treadmills.GetByUpgradeLevel) ~= "function"
    then
        return nil, nil, "Treadmill upgrades are unavailable"
    end
    local nextLevel = (tonumber(data.TreadmillUpgradeLevel) or 0) + 1
    local configOk, config = pcall(treadmills.GetByUpgradeLevel, nextLevel)
    if not configOk or type(config) ~= "table" then
        return nextLevel, nil
    end
    return nextLevel, config
end

function App.stepAutoUpgradeTreadmill(feature, version)
    local multiplier = tonumber(App.Runtime.TreadmillMultiplier) or 1
    local interval = tonumber(App.Runtime.TreadmillInterval) or 0.15
    local speedOk, speedResult = App.callReplicatedNetwork(
        { "Treadmills: SpeedGain", "Treadmills.SpeedGain" },
        multiplier
    )
    if not speedOk then
        speedOk, speedResult = App.invokeNetwork("Treadmills: SpeedGain", multiplier)
    end
    local upgradeOk, upgradeResult = true, nil
    if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoUpgradeTreadmill == true then
        upgradeOk, upgradeResult = App.callReplicatedNetwork(
            { "Treadmills: RequestUpgrade", "Treadmills.RequestUpgrade" }
        )
        if not upgradeOk then
            upgradeOk, upgradeResult = App.invokeNetwork("Treadmills: RequestUpgrade")
        end
    end
    local equipOk, equipResult = true, nil
    if App.Runtime.AutoEquipBestTreadmill ~= false then
        equipOk, equipResult = App.callReplicatedNetwork(
            { "Treadmills: RequestEquipStatic", "Treadmills.RequestEquipStatic" }
        )
        if not equipOk then
            equipOk, equipResult = App.invokeNetwork("Treadmills: RequestEquipStatic")
        end
    end
    if speedOk and upgradeOk and equipOk then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Farming treadmill speed and power")
    elseif not speedOk then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, speedResult or "Treadmill speed remote unavailable")
    elseif not upgradeOk then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, upgradeResult or "Treadmill upgrade remote unavailable")
    else
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, equipResult or "Treadmill equip remote unavailable")
    end
    return interval
end

function App.getSortedShopTrails()
    local trails = App.Game.Modules.Trails
    local result = {}
    for id, config in pairs(trails and trails.Directory or {}) do
        if type(config) == "table" and config.DisplayInShop == true then
            table.insert(result, {
                Id = id,
                Config = config,
            })
        end
    end
    table.sort(result, function(left, right)
        local leftPrice = tonumber(left.Config.Price) or math.huge
        local rightPrice = tonumber(right.Config.Price) or math.huge
        if leftPrice == rightPrice then
            return tostring(left.Id) < tostring(right.Id)
        end
        return leftPrice < rightPrice
    end)
    return result
end

function App.getBestOwnedTrail(data, sortedTrails)
    local best = nil
    for _, entry in ipairs(sortedTrails) do
        if type(data.TrailInventory) == "table"
            and data.TrailInventory[entry.Id] == true
        then
            local multiplier = tonumber(entry.Config.SpeedMultiplier) or 1
            if not best
                or multiplier > best.Multiplier
                or (
                    multiplier == best.Multiplier
                    and (tonumber(entry.Config.Price) or 0) > best.Price
                )
            then
                best = {
                    Id = entry.Id,
                    Multiplier = multiplier,
                    Price = tonumber(entry.Config.Price) or 0,
                }
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

function App.selectBestOwnedTrail(feature, data, sortedTrails)
    local best = App.getBestOwnedTrail(data, sortedTrails)
    if not best or data.EquippedTrail == best.Id then
        return true, best
    end
    local trailMap = App.Game.Constants.NETWORK_MAP.Trails
    local callOk, selected, selectError = App.invokeNetwork(
        trailMap and trailMap.REQUEST_SELECT,
        best.Id
    )
    if not callOk or selected ~= true then
        return false, callOk and selectError or selected
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Equipped the " .. tostring(best.Id) .. " trail")
    return true, best
end

function App.stepAutoBuyTrails(feature, version)
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, dataError)
        return App.Config.TrailLoopDelay
    end
    local sortedTrails = App.getSortedShopTrails()
    local money = tonumber(data.Money) or 0
    local nextAffordable = nil
    local nextUnowned = nil
    for _, entry in ipairs(sortedTrails) do
        local owned = type(data.TrailInventory) == "table"
            and data.TrailInventory[entry.Id] == true
        if not owned then
            nextUnowned = nextUnowned or entry
            if money >= (tonumber(entry.Config.Price) or math.huge) then
                nextAffordable = entry
                break
            end
        end
    end

    if not nextAffordable then
        local selectedOk, selectError = App.selectBestOwnedTrail(
            feature,
            data,
            sortedTrails
        )
        if not selectedOk then
            App.reportFeatureError(
                feature,
                selectError or "Owned trail could not be equipped"
            )
        elseif nextUnowned then
            App.setFeatureDescription(
                feature,
                "Waiting for $"
                    .. tostring(nextUnowned.Config.Price)
                    .. " for "
                    .. tostring(nextUnowned.Id)
            )
        else
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "All cash trails owned")
        end
        return App.Config.TrailLoopDelay
    end

    local trailMap = App.Game.Constants.NETWORK_MAP.Trails
    App.setFeatureDescription(
        feature,
        "Buying " .. tostring(nextAffordable.Id)
    )
    local callOk, purchased, purchaseError = App.invokeNetwork(
        trailMap and trailMap.REQUEST_PURCHASE,
        nextAffordable.Id
    )
    if not callOk or purchased ~= true then
        App.reportFeatureError(
            feature,
            callOk and (purchaseError or "Trail purchase was rejected")
                or purchased
        )
        return App.Config.TrailLoopDelay
    end
    local confirmed = App.waitUntil(
        feature,
        version,
        App.Config.StateConfirmTimeout,
        function()
            local refreshed = App.getSaveData()
            return refreshed
                and type(refreshed.TrailInventory) == "table"
                and refreshed.TrailInventory[nextAffordable.Id] == true
        end
    )
    if not confirmed then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Trail did not show up in time")
        return App.Config.TrailLoopDelay
    end
    App.Runtime.Stats.TrailsBought = App.Runtime.Stats.TrailsBought + 1
    App.setFeatureDescription(
        feature,
        "Bought " .. tostring(nextAffordable.Id)
    )
    return 0.35
end

function App.syncAutoSellMap(feature, version, desired)
    local backpack = App.Game.Constants.NETWORK_MAP.Backpack
    local callOk, accepted, returnedState = App.invokeNetwork(
        backpack and backpack.SET_AUTO_SELL_STATE,
        desired
    )
    if not callOk or accepted ~= true then
        return false, callOk
            and (returnedState or "Auto sell update was rejected")
            or accepted
    end
    if type(returnedState) == "table"
        and App.trueMapsEqual(returnedState, desired)
    then
        return true
    end
    local confirmed = App.waitUntil(
        feature,
        version,
        App.Config.StateConfirmTimeout,
        function()
            local data = App.getSaveData()
            return data
                and App.trueMapsEqual(data.AutoSellRarities, desired)
        end
    )
    if confirmed then
        return true, nil
    end
    return false, "Auto sell did not apply"
end

function App.raritySelected(selection, rarity)
    if type(selection) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end
    if selection[rarity] == true then
        return true
    end
    for _, value in pairs(selection) do
        if value == rarity then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

function App.getAssetDirectoryEntry(assetId)
    local assets = App.Game.Modules.Assets
    local directory = assets and assets.Directory
    local ok, entry = pcall(function()
        return directory and assetId and directory[assetId]
    end)
    return ok and type(entry) == "table" and entry or nil
end

function App.getInventoryAssetRarity(record)
    if type(record) ~= "table" then
        return "Unknown"
    end
    local assetId = record.Category or record.AssetCategory or record.id or record.ItemId
    local entry = App.getAssetDirectoryEntry(assetId)
    local rarity = entry and entry.Rarity or record.Rarity
    if type(rarity) == "table" then
        return tostring(rarity.DisplayName or rarity._id or rarity.Name or "Unknown")
    end
    return tostring(rarity or App.getAssetRarity(assetId) or "Unknown")
end

function App.getDirectSellRemote()
    local networkFolder = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
    if not networkFolder then
        return nil
    end
    return networkFolder:FindFirstChild("AssetInventory: SellAsset")
        or networkFolder:FindFirstChild("AssetInventory.SellAsset")
end

function App.fireDirectSell(remote, uid)
    if not remote then
        return false, "Asset sell remote is unavailable"
    end
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            remote:FireServer({uid})
            return true
        end
        if remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            return remote:InvokeServer({uid})
        end
        return false
    end)
    if not ok then
        return false, result
    end
    if result == false then
        return false, "Asset sell request was rejected"
    end
    return true
end

function App.stepAutoSellPets(feature, version)
    if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoStealEgg then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting while stealing")
        return 2
    end
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, dataError)
        return 2
    end
    local sellAll = App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoSellAllPets == true
    local selection = App.Runtime.SelectedSellPetRarities
    local inventory = type(data.Inventory) == "table" and data.Inventory or {}
    local equipped = type(data.EquippedAssets) == "table" and data.EquippedAssets or {}
    local remote = App.getDirectSellRemote()
    local sold = 0
    local matched = 0
    for uid, record in pairs(inventory) do
        if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
            return 0
        end
        if type(record) == "table" then
            local favorite = record.IsFavorite == true
                or record.Favorite == true
                or record["Jadikan Favorit"] == true
            local isEquipped = record.Equipped == true
                or record.IsEquipped == true
                or record._eq == true
                or table.find(equipped, uid) ~= nil
            local rarity = App.getInventoryAssetRarity(record)
            if (sellAll or App.raritySelected(selection, rarity)) and not favorite then
                matched = matched + 1
                if not isEquipped then
                    local assetCmds = App.Game.Modules.AssetCmds
                    if not assetCmds then
                        local moduleOk, module = App.requirePath({"Library", "Client", "AssetCmds"}, "AssetCmds")
                        if moduleOk then
                            assetCmds = module
                            App.Game.Modules.AssetCmds = module
                        end
                    end
                    if assetCmds and type(assetCmds.RequestEquipAsset) == "function" then
                        pcall(assetCmds.RequestEquipAsset, uid)
                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end
                    local sellOk = App.fireDirectSell(remote, uid)
                    if sellOk then
                        sold = sold + 1
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, string.format("Sold %d pet(s), %d matched", sold, matched))
    return 2
end

function App.stepAutoSellEggs(feature, version)
    if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoStealEgg then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting while stealing")
        return 2
    end
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    if not eggCmds or type(eggCmds.GetOwnerRuntimeRecords) ~= "function" then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Egg inventory module is unavailable")
        return 2
    end
    local recordsOk, records = App.callModuleMethod(eggCmds, "GetOwnerRuntimeRecords", App.LocalPlayer.UserId)
    if not recordsOk or type(records) ~= "table" then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, recordsOk and "Egg inventory is unavailable" or records)
        return 2
    end
    local data = App.getSaveData()
    local equipped = data and type(data.EquippedAssets) == "table" and data.EquippedAssets or {}
    local sellAll = App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoSellAllEggs == true
    local selection = App.Runtime.SelectedSellEggRarities
    local remote = App.getDirectSellRemote()
    local sold = 0
    local matched = 0
    for uid, record in pairs(records) do
        if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
            return 0
        end
        if type(record) == "table" then
            local favorite = record.IsFavorite == true
                or record.Favorite == true
                or record["Jadikan Favorit"] == true
            local placed = record.Placement ~= nil
            local isEquipped = placed
                or record.Equipped == true
                or record.IsEquipped == true
                or record._eq == true
                or table.find(equipped, uid) ~= nil
            local rarity = App.getInventoryAssetRarity(record)
            if (sellAll or App.raritySelected(selection, rarity)) and not favorite then
                matched = matched + 1
                if not placed and not isEquipped then
                    local equipOk, accepted = App.callModuleMethod(eggCmds, "RequestEquipTool", tostring(uid))
                    if equipOk and accepted == true then
                        task.wait(0.15)
                        remote = remote or App.getDirectSellRemote()
                        local sellOk = App.fireDirectSell(remote, tostring(uid))
                        if sellOk then
                            sold = sold + 1
                        end
                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, string.format("Sold %d egg(s), %d matched", sold, matched))
    return 2
end



function App.readableSwatch(colour)
    if typeof(colour) ~= "Color3" then
        return colour
    end
    local hue, saturation, value = colour:ToHSV()
    if value >= App.Config.SwatchMinValue then
        return colour
    end
    return Color3.fromHSV(hue, saturation, App.Config.SwatchMinValue)
end



function App.formatMoney(amount)
    local value = tonumber(amount)
    if not value then
        return tostring(amount)
    end
    local units = { { 1e12, "T" }, { 1e9, "B" }, { 1e6, "M" }, { 1e3, "K" } }
    for _, unit in ipairs(units) do
        if value >= unit[1] then
            local scaled = value / unit[1]
            local text = string.format("%.1f", scaled):gsub("%.0$", "")
            return text .. unit[2]
        end
    end
    return string.format("%d", value)
end

function App.getRarityColour(rarity)
    local rarityDirectory = App.Game.Modules.Rarity
        and App.Game.Modules.Rarity.Rarities
    local configOk, config = pcall(function()
        return rarityDirectory and rarityDirectory[rarity]
    end)
    if configOk
        and type(config) == "table"
        and typeof(config.Color) == "Color3"
    then
        return App.readableSwatch(config.Color)
    end
    return nil
end

function App.getRarityRank(rarity)
    rarity = App.canonicalRarity(rarity)
    local rarityDirectory = App.Game.Modules.Rarity
        and App.Game.Modules.Rarity.Rarities
    local configOk, config = pcall(function()
        return rarityDirectory and rarityDirectory[rarity]
    end)
    if configOk
        and type(config) == "table"
        and type(config.RarityNumber) == "number"
    then
        return config.RarityNumber
    end
    for index, name in ipairs(App.Runtime.RarityOrder) do
        if name == rarity then
            return index
        end
    end
    return 0
end

function App.callModuleMethod(module, methodName, ...)
    if type(module) ~= "table" or type(module[methodName]) ~= "function" then
        return false, "Module method is unavailable: " .. tostring(methodName)
    end
    local throttledMethod = methodName == "RequestAreaEggSnapshot"
        or methodName == "RequestCarryAreaEgg"
        or methodName == "RequestDropHeldAreaEgg"
    if throttledMethod then
        local nowClock = os.clock()
        local lastCallAt = App.Runtime.ModuleMethodLastCallAt[methodName] or 0
        local minimumInterval = math.max(0.06, tonumber(App.Config.SafeNetworkMinInterval) or 0.06)
        local waitTime = minimumInterval - (nowClock - lastCallAt)
        if waitTime > 0 then task.wait(math.min(waitTime, 0.25)) end
        App.Runtime.ModuleMethodLastCallAt[methodName] = os.clock()
    end
    local method = module[methodName]
    local args = table.pack(...)
    local direct = table.pack(pcall(method, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)))
    if direct[1] then
        return true, table.unpack(direct, 2, direct.n)
    end
    local withSelf = table.pack(pcall(method, module, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)))
    if withSelf[1] then
        return true, table.unpack(withSelf, 2, withSelf.n)
    end
    return false, withSelf[2] or direct[2]
end
function App.getAreaEggRecords(forceRefresh)
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    if not forceRefresh
        and type(App.Runtime.AreaEggRecordsCache) == "table"
        and nowClock < (App.Runtime.AreaEggRecordsCacheUntil or 0)
    then
        return App.Runtime.AreaEggRecordsCache
    end
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    local ok, snapshot = App.callModuleMethod(eggCmds, "GetAreaEggSnapshot")
    if not ok then
        return nil, snapshot
    end
    if type(snapshot) ~= "table" or type(snapshot.Records) ~= "table" then
        return nil, "Egg list is unavailable"
    end
    App.Runtime.AreaEggRecordsCache = snapshot.Records
    App.Runtime.AreaEggRecordsCacheUntil = nowClock + 0.2
    return snapshot.Records
end

function App.getAreaEggModel(record)
    if type(record) ~= "table" or type(record.Uid) ~= "string" then
        return nil
    end
    local workspaceService = App.Services.Workspace
    local clientSlots = workspaceService:FindFirstChild("AreaEggSlotsClient")
    local direct = clientSlots and clientSlots:FindFirstChild(record.Uid, true)
    if direct then
        return direct
    end
    local fallback = workspaceService:FindFirstChild(record.Uid, true)
    if fallback and fallback ~= workspaceService then
        return fallback
    end
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    local cache = App.Runtime.AreaEggModelCache
    if type(cache) ~= "table"
        or nowClock >= (App.Runtime.AreaEggModelCacheUntil or 0)
    then
        cache = {}
        for _, descendant in ipairs(workspaceService:GetDescendants()) do
            if not cache[descendant.Name] then
                cache[descendant.Name] = descendant
            end
            local uid = descendant:GetAttribute("UID") or descendant:GetAttribute("Uid")
            if uid ~= nil and not cache[tostring(uid)] then
                cache[tostring(uid)] = descendant
            end
        end
        App.Runtime.AreaEggModelCache = cache
        App.Runtime.AreaEggModelCacheUntil = nowClock + 0.5
    end
    local cached = cache[record.Uid]
    return cached and cached.Parent and cached or nil
end
function App.getAreaEggWeight(record)
    if type(record) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local direct = record.Weight or record.WeightKg or record.KG or record.AssetWeight
    local value = tonumber(direct)
    if value then
        return value
    end
    local model = App.getAreaEggModel(record)
    if not model then
        return nil
    end
    for _, attributeName in ipairs({"Weight", "WeightKg", "KG", "SizeKg", "EggWeight"}) do
        value = tonumber(model:GetAttribute(attributeName))
        if value then
            return value
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function App.getAreaEggMutations(record)
    local names = {}
    local seen = {}
    if type(record) == "table" then
        local function add(value)
            if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" and not seen[value] then
                seen[value] = true
                table.insert(names, value)
            elseif type(value) == "table" then
                for _, nested in pairs(value) do
                    add(nested)
                end
            end
        end
        add(record.BaseMutation)
        add(record.Mutations)
        local model = App.getAreaEggModel(record)
        if model then
            add(model:GetAttribute("BaseMutation"))
            add(model:GetAttribute("Mutation"))
            add(model:GetAttribute("Mutations"))
        end
    end
    if #names == 0 then
        names[1] = "None"
    end
    return names
end
function App.getAreaEggMutationKey(record)
    return table.concat(App.getAreaEggMutations(record), ", ")
end
function App.refreshStealMutations()
    local records = App.getAreaEggRecords()
    local values = {"All Mutations"}
    local seen = { [values[1]] = true }
    if type(records) == "table" then
        for _, record in ipairs(records) do
            for _, mutation in ipairs(App.getAreaEggMutations(record)) do
                if not seen[mutation] then
                    seen[mutation] = true
                    table.insert(values, mutation)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(values, function(left, right)
        if left == "All Mutations" then return true end
        if right == "All Mutations" then return false end
        return left < right
    end)
    return values
end
function App.startMutationRefresh(callback)
    if type(App.Runtime.MutationRefreshThread) == "thread" then
        task.cancel(App.Runtime.MutationRefreshThread)
    end
    App.Runtime.MutationRefreshThread = task.spawn(function()
        while not App.Runtime.Unloading do
            if type(callback) == "function" then
                pcall(callback)
            end
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)
end
function App.stopMutationRefresh()
    if type(App.Runtime.MutationRefreshThread) == "thread" then
        task.cancel(App.Runtime.MutationRefreshThread)
    end
    App.Runtime.MutationRefreshThread = nil
end
function App.schedulePromptSettings()
    if App.Runtime.PromptRefreshScheduled then
        return
    end
    App.Runtime.PromptRefreshScheduled = true
    task.delay(0.2, function()
        App.Runtime.PromptRefreshScheduled = false
        if not App.Runtime.Unloading then
            pcall(App.applyPromptSettings)
        end
    end)
end
function App.applyPromptSettings()
    local roots = {}
    local slots = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("AreaEggSlotsClient")
    local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
    local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
    if slots then table.insert(roots, slots) end
    if areas then table.insert(roots, areas) end
    local current = {}
    for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
        current[root] = true
        if not App.Runtime.PromptConnections[root] then
            App.Runtime.PromptConnections[root] = root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
                if instance:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                    task.defer(function()
                        if instance.Parent and not App.Runtime.Unloading then
                            App.schedulePromptSettings()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
        for _, instance in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            if instance:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                current[instance] = true
                if App.Runtime.PromptBackup[instance] == nil then
                    App.Runtime.PromptBackup[instance] = {
                        Enabled = instance.Enabled,
                        HoldDuration = instance.HoldDuration,
                    }
                end
                local backup = App.Runtime.PromptBackup[instance]
                local isGameplayPrompt = areas ~= nil and instance:IsDescendantOf(areas)
                if App.Runtime.RemoveGameplayPrompt and isGameplayPrompt then
                    instance.Enabled = false
                else
                    instance.Enabled = backup.Enabled
                end
                instance.HoldDuration = App.Runtime.InstantPrompt and 0 or backup.HoldDuration
            end
        end
    end
    for root, connection in pairs(App.Runtime.PromptConnections) do
        if not current[root] then
            pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
            App.Runtime.PromptConnections[root] = nil
        end
    end
    for instance, backup in pairs(App.Runtime.PromptBackup) do
        if not instance.Parent then
            App.Runtime.PromptBackup[instance] = nil
        elseif not current[instance] and not App.Runtime.RemoveGameplayPrompt then
            pcall(function()
                instance.Enabled = backup.Enabled
                instance.HoldDuration = backup.HoldDuration
            end)
        end
    end
end
function App.isAreaEggBig(record)
    if type(record) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end
    local model = App.getAreaEggModel(record)
    if model and model:GetAttribute("IsBig") == true then
        return true
    end
    local uid = tostring(record.Uid or "")
    if uid == "" then
        return false
    end
    local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
    local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
    if not areas then
        return false
    end
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    local bigCache = App.Runtime.BigAreaEggCache
    if type(bigCache) ~= "table"
        or nowClock >= (App.Runtime.BigAreaEggCacheUntil or 0)
    then
        bigCache = {}
        for _, instance in ipairs(areas:GetDescendants()) do
            local matchedUid
            for _, attributeName in ipairs({"Uid", "UID", "AreaEggUid", "EggUid"}) do
                local value = instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)
                if value ~= nil then
                    matchedUid = tostring(value)
                    break
                end
            end
            local instanceUid = matchedUid or instance.Name
            if instanceUid and instance:GetAttribute("IsBig") == true then
                bigCache[instanceUid] = true
            end
            local parent = instance.Parent
            if instanceUid and parent and parent:GetAttribute("IsBig") == true then
                bigCache[instanceUid] = true
            end
        end
        App.Runtime.BigAreaEggCache = bigCache
        App.Runtime.BigAreaEggCacheUntil = nowClock + 1.5
    end
    return bigCache[uid] == true
end
function App.firstAreaEggBelongsToLocalPlayer(record)
    local identity = App.Game.Modules.AreaEggSlotIdentity
    local isFirstOk, isFirst = App.callModuleMethod(
        identity,
        "IsFirstAreaUid",
        record.Uid
    )
    if not isFirstOk or isFirst ~= true then
        return true
    end
    local ownerOk, ownerUserId = App.callModuleMethod(
        identity,
        "GetFirstAreaOwnerUserId",
        record.Uid
    )
    return ownerOk and ownerUserId == App.LocalPlayer.UserId
end

function App.matchesSelectedStealArea(record)
    local runtime = App.Runtime
    if runtime.SelectedStealAreaId == nil then return true end
    if type(record) ~= "table" then return false end
    local wantedId = tostring(runtime.SelectedStealAreaId):lower()
    local wantedName = tostring(runtime.SelectedStealAreaName or ""):lower()
    local values = {
        record.AreaId,
        record.Area,
        record.AreaName,
        record.AreaIdName,
        record.areaId,
        record.areaName,
    }
    local directory = App.getIndexAreaDirectory()
    local selectedConfig = type(directory) == "table" and directory[runtime.SelectedStealAreaId] or nil
    if not selectedConfig and type(directory) == "table" then
        for areaKey, areaConfig in pairs(directory) do
            if tostring(areaKey) == wantedId then
                selectedConfig = areaConfig
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if type(selectedConfig) == "table" then
        wantedName = tostring(selectedConfig.DisplayName or runtime.SelectedStealAreaName or ""):lower()
    end
    for _, value in ipairs(values) do
        local text = tostring(value or ""):lower()
        if text ~= "" and (text == wantedId or (wantedName ~= "" and text == wantedName)) then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

function App.getStealMethod()
