    local method = tostring(App.Runtime.StealMethod or "Tween TP")
    if method == "Walk" or method == "Tween TP" or method == "Fly + Tween" then
        return method
    end
    return "Tween TP"
end

function App.selectStealTarget(requireBig)
    local records, recordsError = App.getAreaEggRecords()
    if not records then
        return nil, recordsError
    end
    local _, _, root, rootError = App.getCharacterParts()
    if not root then
        return nil, rootError
    end
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then
        return nil, dataError
    end
    local currentSpeedPower = tonumber(data.SpeedPower) or 0
    local stealMethod = App.getStealMethod()
    local tweenTravel = stealMethod ~= "Walk"
    local selectedRarities = type(App.Runtime.SelectedStealRarities) == "table"
        and App.Runtime.SelectedStealRarities
        or {}
    local areaOnly = App.Runtime.StealAreaEnabled == true
        and App.Runtime.SelectedStealAreaId ~= nil
    local useAllRarities = areaOnly or next(selectedRarities) == nil
    local candidates = {}
    for _, record in ipairs(records) do
        if type(record) == "table"
            and type(record.Uid) == "string"
            and record.AssetCategory ~= "Chicken"
            and (
                record.State == "Slot"
                or (
                    record.State == "Dropped"
                    and record.CarrierUserId == nil
                )
            )
            and (requireBig ~= true or App.isAreaEggBig(record))
            and App.matchesSelectedStealArea(record)
        then
            local weight = App.getAreaEggWeight(record)
            local minWeight = App.Runtime.StealMinWeightKg
            local maxWeight = App.Runtime.StealMaxWeightKg
            local weightOk = (minWeight == nil or (weight and weight >= minWeight))
                and (maxWeight == nil or (weight and weight <= maxWeight))
            local selectedMutations = App.Runtime.SelectedStealMutations
            local mutationOk = next(selectedMutations) == nil
            for _, mutation in ipairs(App.getAreaEggMutations(record)) do
                if selectedMutations[mutation] == true
                    or selectedMutations["All Mutations"] == true
                then
                    mutationOk = true
                    break
                end
            end
            local rarity = App.canonicalRarity(
                record.Rarity
                    or record.RarityId
                    or App.getAssetRarity(record.AssetCategory)
            )
            local guard = App.getGuardModel(record.AreaId)
            local guardState = guard and guard:GetAttribute("GuardState")
            local positionCFrame = record.BottomCFrame or record.BoundsCFrame
            local requiredSpeed = guard
                and App.getGuardSpeedRequirement(record.AreaId, guard)
                or nil


            local canOutrunGuard = guard == nil
                or tweenTravel
                or (
                    type(requiredSpeed) == "number"
                    and currentSpeedPower >= requiredSpeed
                )
            if rarity
                and weightOk
                and mutationOk
                and (useAllRarities or selectedRarities[rarity] == true or (function()
                    for selectedName, selected in pairs(selectedRarities) do
                        if selected == true and App.canonicalRarity(selectedName) == rarity then return true end
                    end
                    return false
                end)())
                and (guard == nil or guardState == "Sleeping")
                and canOutrunGuard
                and typeof(positionCFrame) == "CFrame"
            then
                table.insert(candidates, {
                    Record = record,
                    Rarity = rarity,
                    Rank = App.getRarityRank(rarity),
                    Distance = (root.Position - positionCFrame.Position).Magnitude,
                    Position = positionCFrame.Position,
                    RequiredSpeed = requiredSpeed,
                })
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(candidates, function(left, right)
        if left.Rank ~= right.Rank then
            return left.Rank > right.Rank
        end
        if math.abs(left.Distance - right.Distance) > 0.01 then
            return left.Distance < right.Distance
        end
        return left.Record.Uid < right.Record.Uid
    end)
    return candidates[1]
end

function App.findAreaEggRecord(uid)
    local records, recordsError = App.getAreaEggRecords()
    if type(records) ~= "table" then
        return nil, recordsError or "Egg list is unavailable"
    end
    for _, record in ipairs(records) do
        if record.Uid == uid then
            return record, nil
        end
    end
    return nil, nil
end

function App.getStartAreaReturnCFrame()
    local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
    local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
    local startArea = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("StartArea")
    if not startArea then return nil end
    if startArea:IsA("BasePart") then return CFrame.new(startArea.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) end
    if startArea:IsA("Model") then
        local ok, pivot = pcall(function() return startArea:GetPivot() end)
        if ok and typeof(pivot) == "CFrame" then return pivot + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0) end
    end
    local part = startArea:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    return part and CFrame.new(part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) or nil
end
function App.getPlotReturnPosition()
    local plotCmds = App.Game.Modules.PlotCmds
    local ok, plotData = App.callModuleMethod(plotCmds, "GetPlotData")
    if not ok or type(plotData) ~= "table" then
        return nil, ok and "Your plot is unavailable" or plotData
    end
    if not plotData.PetArea or not plotData.PetArea:IsA("BasePart") then
        return nil, "Your plot PetArea is unavailable"
    end
    return plotData.PetArea.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
end

function App.getAreaEscapePosition(record)
    local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
    local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
    local separationLine = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("SeparationLine")
    local positionCFrame = record
        and (record.BottomCFrame or record.BoundsCFrame)
    if not separationLine
        or not separationLine:IsA("BasePart")
        or typeof(positionCFrame) ~= "CFrame"
    then
        return nil
    end
    local position = positionCFrame.Position
    local gameplayDirection = separationLine.CFrame.LookVector
    local signedDistance = (position - separationLine.Position):Dot(
        gameplayDirection
    )
    return position
        - gameplayDirection * (signedDistance + 12)
        + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
end

function App.isAreaEggCarriedByLocalPlayer(uid)
    if App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying
        and App.Runtime.CarryState.Uid == uid
    then
        return true
    end
    local record = App.findAreaEggRecord(uid)
    return record
        and record.State == "Carried"
        and record.CarrierUserId == App.LocalPlayer.UserId
        or false
end

function App.dropHeldAreaEgg()
    if not App.Game.Ready then
        return false
    end
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    if type(eggCmds.RequestDropHeldAreaEgg) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end
    local ok, dropped = App.callModuleMethod(
        eggCmds,
        "RequestDropHeldAreaEgg",
        "PlayerRequest"
    )
    return ok and dropped == true
end

function App.ensureAreaEggReleased(uid)
    for _ = 1, 3 do
        local record, recordError = App.findAreaEggRecord(uid)
        local runtimeCarrying = App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying
            and App.Runtime.CarryState.Uid == uid
        local snapshotCarrying = record
            and record.State == "Carried"
            and record.CarrierUserId == App.LocalPlayer.UserId
            or false
        if not recordError and not runtimeCarrying and not snapshotCarrying then
            return true
        end
        App.dropHeldAreaEgg()
        local released = false
        local deadline = os.clock() + 2
        repeat
            local current, currentError = App.findAreaEggRecord(uid)
            if not currentError then
                local stillRuntimeCarrying = App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying
                    and App.Runtime.CarryState.Uid == uid
                local stillSnapshotCarrying = current
                    and current.State == "Carried"
                    and current.CarrierUserId == App.LocalPlayer.UserId
                    or false
                if not stillRuntimeCarrying and not stillSnapshotCarrying then
                    released = true
                    break
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        until os.clock() >= deadline
        if released then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

function App.failStealAfterCarry(feature, uid, message)
    local errorText = tostring(message or "Steal trip failed")
    local released = App.ensureAreaEggReleased(uid)
    if released then
        App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = nil
        App.Runtime.StealRecoveryUid = nil
    else
        App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = "Recovery"
        App.Runtime.StealRecoveryUid = uid
    end
    App.reportFeatureError(
        feature,
        released
            and errorText
            or (errorText .. "; carried egg recovery is pending")
    )
    return App.Config.StealLoopDelay
end

local TweenToPoint
local EnsureGameplayFlag
local IsCarryingEgg
local FindEggPrompt
local HardStop

function App.stepAutoStealEgg(feature, version)
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    local rarityWeights = {
        Basic = 0,
        Common = 1,
        Uncommon = 2,
        Rare = 3,
        SuperRare = 3.5,
        Epic = 4,
        Legendary = 5,
        Mythic = 6,
        Mythical = 6,
        Cosmic = 7,
        Secret = 8,
        Eternal = 9,
        Divine = 10,
        Celestial = 11,
        Exotic = 12,
        Superior = 13,
        BrainrotGod = 14,
        Rainbow = 15,
        Prismatic = 15,
        Transcendent = 16,
        Exclusive = 17,
        Limited = 18,
        Admin = 19,
    }
    local activeMoveTweens = {}
    local recentlyClaimedByMe = {}
    local watchedMatchingUids = {}
    local hasSettledAtSafezoneWhileIdle = false
    local safezoneCF = App.getStartAreaReturnCFrame()

    local function stealCancelled()
        return not App.featureAlive(feature, version)
    end

    local function getTrapOnPath(startPosition, targetPosition)
        if not App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoAvoidTrap then
            return nil
        end
        for _, trapPosition in ipairs(App.getActiveTrapPositions()) do
            if App.pointToSegmentDistance(trapPosition, startPosition, targetPosition) <= App.Config.TrapAvoidRadius then
                return trapPosition
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function getSafeTrapPoint(startPosition, trapPosition)
        local away = Vector3.new(startPosition.X - trapPosition.X, 0, startPosition.Z - trapPosition.Z)
        if away.Magnitude < 0.1 then
            away = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
        else
            away = away.Unit
        end
        local distance = App.Config.TrapAvoidRadius + 14
        return Vector3.new(
            trapPosition.X + away.X * distance,
            startPosition.Y,
            trapPosition.Z + away.Z * distance
        )
    end

    local function TweenMoveTo(hrp, hum, targetCFrame, isCancelledFn, speedMultiplierOverride)
        if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or not hum or not hum.Parent then
            return false
        end
        if isCancelledFn and isCancelledFn() then
            return false
        end
        local previousTween = activeMoveTweens[hrp]
        if previousTween then
            pcall(function()
                previousTween:Cancel()
            end)
            activeMoveTweens[hrp] = nil
        end
        while hrp.Parent and hum.Parent and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
            if isCancelledFn and isCancelledFn() then
                return false
            end
            local trapPosition = getTrapOnPath(hrp.Position, targetCFrame.Position)
            if trapPosition then
                local safePoint = App.getSafeTrapPoint(hrp.Position, trapPosition)
                local routeOk = App.moveHumanoidTo(
                    "AutoStealEgg",
                    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoStealEgg,
                    safePoint,
                    true,
                    isCancelledFn
                )
                if isCancelledFn and isCancelledFn() then
                    return false
                end
                if not routeOk then
                    task.wait(0.15)
                end
                while App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoAvoidTrap and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
                    if isCancelledFn and isCancelledFn() then
                        return false
                    end
                    if not getTrapOnPath(hrp.Position, targetCFrame.Position) then
                        break
                    end
                    task.wait(0.2)
                end
            end
            local distance = (targetCFrame.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
            if distance < 0.1 then
                hrp.CFrame = targetCFrame
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                return true
            end
            local speed = hum.WalkSpeed
            if not speed or speed <= 0 then
                speed = 16
            end
            local travelConfig = App.Runtime.StealTravel or {}
            local multiplier = tonumber(speedMultiplierOverride)
                or tonumber(travelConfig.TweenSpeedMultiplier)
                or tonumber(App.Config.TweenSpeedMultiplier)
                or 2
            if multiplier <= 0 then
                multiplier = 2
            end
            local duration = math.max(distance / (speed * multiplier), 0.15)
            local tween = App.Services.TweenService:Create(
                hrp,
                TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
                { CFrame = targetCFrame }
            )
            activeMoveTweens[hrp] = tween
            tween:Play()
            local tweenStartedAt = os.clock()
            local tweenTimeout = duration + math.max(1, tonumber(App.Config.TweenHopTimeout) or 4)
            local interruptedByTrap = false
            while tween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing do
                if isCancelledFn and isCancelledFn() then
                    tween:Cancel()
                    break
                end
                if os.clock() - tweenStartedAt > tweenTimeout then
                    tween:Cancel()
                    break
                end
                if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoAvoidTrap then
                    local activeTrap = getTrapOnPath(hrp.Position, targetCFrame.Position)
                    if activeTrap then
                        interruptedByTrap = true
                        tween:Cancel()
                        break
                    end
                end
                App.Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            if activeMoveTweens[hrp] == tween then
                activeMoveTweens[hrp] = nil
            end
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            if isCancelledFn and isCancelledFn() then
                return false
            end
            if not interruptedByTrap then
                if tween.PlaybackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
                    return false
                end
                if (targetCFrame.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > 2 then
                    pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = targetCFrame end)
                end
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function moveStealPoint(targetCFrame, speedMultiplierOverride, currentHrp, currentHum)
        local method = App.getStealMethod()
        if method == "Walk" then
            local moved = App.moveHumanoidTo(
                "AutoStealEgg",
                App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoStealEgg,
                targetCFrame.Position,
                true,
                stealCancelled
            )
            return moved == true
        end
        return TweenMoveTo(
            currentHrp,
            currentHum,
            targetCFrame,
            stealCancelled,
            speedMultiplierOverride
        )
    end
    local function settleNearEgg(targetPosition, timeout)
        local deadline = os.clock() + (timeout or 4)
        while not stealCancelled() and os.clock() < deadline do
            local currentCharacter, currentHumanoid, currentRoot = App.getCharacterParts()
            if not currentCharacter or not currentHumanoid or not currentRoot then return false end
            local flatOffset = Vector3.new(targetPosition.X - currentRoot.Position.X, 0, targetPosition.Z - currentRoot.Position.Z)
            local distance = (currentRoot.Position - targetPosition).Magnitude
            if distance <= 7 or flatOffset.Magnitude <= 6 then
                pcall(function()
                    currentHumanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                    currentHumanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
                    currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    currentRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)
                return true
            end
            pcall(function()
                currentHumanoid.PlatformStand = false
                currentHumanoid.Sit = false
                currentHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                currentHumanoid:MoveTo(targetPosition)
            end)
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        return false
    end
    local function verifyCarriedUid(uid, timeout)
        local deadline = os.clock() + (timeout or 1.5)
        repeat
            if App.isAreaEggCarriedByLocalPlayer(uid) then return true end
            task.wait(0.05)
        until stealCancelled() or os.clock() >= deadline
        return App.isAreaEggCarriedByLocalPlayer(uid)
    end

    local function getRarity(record)
        if type(record) ~= "table" then return "Unknown" end
        local rarity = record.Rarity
            or record.RarityId
            or record.RarityName
            or record.RarityType
            or record.AssetRarity
            or App.getAssetRarity(record.AssetCategory)
        if type(rarity) == "table" then
            rarity = rarity._id or rarity.Id or rarity.Name or rarity.DisplayName
        end
        return tostring(rarity or "Unknown")
    end

    local function matchesRarity(rarity)
        if App.Runtime.StealAreaEnabled == true and App.Runtime.SelectedStealAreaId ~= nil then
            return true
        end
        local selected = type(App.Runtime.SelectedStealRarities) == "table"
            and App.Runtime.SelectedStealRarities
            or {}
        if next(selected) == nil then return true end
        local canonical = App.canonicalRarity(rarity)
        if selected[canonical] == true then return true end
        for selectedName, enabled in pairs(selected) do
            if enabled == true and App.canonicalRarity(selectedName) == canonical then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function matchesArea(record)
        return App.matchesSelectedStealArea(record)
    end

    while App.featureAlive(feature, version) do
        local nowClock = os.clock()
        for uid, claimedAt in pairs(recentlyClaimedByMe) do
            if nowClock - claimedAt > 5 then
                recentlyClaimedByMe[uid] = nil
            end
        end

        local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        safezoneCF = App.getStartAreaReturnCFrame() or safezoneCF
        if character and hrp and hum then
            local snapshotOk, eggSnapshot
            local snapshotNow = os.clock()
            if not App.Runtime.AutoStealSnapshot
                or snapshotNow - (App.Runtime.AutoStealSnapshotAt or 0) >= (tonumber(App.Config.SafeSnapshotInterval) or 0.35)
            then
                snapshotOk, eggSnapshot = App.callModuleMethod(
                    eggCmds,
                    "RequestAreaEggSnapshot"
                )
                if not snapshotOk or type(eggSnapshot) ~= "table" or type(eggSnapshot.Records) ~= "table" then
                    snapshotOk, eggSnapshot = App.callModuleMethod(
                        eggCmds,
                        "GetAreaEggSnapshot"
                    )
                end
                if snapshotOk and type(eggSnapshot) == "table" and type(eggSnapshot.Records) == "table" then
                    App.Runtime.AutoStealSnapshot = eggSnapshot
                    App.Runtime.AutoStealSnapshotAt = snapshotNow
                end
            else
                snapshotOk = true
                eggSnapshot = App.Runtime.AutoStealSnapshot
            end
            local targetEgg
            local currentMatchingUids = {}
            if snapshotOk and type(eggSnapshot) == "table" and type(eggSnapshot.Records) == "table" then
                for _, record in ipairs(eggSnapshot.Records) do
                    if record.State == "Carried"
                        and record.CarrierUserId == App.LocalPlayer.UserId
                    then
                        targetEgg = record
                        targetEgg.Rarity = getRarity(record)
                        break
                    end
                end
                if not targetEgg then
                    local pendingUid = App.Runtime.AutoRecarryDroppedEgg
                        and App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.Uid
                    if pendingUid then
                        for recordKey, record in ipairs(eggSnapshot.Records) do
                            local recordUid = record.Uid or recordKey
                            if tostring(recordUid) == tostring(pendingUid)
                                and (record.State == "Slot" or record.State == "Dropped")
                                and record.BottomCFrame
                                and record.AssetCategory ~= "Chicken"
                            then
                                targetEgg = record
                                targetEgg.Uid = recordUid
                                targetEgg.Rarity = getRarity(record)
                                App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.LastAttemptAt = os.clock()
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if not targetEgg then
                    for recordKey, record in ipairs(eggSnapshot.Records) do
                        local recordUid = record.Uid or recordKey
                        local rarity = getRarity(record)
                        if not recentlyClaimedByMe[recordUid]
                            and (record.State == "Slot" or record.State == "Dropped")
                            and record.BottomCFrame
                            and record.AssetCategory ~= "Chicken"
                            and matchesRarity(rarity)
                            and matchesArea(record)
                        then
                            currentMatchingUids[recordUid] = true
                            targetEgg = record
                            targetEgg.Uid = recordUid
                            targetEgg.Rarity = rarity
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            watchedMatchingUids = currentMatchingUids

            if targetEgg and not safezoneCF then
                App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "StartArea is unavailable")
                targetEgg = nil
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
            if targetEgg then
                hasSettledAtSafezoneWhileIdle = false
                App.notify(
                    "Auto Steal",
                    "Starting " .. tostring(targetEgg.Rarity) .. " " .. tostring(targetEgg.AssetCategory),
                    1.5
                )
                App.Runtime.IsStealing = true
                App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = "Outbound"
                App.setFeatureDescription(
                    feature,
                    "Tweening to " .. tostring(targetEgg.Rarity) .. " " .. tostring(targetEgg.AssetCategory)
                )
                local success, err = pcall(function()
                    hum:UnequipTools()
                    if App.isGameplayAreaBlocked() then
                        while App.isGameplayAreaBlocked() and not stealCancelled() do
                            task.wait(0.2)
                        end
                        if stealCancelled() then
                            return
                        end
                    end

                    local eggCF = targetEgg.BottomCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
                    local identity = App.Game.Modules.AreaEggSlotIdentity
                    local firstAreaSlotKey
                    local isFirstOk, isFirst = App.callModuleMethod(
                        identity,
                        "IsFirstAreaUid",
                        tostring(targetEgg.Uid)
                    )
                    if isFirstOk and isFirst == true then
                        local keyOk, slotKey = App.callModuleMethod(
                            identity,
                            "BuildSlotKey",
                            targetEgg.AreaId,
                            targetEgg.NestId
                        )
                        if keyOk then
                            firstAreaSlotKey = slotKey
                        end
                    end

                    local outerAttempt = 0
                    local maxOuterAttempts = 4
                    local finalOutcome
                    while not finalOutcome and not stealCancelled() do
                        outerAttempt = outerAttempt + 1
                        if outerAttempt > maxOuterAttempts then
                            finalOutcome = "gaveup"
                            break
                        end
                        local carryOk
                        local carryError
                        local retryOuter = false
                        local eggStillThere = true
                        local alreadyCarryingTarget = targetEgg.State == "Carried"
                            and targetEgg.CarrierUserId == App.LocalPlayer.UserId
                        if alreadyCarryingTarget then
                            carryOk = true
                        else
                            local pathClear = true
                            pcall(function()
                                local from = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                                local to = eggCF.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                                local excludeList = { character }
                                local params = RaycastParams.new()
                                params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                                local origin = from
                                for _ = 1, 8 do
                                    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = excludeList
                                    local direction = to - origin
                                    if direction.Magnitude < 1 then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    local result = App.Services.Workspace:Raycast(origin, direction, params)
                                    if not result then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    if result.Instance.CanCollide then
                                        pathClear = false
                                        break
                                    end
                                    table.insert(excludeList, result.Instance)
                                    origin = result.Position + direction.Unit * 0.1
                                end
                            end)
                            if not pathClear then
                                local safeMoveOk = App.moveHumanoidTo(
                                    "AutoStealEgg",
                                    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoStealEgg,
                                    safezoneCF.Position,
                                    true,
                                    stealCancelled
                                )
                                if not safeMoveOk or stealCancelled() then
                                    finalOutcome = stealCancelled() and "cancelled" or "gaveup"
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                            local movedToEgg, movedToEggError = App.moveHumanoidTo(
                                "AutoStealEgg",
                                App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoStealEgg,
                                eggCF.Position,
                                true,
                                stealCancelled
                            )
                            if not movedToEgg or stealCancelled() then
                                finalOutcome = stealCancelled() and "cancelled" or (movedToEggError or "trusted approach failed")
                                break
                            end
                            if not settleNearEgg(eggCF.Position, 5) then
                                finalOutcome = stealCancelled() and "cancelled" or "gaveup"
                                break
                            end
                            local loopConn = nil
                            task.wait(0.35)
                            if stealCancelled() then
                                finalOutcome = "cancelled"
                                break
                            end
                            local round = 0
                            local maxRounds = 8
                            local nextPromptAttemptAt = 0
                            repeat
                                round = round + 1
                                for _ = 1, 10 do
                                    if stealCancelled() then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    local requestInvokeOk, requestAccepted, requestMessage = App.callModuleMethod(
                                        eggCmds,
                                        "RequestCarryAreaEgg",
                                        targetEgg.Uid,
                                        firstAreaSlotKey
                                    )
                                    carryOk = requestInvokeOk and requestAccepted == true
                                    carryError = requestMessage
                                    if carryOk then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    task.wait(0.03)
                                end
                                if stealCancelled() then
                                    break
                                end
                                if not carryOk and eggStillThere and os.clock() >= nextPromptAttemptAt then
                                    nextPromptAttemptAt = os.clock() + 0.35
                                    carryOk, carryError = App.tryLinkedCarryFallback(
                                        eggCmds,
                                        targetEgg,
                                        firstAreaSlotKey,
                                        hrp.Position
                                    )
                                end
                                if not carryOk then
                                    local recordOk, freshRecord = App.callModuleMethod(
                                        eggCmds,
                                        "GetAreaEggRecord",
                                        targetEgg.Uid
                                    )
                                    eggStillThere = recordOk
                                        and freshRecord ~= nil
                                        and (freshRecord.State == "Slot" or freshRecord.State == "Dropped")
                                    if eggStillThere and freshRecord.BottomCFrame then
                                        eggCF = freshRecord.BottomCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
                                    end
                                    if eggStillThere and round < maxRounds then
                                        local recarryMoveOk = App.moveHumanoidTo(
                                            "AutoStealEgg",
                                            App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoStealEgg,
                                            eggCF.Position,
                                            true,
                                            stealCancelled
                                        )
                                        if not recarryMoveOk or stealCancelled() then
                                            break
                                        end
                                        settleNearEgg(eggCF.Position, 4)
                                        task.wait(0.15)
                                    end
                                end
                            until carryOk or not eggStillThere or round >= maxRounds
                            if loopConn then loopConn:Disconnect() end
                        end

                        if stealCancelled() then
                            finalOutcome = "cancelled"
                            break
                        end
                        if carryOk and not verifyCarriedUid(targetEgg.Uid, 1.5) then
                            carryOk = false
                            carryError = "Carry was not confirmed for the selected UID"
                        end
                        if not carryOk then
                            if eggStillThere and outerAttempt < maxOuterAttempts then
                                retryOuter = true
                                task.wait(0.2)
                            else
                                finalOutcome = eggStillThere and "gaveup" or "lost"
                            end
                        end

                        if not retryOuter and not finalOutcome then
                            recentlyClaimedByMe[targetEgg.Uid] = os.clock()
                        App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = "Returning"
                        local droppedAgain = false
                        local confirmedCarrying = false
                        local lastDropCheckAt = 0
                        local function abortTripIfDropped()
                            if stealCancelled() then
                                return true
                            end
                            local checkNow = os.clock()
                            if checkNow - lastDropCheckAt < 0.15 then
                                return false
                            end
                            lastDropCheckAt = checkNow
                            local recordOk, record = App.callModuleMethod(
                                eggCmds,
                                "GetAreaEggRecord",
                                targetEgg.Uid
                            )
                            if not recordOk or record == nil then
                                return false
                            end
                            if record.State == "Carried"
                                and record.CarrierUserId == App.LocalPlayer.UserId
                            then
                                confirmedCarrying = true
                                return false
                            end
                            if not confirmedCarrying then
                                return false
                            end
                            droppedAgain = true
                            if App.Runtime.AutoRecarryDroppedEgg then
                                local recarry = App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState
                                recarry.Uid = targetEgg.Uid
                                recarry.AreaId = targetEgg.AreaId
                                recarry.NestId = targetEgg.NestId
                                recarry.AssetCategory = targetEgg.AssetCategory
                                recarry.LastAttemptAt = 0
                                recarry.LastSeenCarried = false
                            end
                            if record.BottomCFrame then
                                eggCF = record.BottomCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
                            end
                            return true
                        end
                        local function TweenStealInSky()
                            local currentPosition = hrp.Position
                            local safezonePosition = safezoneCF.Position
                            local skyY = math.max(currentPosition.Y + 160, safezonePosition.Y + 160)
                            local currentRotation = hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position
                            local safezoneRotation = safezoneCF - safezoneCF.Position
                            local skyStart = CFrame.new(currentPosition.X, skyY, currentPosition.Z) * currentRotation
                            local skyDestination = CFrame.new(safezonePosition.X, skyY, safezonePosition.Z) * safezoneRotation
                            local ascentMultiplier = math.max(
                                tonumber(App.Runtime.SkyAscentSpeedMultiplier) or 8,
                                tonumber(App.Runtime.TweenSpeedMultiplier) or 2
                            )
                            local upOk = TweenMoveTo(
                                hrp,
                                hum,
                                skyStart,
                                abortTripIfDropped,
                                ascentMultiplier
                            )
                            if not upOk or abortTripIfDropped() then
                                return false
                            end
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            local acrossOk = TweenMoveTo(hrp, hum, skyDestination, abortTripIfDropped)
                            if not acrossOk or abortTripIfDropped() then
                                return false
                            end
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            return TweenMoveTo(hrp, hum, safezoneCF, abortTripIfDropped)
                        end
                        local returnOk
                        if App.getStealMethod() == "Fly + Tween" then
                            returnOk = TweenStealInSky()
                        elseif App.getStealMethod() == "Walk" then
                            returnOk = App.moveHumanoidTo(
                                "AutoStealEgg",
                                App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoStealEgg,
                                safezoneCF.Position,
                                true,
                                abortTripIfDropped
                            )
                        else
                            returnOk = TweenMoveTo(hrp, hum, safezoneCF, abortTripIfDropped)
                        end
                        if not returnOk then
                            if stealCancelled() then
                                finalOutcome = "cancelled"
                            elseif droppedAgain then
                                finalOutcome = "lost"
                            end
                            break
                        end
                        if stealCancelled() then
                            finalOutcome = "cancelled"
                            break
                        end
                        if not droppedAgain then
                            local safezoneConn = App.Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                                if hrp.Parent then
                                    hrp.CFrame = safezoneCF
                                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                end
                            end)
                            local claimWaited = 0
                            local maxClaimWait = 5
                            repeat
                                task.wait(0.03)
                                claimWaited = claimWaited + 0.03
                                if claimWaited >= 0.06 then
                                    local recordOk, record = App.callModuleMethod(
                                        eggCmds,
                                        "GetAreaEggRecord",
                                        targetEgg.Uid
                                    )
                                    if recordOk and record == nil then
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            until claimWaited >= maxClaimWait or stealCancelled()
                            safezoneConn:Disconnect()
                            if stealCancelled() then
                                finalOutcome = "cancelled"
                                break
                            end
                            if claimWaited >= maxClaimWait then
                                local recordOk, record = App.callModuleMethod(
                                    eggCmds,
                                    "GetAreaEggRecord",
                                    targetEgg.Uid
                                )
                                if not recordOk or record == nil then
                                    finalOutcome = "claimed"
                                elseif record.State ~= "Carried"
                                    or record.CarrierUserId ~= App.LocalPlayer.UserId
                                then
                                    if record.BottomCFrame then
                                        eggCF = record.BottomCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
                                    end
                                else
                                    finalOutcome = "claimed"
                                end
                            else
                                finalOutcome = "claimed"
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if finalOutcome == "claimed" then
                        if App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.Uid
                            and tostring(App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.Uid) == tostring(targetEgg.Uid)
                        then
                            App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.Uid = nil
                            App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.AreaId = nil
                            App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.NestId = nil
                            App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.AssetCategory = nil
                        end
                        App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = nil
                        App.Runtime.StealRecoveryUid = nil
                        App.Runtime.Stats.EggsStolen = (tonumber(App.Runtime.Stats.EggsStolen) or 0) + 1
                        App.setFeatureDescription(
                            feature,
                            "Stole " .. tostring(targetEgg.Rarity) .. " "
                                .. tostring(targetEgg.AssetCategory) .. " ("
                                .. tostring(App.Runtime.Stats.EggsStolen) .. ")"
                        )
                    elseif finalOutcome == "lost" then
                        if App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.Uid
                            and tostring(App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.Uid) == tostring(targetEgg.Uid)
                        then
                            App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.Uid = nil
                            App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.AreaId = nil
                            App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.NestId = nil
                            App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.AssetCategory = nil
                        end
                        App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = nil
                        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Egg was taken by another player")
                    elseif finalOutcome == "gaveup" then
                        App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = nil
                        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Retrying the egg")
                    end
                        end
                end)
                if not success and err then
                    App.reportFeatureError(feature, err)
                end
                App.Runtime.IsStealing = false
                App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = nil
                task.wait(0.05)
            else
                if not safezoneCF then
                    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "StartArea is unavailable")
                    task.wait(0.5)
                else
                    App.callModuleMethod(eggCmds, "RequestAreaEggSnapshot")
                    if not hasSettledAtSafezoneWhileIdle then
                        if (hrp.Position - safezoneCF.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                            moveStealPoint(safezoneCF, nil, hrp, hum)
                        end
                        hasSettledAtSafezoneWhileIdle = true
                    end
                    task.wait(0.3)
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    for hrp, tween in pairs(activeMoveTweens) do
        pcall(function()
            tween:Cancel()
        end)
        activeMoveTweens[hrp] = nil
    end
        App.Runtime.IsStealing = false
    App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = nil
    return 0
end
function App.removeEspEntry(id)
    local entry = App.Runtime.EspEntries[id]
    if not entry then
        return
    end
    if entry.Gui then
        pcall(function() entry.Gui:Destroy() end)
    end
    if entry.Anchor then
        pcall(function() entry.Anchor:Destroy() end)
    end
    App.Runtime.EspEntries[id] = nil
end

function App.getEspAnchor(target, position)
    if target and target:IsA("BasePart") then
        return target
    end
    if target and target:IsA("Attachment") then
        return target.Parent and target.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and target.Parent or nil
    end
    if target and target:IsA("Model") then
        return target.PrimaryPart or target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end
    if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
        return nil
    end
    local anchor = Instance.new("Part")
    anchor.Name = "StealAnEggESPAnchor"
    anchor.Anchored = true
    anchor.CanCollide = false
    anchor.CanTouch = false
    anchor.CanQuery = false
    anchor.Transparency = 1
    anchor.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
    anchor.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
    anchor.Parent = App.Services.Workspace
    return anchor
end

function App.setEspLabel(id, target, position, text, colour, seen, playerGui)
    local entry = App.Runtime.EspEntries[id]
    local anchor = entry and entry.Anchor or App.getEspAnchor(target, position)
    if not anchor then
        return
    end
    seen[id] = true
    if not entry then
        local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        gui.Name = "StealAnEggESP"
        gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
        gui.LightInfluence = 0
        gui.MaxDistance = 10000
        gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 44)
        gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
        gui.Adornee = anchor
        gui.Parent = playerGui
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
        label.TextColor3 = colour or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        label.TextSize = 14
        label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        label.TextWrapped = true
        label.Parent = gui
        entry = {Gui = gui, Label = label, Anchor = target and nil or anchor}
        App.Runtime.EspEntries[id] = entry
    end
    if entry.Anchor then
        entry.Anchor.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
    end
    entry.Gui.Adornee = anchor
    entry.Label.Text = text
    entry.Label.TextColor3 = colour or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
end

function App.getEspWorldContainers(names)
    local containers = {}
    local seen = {}
    local wanted = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(names) do
        wanted[name] = true
    end
    local function add(container)
        if container and not seen[container] then
            seen[container] = true
            table.insert(containers, container)
        end
    end
    local workspace = App.Services.Workspace
    for _, root in ipairs({
        workspace,
        workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS"),
        workspace:FindFirstChild("__THINGS"),
    }) do
        if root then
            for name in pairs(wanted) do
                add(root:FindFirstChild(name))
                add(root:FindFirstChild(name, true))
            end
        end
    end
    return containers
end

function App.getEspModelName(model, fallback)
    if not model then
        return fallback or "Unknown"
    end
    for _, name in ipairs({"DisplayName", "PetName", "AssetName", "Name", "Id", "PetId"}) do
        local value = model:GetAttribute(name)
        if value ~= nil and tostring(value) ~= "" then
            return tostring(value)
        end
    end
    return model.Name ~= "" and model.Name or (fallback or "Unknown")
end

function App.getEspMachineModels()
    local now = os.clock()
    if type(App.Runtime.EspMachineCache) == "table"
        and now < (App.Runtime.EspMachineCacheUntil or 0)
    then
        return App.Runtime.EspMachineCache
    end
    local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
    local machines = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Machines")
    local models = {}
    if machines then
        for _, descendant in ipairs(machines:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("Model")
                and descendant:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
            then
                table.insert(models, descendant)
            end
        end
    end
    App.Runtime.EspMachineCache = models
    App.Runtime.EspMachineCacheUntil = now + 2
    return models
end

function App.espDistance(position, localPosition)
    if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(localPosition) ~= "Vector3" then
        return "?"
    end
    return tostring(math.floor((position - localPosition).Magnitude)) .. " studs"
end

function App.updateEsp(feature)
    if not App.Runtime.Enabled.ESP then return end
    if os.clock() < (App.Runtime.EspNextUpdateAt or 0) then return end
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    App.Runtime.EspNextUpdateAt = nowClock + 0.35
    local modes = App.Runtime.EspModes
    local playerGui = App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not playerGui then return end
    local localRoot = App.LocalPlayer.Character and App.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local localPosition = localRoot and localRoot.Position
    if not localPosition then return end
    local seen = {}
    local function add(id, target, position, text, colour)
        App.setEspLabel(id, target, position, text, colour, seen, playerGui)
    end
    if modes.Players then
        for _, player in ipairs(App.Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= App.LocalPlayer then
                local character = player.Character
                local root = character and (
                    character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    or character.PrimaryPart
                    or character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                )
                if root then
                    local displayName = player.DisplayName ~= "" and player.DisplayName or player.Name
                    add("player:" .. tostring(player.UserId), root, root.Position, displayName .. "\n" .. App.espDistance(root.Position, localPosition), Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if modes.Plots then
        local plots = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") or App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("BasePlots")
        if plots then
            for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                local part = App.getEspAnchor(plot)
                if part then
                    local owner = plot:GetAttribute("OwnerName") or plot:GetAttribute("Owner") or plot.Name
                    add("plot:" .. plot:GetFullName(), part, part.Position, tostring(owner) .. "\n" .. App.espDistance(part.Position, localPosition), Color3.new(0, 1, 0))
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if modes.WorldEggs then
        local records
        if nowClock >= (App.Runtime.EspEggRecordsNextRefreshAt or 0) then
            local freshRecords = App.getAreaEggRecords()
            App.Runtime.EspEggRecordsCache = type(freshRecords) == "table" and freshRecords or {}
            App.Runtime.EspEggRecordsNextRefreshAt = nowClock + 0.75
        end
        records = App.Runtime.EspEggRecordsCache
        if type(records) == "table" then
            for _, record in ipairs(records) do
                local cf = record.BottomCFrame or record.BoundsCFrame
                if typeof(cf) == "CFrame" then
                    local rarity = App.getAssetRarity(record.AssetCategory) or "Egg"
                    add("egg:" .. tostring(record.Uid), nil, cf.Position, rarity .. "\n" .. App.espDistance(cf.Position, localPosition), App.getRarityColour(rarity) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if modes.Machines then
        for _, machine in ipairs(App.getEspMachineModels()) do
            local part = App.getEspAnchor(machine)
            if part then
                add("machine:" .. machine:GetFullName(), part, part.Position, App.getEspModelName(machine, "Machine") .. "\n" .. App.espDistance(part.Position, localPosition), Color3.new(0, 1, 1))
            end
        end
    end
    if modes.Guards then
        local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
        local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
        local guards = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
        if guards then
            for _, area in ipairs(guards:GetChildren()) do
                local guard = area:FindFirstChild("Guard", true)
                local part = App.getEspAnchor(guard or area)
                if part then
                    add("guard:" .. area:GetFullName(), part, part.Position, "Guard\n" .. App.espDistance(part.Position, localPosition), Color3.new(1, 0, 0))
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if modes.CarriedEggs then
        for _, player in ipairs(App.Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= App.LocalPlayer then
                local character = player.Character
                local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root and character:FindFirstChild("CarriedEgg") then
                    add("carried:" .. tostring(player.UserId), root, root.Position, "Carried Egg\n" .. App.espDistance(root.Position, localPosition), Color3.new(1, 0.5, 0))
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for id in pairs(App.Runtime.EspEntries) do
        if not seen[id] then App.removeEspEntry(id) end
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "ESP active")
end

function App.stopEsp()
    local feature = "ESP"
    App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] = false
    App.Runtime.PendingStarts[feature] = nil
    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] = (App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] or 0) + 1
    local connection = App.Runtime.EspConnection
    App.Runtime.EspConnection = nil
    if connection then pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
    local thread = App.Runtime.EspThread
    App.Runtime.EspThread = nil
    if type(thread) == "thread" and task and type(task.cancel) == "function" then
        pcall(task.cancel, thread)
    end
    for id in pairs(App.Runtime.EspEntries) do App.removeEspEntry(id) end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Disabled")
    return true
end

function App.startEsp()
    local feature = "ESP"
    App.stopEsp()
    App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] = true
    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] = (App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] or 0) + 1
    local version = App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature]
    local thread
    thread = task.spawn(function()
        while App.featureAlive(feature, version) do
            pcall(App.updateEsp, feature)
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
        if App.Runtime.EspThread == thread then
            App.Runtime.EspThread = nil
        end
    end)
    App.Runtime.EspThread = thread
    App.updateEsp(feature)
    return thread
end

function App.pulseAntiAfk(feature)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local cameraCFrame = camera and camera.CFrame or CFrame.new()
    local pulseOk = pcall(function()
        App.Services.VirtualUser:CaptureController()
        App.Services.VirtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.zero, cameraCFrame)
    end)
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local mode = App.Runtime.AntiAfkMode == "Jump" and "Jump" or "Walk"
    local actionOk = pcall(function()
        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end
        if mode == "Jump" then
            humanoid.Jump = true
        else
            humanoid:Move(Vector3.new(1, 0, 0), false)
            task.delay(0.2, function()
                if humanoid and humanoid.Parent and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                    pcall(function()
                        humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    if not pulseOk and not actionOk then
        return false
    end

    App.Runtime.Stats.AntiAfkPulses = (tonumber(App.Runtime.Stats.AntiAfkPulses) or 0) + 1
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Session protected: " .. mode)
    return true
end

function App.stopAntiAfk()
    local feature = "AntiAFK"
    App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] = false
    App.Runtime.PendingStarts[feature] = nil
    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] = (App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] or 0) + 1

    local connection = App.Runtime.AntiAfkConnection
    App.Runtime.AntiAfkConnection = nil
    if connection then
        pcall(function()
            connection:Disconnect()
        end)
    end

    local thread = App.Runtime.Threads[feature]
    App.Runtime.Threads[feature] = nil
    if type(thread) == "thread"
        and task
        and type(task.cancel) == "function"
    then
        pcall(task.cancel, thread)
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Disabled")
    return true
end

function App.startAntiAfk()
    local feature = "AntiAFK"
    local previousConnection = App.Runtime.AntiAfkConnection
    App.Runtime.AntiAfkConnection = nil
    if previousConnection then
        pcall(function()
            previousConnection:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    local previousThread = App.Runtime.Threads[feature]
    if type(previousThread) == "thread"
        and task
        and type(task.cancel) == "function"
    then
        pcall(task.cancel, previousThread)
    end

    App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] = true
    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] = (App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] or 0) + 1
    local version = App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature]
    if not App.pulseAntiAfk(feature) then
        App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] = false
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Session protection is unavailable")
        return false
    end

    App.Runtime.AntiAfkConnection = App.connectGuarded(
        App.LocalPlayer.Idled,
        function()
            if App.featureAlive(feature, version)
                and not App.pulseAntiAfk(feature)
            then
                App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Session protection was interrupted")
            end
        end
    )

    local thread
    thread = task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            while App.featureAlive(feature, version) do
                local interval = math.clamp(
                    tonumber(App.Runtime.AntiAfkInterval) or App.Config.AntiAfkPulseDelay,
                    1,
                    3600
                )
                task.wait(interval)
                if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
                    break
                end
                if not App.pulseAntiAfk(feature) then
                    App.reportFeatureError(
                        feature,
                        "Session protection was interrupted"
                    )
                end
            end
        end)
        if App.Runtime.Threads[feature] == thread then
            App.Runtime.Threads[feature] = nil
        end
    end)
    App.Runtime.Threads[feature] = thread
    return thread
end

function App.invokeRawNetwork(remotePath, ...)
    local networkFolder = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
    if not networkFolder then
        return false, "Network is unavailable"
    end
    local current = networkFolder
    for segment in string.gmatch(remotePath, "[^%.]+") do
        current = current and current:FindFirstChild(segment)
    end
    if not current then
        return false, "Remote is unavailable: " .. tostring(remotePath)
    end
    local args = table.pack(...)
    if current:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
        local ok, result = pcall(current.InvokeServer, current, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
        return ok, result
    end
    if current:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        local ok, result = pcall(current.FireServer, current, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
        return ok, result
    end
    return false, "Remote is not invokable: " .. tostring(remotePath)
end

function App.claimIndexRewards(feature)
    local ok, result = App.invokeNetwork("Index: RequestClaimAll")
    if not ok then
        ok, result = App.invokeRawNetwork("Index.RequestClaimAll")
    end
    if not ok then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, result or "Index rewards are unavailable")
        return false
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Index rewards claimed")
    return true
end

function App.claimOfflineCash(feature)
    local constants = App.Game.Constants
    local networkMap = type(constants) == "table" and constants.NETWORK_MAP or nil
    local offlineMap = type(networkMap) == "table" and networkMap.OfflineAssets or nil
    local endpoint = type(offlineMap) == "table" and offlineMap.REQUEST_REDEEM or nil
    local network = App.Game.Modules.Network
    if type(endpoint) ~= "string" or endpoint == "" then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Offline redeem request is unavailable")
        return false
    end
    if type(network) ~= "table" or type(network.Fire) ~= "function" then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Network fire is unavailable")
        return false
    end
    local ok, result = pcall(network.Fire, endpoint)
    if not ok then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, result or "Offline redeem request failed")
        return false
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Offline cash redemption requested")
    return true
end

function App.stepAutoClaimIndex(feature, version)
    App.claimIndexRewards(feature)
    return App.Config.ClaimLoopDelay
end

function App.stepAutoClaimOfflineCash(feature, version)
    App.claimOfflineCash(feature)
    return 90
end

function App.getDiscoveredEndpoint(kind)
    local constants = App.Game.Constants
    local map = type(constants) == "table" and constants.NETWORK_MAP or nil
    if type(map) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local wanted = string.lower(tostring(kind))
    for groupName, group in pairs(map) do
        if type(group) == "table" then
            local groupText = string.lower(tostring(groupName))
            for key, endpoint in pairs(group) do
                if type(endpoint) == "string" then
                    local text = groupText .. " " .. string.lower(tostring(key)) .. " " .. string.lower(endpoint)
                    if string.find(text, wanted, 1, true) then
                        return endpoint
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function App.invokeDiscoveredEndpoint(endpoint, ...)
    if type(endpoint) ~= "string" or endpoint == "" then
        return false, "Endpoint unavailable"
    end
    if string.find(endpoint, ":", 1, true) then
        return App.invokeNetwork(endpoint, ...)
    end
    return App.invokeRawNetwork(endpoint, ...)
end

function App.getSpinWheelEndpoint()
    if App.Runtime.SpinWheelEndpoint then
        return App.Runtime.SpinWheelEndpoint
    end
    local endpoint = App.getDiscoveredEndpoint("spin") or App.getDiscoveredEndpoint("wheel")
    if endpoint and not string.find(string.lower(endpoint), "fuse", 1, true) then
        App.Runtime.SpinWheelEndpoint = endpoint
    end
    return App.Runtime.SpinWheelEndpoint
end

function App.stepAutoSpinWheel(feature, version)
    local endpoint = App.getSpinWheelEndpoint()
    if not endpoint then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Spin remote not found")
        return 3
    end
    local ok, result = App.invokeDiscoveredEndpoint(endpoint)
    if ok then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Wheel spun")
    else
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, result or "Spin failed")
    end
    return 1
end

function App.discoverFusePets()
    local data = App.getSaveData()
    local inventory = type(data) == "table" and data.Inventory or nil
    local serializer = App.Game.Modules.AssetItemSerialization
    if not serializer then
        local ok, result = App.requirePath({"Library", "Util", "AssetItemSerialization"}, "AssetItemSerialization")
        if ok then
            serializer = result
            App.Game.Modules.AssetItemSerialization = result
        end
    end
    local fuseUtil = App.Game.Modules.FuseKernelUtil
    if not fuseUtil then
        local ok, result = App.requirePath({"Library", "Util", "FuseKernelUtil"}, "FuseKernelUtil")
        if ok then
            fuseUtil = result
            App.Game.Modules.FuseKernelUtil = result
        end
    end
    local grouped = {}
    local records = {}
    if type(inventory) == "table" and type(serializer) == "table" and type(serializer.Deserialize) == "function" then
        for inventoryId, raw in pairs(inventory) do
            local ok, pet = pcall(serializer.Deserialize, raw)
            if ok and type(pet) == "table" then
                local category = tostring(pet.Category or pet.AssetCategory or pet.Id or "")
                local allowed = category ~= ""
                if allowed and type(fuseUtil) == "table" and type(fuseUtil.CanSelectPet) == "function" then
                    local checkOk, checkResult = pcall(
                        fuseUtil.CanSelectPet,
                        category,
                        pet,
                        nil,
                        false
                    )
                    allowed = checkOk and checkResult ~= false
                end
                local name = tostring(pet.DisplayName or pet.Name or category)
                if allowed and name ~= "" then
                    grouped[name] = grouped[name] or {}
                    grouped[name][#grouped[name] + 1] = tostring(inventoryId)
                    records[tostring(inventoryId)] = pet
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local options = {}
    local ids = {}
    for name, entries in pairs(grouped) do
        if #entries >= 3 then
            options[#options + 1] = name
            ids[name] = entries
        end
    end
    table.sort(options)
    App.Runtime.FusePetOptions = options
    App.Runtime.FusePetIds = ids
    App.Runtime.FusePetRecords = records
    if not App.Runtime.SelectedFusePet or not ids[App.Runtime.SelectedFusePet] then
        App.Runtime.SelectedFusePet = options[1]
    end
    return options
end

function App.getFuseEndpoint(kind)
    local constants = App.Game.Constants
    local map = type(constants) == "table" and constants.NETWORK_MAP or nil
    local fuseMap = type(map) == "table" and (map.FuseMachine or (map.Endpoints and map.Endpoints.FuseMachine)) or nil
    if type(fuseMap) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    if kind == "insert" then return fuseMap.INSERT_MOB end
    if kind == "remove" then return fuseMap.REMOVE_MOB end
    if kind == "start" then return fuseMap.START_FUSE end
    if kind == "complete" then return fuseMap.COMPLETE_REVEAL end
    if kind == "acknowledge" then return fuseMap.ACKNOWLEDGE_INFO end
    return nil
end

function App.getFuseSlotRecords(data)
    data = data or App.getSaveData()
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return {}, data end
    local inventory = type(data.Inventory) == "table" and data.Inventory or {}
    local serializer = App.Game.Modules.AssetItemSerialization
    if not serializer then
        local ok, result = App.requirePath({"Library", "Util", "AssetItemSerialization"}, "AssetItemSerialization")
        if ok then
            serializer = result
            App.Game.Modules.AssetItemSerialization = result
        end
    end
    local result = {}
    local slots = type(data.FusionSlots) == "table" and data.FusionSlots or {}
    for index = 1, 3 do
        local uid = slots[index]
        if uid ~= nil then
            local raw = inventory[uid] or inventory[tostring(uid)]
            local pet = raw
            if raw and type(serializer) == "table" and type(serializer.Deserialize) == "function" then
                local ok, decoded = pcall(serializer.Deserialize, raw)
                if ok and type(decoded) == "table" then pet = decoded end
            end
            if type(pet) == "table" then
                result[#result + 1] = {Index = index, Uid = tostring(uid), Pet = pet}
            end
        end
    end
    return result, data
end

function App.getFusePrediction()
    local slots, data = App.getFuseSlotRecords()
    local pets = {}
    local scales = {}
    local category = nil
    for _, slot in ipairs(slots) do
        local pet = slot.Pet
        pets[#pets + 1] = pet
        local value = tonumber(pet.Scale or pet.Weight or pet.Size)
        if value and value > 0 then scales[#scales + 1] = value end
        category = category or pet.Category or pet.AssetCategory
    end
    local prediction = {
        Slots = slots,
        Count = #slots,
        Category = category,
        Scales = scales,
        AverageScale = nil,
        RolledScale = nil,
        Price = nil,
        Locked = data and data.FusionLocked == true or false,
        RewardReady = data and (data.FusionEggReward == true or data.FusionReward ~= nil) or false,
    }
    if #scales == 3 then
        prediction.AverageScale = (scales[1] + scales[2] + scales[3]) / 3
    end
    local fuseUtil = App.Game.Modules.FuseKernelUtil
    if not fuseUtil then
        local ok, result = App.requirePath({"Library", "Util", "FuseKernelUtil"}, "FuseKernelUtil")
        if ok then
            fuseUtil = result
            App.Game.Modules.FuseKernelUtil = result
        end
    end
    if #pets == 3 and type(fuseUtil) == "table" then
        if type(fuseUtil.CalculateFusePrice) == "function" then
            local ok, value = pcall(fuseUtil.CalculateFusePrice, pets)
            if ok then prediction.Price = tonumber(value) end
        end
        if prediction.AverageScale and category and type(fuseUtil.RollFusedAssetScale) == "function" then
            local ok, value = pcall(fuseUtil.RollFusedAssetScale, scales, category)
            if ok and tonumber(value) then prediction.RolledScale = tonumber(value) end
        end
    end
    return prediction
end

function App.runFuseMachine()
    local insertEndpoint = App.getFuseEndpoint("insert")
    local startEndpoint = App.getFuseEndpoint("start")
    local completeEndpoint = App.getFuseEndpoint("complete")
    local acknowledgeEndpoint = App.getFuseEndpoint("acknowledge")
    if not insertEndpoint or not startEndpoint or not completeEndpoint then
        return false, "Fuse Machine endpoints are unavailable"
    end
    local slots, data = App.getFuseSlotRecords()
    if data and (data.FusionEggReward == true or data.FusionReward ~= nil) then
        local revealOk, revealResult = App.invokeNetwork(completeEndpoint)
        if revealOk and acknowledgeEndpoint then
            App.invokeNetwork(acknowledgeEndpoint)
        end
        return revealOk, revealResult
    end
    if data and data.FusionLocked == true then
        return false, "Fuse Machine is processing the current fuse"
    end
    local selected = App.Runtime.SelectedFusePet
    local ids = selected and App.Runtime.FusePetIds[selected]
    if type(ids) ~= "table" or #ids < 3 then
        return false, "Select a pet with at least 3 eligible copies"
    end
    local used = {}
    for _, slot in ipairs(slots) do used[slot.Uid] = true end
    local needed = 3 - #slots
    local nextIndex = 1
    while needed > 0 and nextIndex <= #ids do
        local uid = tostring(ids[nextIndex])
        nextIndex = nextIndex + 1
        if not used[uid] then
            local ok, result = App.invokeNetwork(insertEndpoint, uid)
            if not ok then return false, result end
            used[uid] = true
            needed = needed - 1
            task.wait(0.12)
        end
    end
    local refreshedSlots, refreshedData = App.getFuseSlotRecords()
    if #refreshedSlots < 3 then
        return true, "Loading fuse slots" 
    end
    if refreshedData and refreshedData.FusionLocked == true then
        return true, "Fuse started"
    end
    local started, result = App.invokeNetwork(startEndpoint)
    if started and acknowledgeEndpoint and refreshedData and refreshedData.FusionInfoAcknowledged == false then
        App.invokeNetwork(acknowledgeEndpoint)
    end
    return started, result
end

function App.formatClock(seconds)
    seconds = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(seconds) or 0))
    return string.format("%02d:%02d", math.floor(seconds / 60), seconds % 60)
end

function App.updateNightLabel()
    local label = App.Runtime.NightLabel
    if not label then return end
    local util = App.Game.Modules.AreaEggResetTimeUtil
    if not util then
        local ok, result = App.requirePath({"Library", "Util", "AreaEggResetTimeUtil"}, "AreaEggResetTimeUtil")
        if ok then
            util = result
            App.Game.Modules.AreaEggResetTimeUtil = result
        end
    end
    local now = os.time()
    local left = 0
    local nextNight = nil
    if type(util) == "table" then
        if type(util.GetTimeLeft) == "function" then
            local ok, value = pcall(util.GetTimeLeft, now)
            if ok then left = tonumber(value) or 0 end
        end
        if type(util.GetNextNightAt) == "function" then
            local ok, value = pcall(util.GetNextNightAt, now)
            if ok then nextNight = tonumber(value) end
        end
    end
    local data = App.getSaveData()
    local speed = data and (data.SpeedPower or data.Speed or data.LeaderstatSpeed) or nil
    local leaderstats = App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
    if leaderstats then
        local speedValue = leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Speed") or leaderstats:FindFirstChild("SpeedPower")
        if speedValue and tonumber(speedValue.Value) then
            speed = speedValue.Value
        end
    end
    local nightText
    if left > 0 then
        nightText = "Night Time: Active, ends in " .. App.formatClock(left)
    elseif nextNight then
        nightText = "Night Time: Starts in " .. App.formatClock(nextNight - now)
    else
        nightText = "Night Time: Unavailable"
    end
    local progressionText = string.format(
        "%s\nLeaderstat Speed: %s   Money/s: %s",
        nightText,
        App.formatIncome(speed or 0),
        App.formatIncome(App.getIncomePerSecond())
    )
    pcall(function() label:SetText(progressionText) end)
end

local WeatherDescriptions = {
    FrostWeather = "Frozen weather: applies Frozen mutation and boosts Frozen production.",
    ThunderstormWeather = "Thunderstorm: applies Shocked mutation and boosts Shocked production.",
    MagmaWeather = "Magma weather: applies Magma mutation and boosts Magma production.",
}
local WeatherDisplayNames = {
    FrostWeather = "Frost Weather",
    ThunderstormWeather = "Thunderstorm",
    MagmaWeather = "Magma Weather",
}
function App.getWeatherIcon(weatherId)
    weatherId = tostring(weatherId or "")
    if App.Runtime.WeatherIconCache[weatherId] then return App.Runtime.WeatherIconCache[weatherId] end
    local roots = {App.Services.ReplicatedStorage, App.Services.Workspace}
    for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
        for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            local name = string.lower(obj.Name or "")
            if string.find(name, string.lower(weatherId), 1, true) then
                local image = obj:IsA("ImageLabel") and obj.Image
                    or obj:IsA("ImageButton") and obj.Image
                    or obj:IsA("Decal") and obj.Texture
                    or obj:IsA("Texture") and obj.Texture
                if type(image) == "string" and image ~= "" then
                    App.Runtime.WeatherIconCache[weatherId] = image
                    return image
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return ""
end
function App.getWeatherPrediction()
    local schedule = App.Game.Modules.WeatherSchedule
    if not schedule then
        local ok, result = App.requirePath({"Library", "Weather", "Schedule"}, "Weather Schedule")
        if ok then
            schedule = result
            App.Game.Modules.WeatherSchedule = result
        end
    end
    if type(schedule) ~= "table" then return {} end
    local now = os.time()
    local result = {}
    local currentIndex
    local currentStart
    local currentWeather
    if type(schedule.GetCycleIndex) == "function" and type(schedule.GetTimeIntoCycle) == "function" and type(schedule.ChooseCycleWeathers) == "function" then
        local okIndex, index = pcall(schedule.GetCycleIndex, now)
        local okInto, into = pcall(schedule.GetTimeIntoCycle, now)
        if okIndex and okInto then
            currentIndex = tonumber(index)
            currentStart = now - (tonumber(into) or 0)
