            local okWeather, list = pcall(schedule.ChooseCycleWeathers, currentIndex)
            if okWeather and type(list) == "table" then currentWeather = list[1] end
        end
    end
    local duration = tonumber(schedule.DEFAULT_DURATION) or 180
    if currentWeather then
        local entry = type(schedule.GetEntry) == "function" and schedule.GetEntry(currentWeather) or nil
        local endAt = currentStart + duration
        if endAt > now then
            result[#result + 1] = {
                Id = currentWeather,
                Name = WeatherDisplayNames[currentWeather] or currentWeather,
                Description = WeatherDescriptions[currentWeather] or "Current weather event.",
                StartsAt = currentStart,
                EndsAt = endAt,
                SecondsLeft = endAt - now,
                Current = true,
                Icon = App.getWeatherIcon(currentWeather),
                Boosts = entry and entry.Boosts,
            }
        end
    end
    if type(schedule.GetUpcomingWeathers) == "function" then
        local okUpcoming, upcoming = pcall(schedule.GetUpcomingWeathers, now, 6, false)
        if okUpcoming and type(upcoming) == "table" then
            for _, item in ipairs(upcoming) do
                local id = tostring(item.WeatherId or "")
                local startsAt = tonumber(item.StartsAt)
                if id ~= "" and startsAt then
                    result[#result + 1] = {
                        Id = id,
                        Name = WeatherDisplayNames[id] or id,
                        Description = WeatherDescriptions[id] or "Upcoming weather event.",
                        StartsAt = startsAt,
                        EndsAt = startsAt + duration,
                        SecondsLeft = math.max(0, startsAt - now),
                        Current = false,
                        Icon = App.getWeatherIcon(id),
                    }
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return result
end
function App.formatWeatherTime(timestamp)
    return os.date("%I:%M %p", math.floor(tonumber(timestamp) or os.time()))
end
function App.clearWeatherDisplay()
    for _, element in ipairs(App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionElements or {}) do
        pcall(function() element:Destroy() end)
    end
    App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionElements = {}
end
function App.renderWeatherPrediction(addLabel)
    App.clearWeatherDisplay()
    local entries = App.getWeatherPrediction()
    if #entries == 0 then
        local label = addLabel("Weather data is unavailable")
        table.insert(App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionElements, label)
        return
    end
    for _, item in ipairs(entries) do
        local title = item.Current and "NOW: " or "NEXT: "
        local left = item.Current and App.formatClock(item.SecondsLeft) or ("starts in " .. App.formatClock(item.SecondsLeft))
        local text = title .. item.Name .. "\n" .. item.Description .. "\nTime Left: " .. left .. " | Time Will End: " .. App.formatWeatherTime(item.EndsAt)
        local holder = addLabel(text, item.Icon)
        table.insert(App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionElements, holder)
    end
end
function App.startWeatherPrediction(addLabel)
    if App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionThread then return end
    App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionThread = task.spawn(function()
        while not App.Runtime.Unloading and App.Runtime.Enabled.WeatherPrediction do
            pcall(App.renderWeatherPrediction, addLabel)
            task.wait(1)
        end
        App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionThread = nil
    end)
end
function App.stopWeatherPrediction()
    App.Runtime.Enabled.WeatherPrediction = false
    if App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionThread and task.cancel then pcall(task.cancel, App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionThread) end
    App.Runtime.WeatherPredictionThread = nil
    App.clearWeatherDisplay()
end

function App.getIncomePerSecond()
    local data = App.getSaveData()
    local util = App.Game.Modules.PlayerMoneyPerSecondUtil
    if not util then
        local ok, result = App.requirePath(
            { "Library", "Util", "PlayerMoneyPerSecondUtil" },
            "PlayerMoneyPerSecondUtil"
        )
        if ok then
            util = result
            App.Game.Modules.PlayerMoneyPerSecondUtil = result
        end
    end
    if type(data) ~= "table" or type(util) ~= "table" or type(util.GetTotalForProfile) ~= "function" then
        return 0
    end
    local ok, value = pcall(util.GetTotalForProfile, util, data)
    return ok and math.max(tonumber(value) or 0, 0) or 0
end

function App.formatIncome(value)
    value = tonumber(value) or 0
    local suffixes = {
        {1e27, "Oc"},
        {1e24, "Sp"},
        {1e21, "Sx"},
        {1e18, "Qi"},
        {1e15, "Qa"},
        {1e12, "T"},
        {1e9, "B"},
        {1e6, "M"},
        {1e3, "K"},
    }
    for _, suffix in ipairs(suffixes) do
        if value >= suffix[1] then
            return string.format("%.2f%s", value / suffix[1], suffix[2])
        end
    end
    return string.format("%.0f", value)
end

function App.getTrapPosition(trap)
    if not trap or not trap.Parent then
        return nil
    end
    local hitbox = trap:FindFirstChild("Hitbox", true)
    if hitbox and hitbox:IsA("BasePart") then
        return hitbox.Position
    end
    local pivotOk, pivot = pcall(function()
        return trap:GetPivot()
    end)
    if pivotOk and typeof(pivot) == "CFrame" then
        return pivot.Position
    end
    return nil
end

function App.isTrapOnPath(startPosition, targetPosition)
    if not App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoAvoidTrap then
        return nil
    end
    for _, trapPosition in ipairs(App.getActiveTrapPositions()) do
        if App.pointToSegmentDistance(trapPosition, startPosition, targetPosition) <= App.Config.TrapAvoidRadius then
            return trapPosition
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function App.getSafeTrapPoint(startPosition, trapPosition)
    local away = Vector3.new(startPosition.X - trapPosition.X, 0, startPosition.Z - trapPosition.Z)
    if away.Magnitude < 0.1 then
        away = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
    else
        away = away.Unit
    end
    local distance = App.Config.TrapAvoidRadius + 14
    return Vector3.new(
        trapPosition.X + away.X * distance,
        startPosition.Y,
        trapPosition.Z + away.Z * distance
    )
end

function App.stopTrapESP()
    for trap, billboard in pairs(App.Runtime.TrapESPLabels or {}) do
        if billboard and billboard.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                billboard:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        App.Runtime.TrapESPLabels[trap] = nil
    end
    local folder = App.Runtime.TrapESPFolder
    if folder and folder.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            folder:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    App.Runtime.TrapESPFolder = nil
    App.Runtime.TrapESPLabels = {}
end

function App.updateTrapESP()
    if not App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoESPTrap or App.Runtime.Unloading then
        return
    end
    local folder = App.Runtime.TrapESPFolder
    if not folder or not folder.Parent then
        folder = Instance.new("Folder")
        folder.Name = "StealAnEggTrapESP"
        folder.Parent = App.Services.Workspace
        App.Runtime.TrapESPFolder = folder
    end
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local seen = {}
    for _, trap in ipairs(App.Services.CollectionService:GetTagged("PlacedTrap")) do
        if trap.Parent and trap:GetAttribute("TrapActive") ~= true then
            local position = App.getTrapPosition(trap)
            if position then
                seen[trap] = true
                local billboard = App.Runtime.TrapESPLabels[trap]
                if not billboard or not billboard.Parent then
                    billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                    billboard.Name = "StealAnEggTrapESPLabel"
                    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
                    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
                    billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge
                    billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 42)
                    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
                    billboard.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
                    billboard.Adornee = trap:FindFirstChild("Hitbox", true)
                    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
                    frame.Name = "TrapESPFrame"
                    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    frame.Parent = billboard
                    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                    icon.Name = "TrapIcon"
                    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    icon.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    icon.Image = "rbxassetid://98146157051289"
                    icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28)
                    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -14)
                    icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
                    icon.Parent = frame
                    local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    nameLabel.Name = "TrapName"
                    nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    nameLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                    nameLabel.TextSize = 14
                    nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 19)
                    nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 1)
                    nameLabel.Text = "Trap"
                    nameLabel.Parent = frame
                    local distanceLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    distanceLabel.Name = "TrapDistance"
                    distanceLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    distanceLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    distanceLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
                    distanceLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 80)
                    distanceLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                    distanceLabel.TextSize = 12
                    distanceLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    distanceLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 17)
                    distanceLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 20)
                    distanceLabel.Parent = frame
                    billboard.Parent = folder
                    App.Runtime.TrapESPLabels[trap] = billboard
                end
                local hitbox = trap:FindFirstChild("Hitbox", true)
                billboard.Adornee = hitbox and hitbox:IsA("BasePart") and hitbox or trap
                local distanceLabel = billboard:FindFirstChild("TrapESPFrame") and billboard.TrapESPFrame:FindFirstChild("TrapDistance")
                if distanceLabel then
                    local distance = root and (root.Position - position).Magnitude or 0
                    distanceLabel.Text = string.format("%dm", math.floor(distance + 0.5))
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for trap, billboard in pairs(App.Runtime.TrapESPLabels) do
        if not seen[trap] or not trap.Parent then
            if billboard and billboard.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    billboard:Destroy()
                end)
            end
            App.Runtime.TrapESPLabels[trap] = nil
        end
    end
end

function App.stepAutoESPTrap(feature, version)
    App.updateTrapESP()
    return 0.5
end

function App.updateIncomeOverlay()
    local label = App.Runtime.IncomeGui and App.Runtime.IncomeGui:FindFirstChild("IncomeLabel")
    if not label then return end
    local perSecond = App.getIncomePerSecond()
    label.Text = string.format(
        "Income  %s/s   %s/hr   %s/day",
        App.formatIncome(perSecond),
        App.formatIncome(perSecond * 3600),
        App.formatIncome(perSecond * 86400)
    )
end

function App.startIncomeOverlay()
    if App.Runtime.IncomeGui then return end
    local playerGui = App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not playerGui then return end
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "StealAnEggIncomeESP"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.Parent = playerGui
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "IncomeLabel"
    label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8)
    label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 22)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    label.TextSize = 14
    label.Parent = gui
    App.Runtime.IncomeGui = gui
    App.updateIncomeOverlay()
    local thread
    thread = task.spawn(function()
        while not App.Runtime.Unloading and App.Runtime.EspModes.IncomeMoney and App.Runtime.IncomeGui == gui do
            task.wait(1)
            if App.Runtime.Unloading or not App.Runtime.EspModes.IncomeMoney or App.Runtime.IncomeGui ~= gui then
                break
            end
            pcall(App.updateIncomeOverlay)
        end
        if App.Runtime.IncomeThread == thread then
            App.Runtime.IncomeThread = nil
        end
    end)
    App.Runtime.IncomeThread = thread
end
function App.stopIncomeOverlay()
    if App.Runtime.IncomeConnection then
        pcall(function() App.Runtime.IncomeConnection:Disconnect() end)
        App.Runtime.IncomeConnection = nil
    end
    local thread = App.Runtime.IncomeThread
    App.Runtime.IncomeThread = nil
    if type(thread) == "thread" and task and type(task.cancel) == "function" then
        pcall(task.cancel, thread)
    end
    if App.Runtime.IncomeGui then
        pcall(function() App.Runtime.IncomeGui:Destroy() end)
        App.Runtime.IncomeGui = nil
    end
end

function App.getLocalHumanoid()
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

function App.setFpsBooster(enabled)
    if enabled then
        if App.Runtime.FpsBoosterState then return true end
        local state = {
            Lighting = {},
            Terrain = nil,
            Instances = {},
        }
        local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
        state.Lighting.GlobalShadows = lighting.GlobalShadows
        state.Lighting.FogEnd = lighting.FogEnd
        lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        lighting.FogEnd = 1000000
        local terrain = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
        if terrain then
            state.Terrain = {Object = terrain, Decoration = terrain.Decoration}
            terrain.Decoration = false
        end
        for _, object in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
            local property
            if object:IsA("PostEffect") then
                property = "Enabled"
            elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or object:IsA("Trail") or object:IsA("Beam") then
                property = "Enabled"
            elseif object:IsA("BasePart") then
                property = "CastShadow"
                local materialOk, material = pcall(function() return object.Material end)
                if materialOk then
                    table.insert(state.Instances, {Object = object, Property = "Material", Value = material})
                    pcall(function() object.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic end)
                end
            end
            if property then
                local ok, value = pcall(function() return object[property] end)
                if ok then
                    table.insert(state.Instances, {Object = object, Property = property, Value = value})
                    pcall(function() object[property] = property == "CastShadow" and false or false end)
                end
            end
        end
        App.Runtime.FpsBoosterState = state
        App.Runtime.Enabled.FpsBooster = true
        return true
    end
    local state = App.Runtime.FpsBoosterState
    App.Runtime.FpsBoosterState = nil
    App.Runtime.Enabled.FpsBooster = false
    if not state then return true end
    for _, entry in ipairs(state.Instances or {}) do
        if entry.Object and entry.Object.Parent then
            pcall(function() entry.Object[entry.Property] = entry.Value end)
        end
    end
    local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    if state.Lighting then
        pcall(function() lighting.GlobalShadows = state.Lighting.GlobalShadows end)
        pcall(function() lighting.FogEnd = state.Lighting.FogEnd end)
    end
    if state.Terrain and state.Terrain.Object and state.Terrain.Object.Parent then
        pcall(function() state.Terrain.Object.Decoration = state.Terrain.Decoration end)
    end
    return true
end

function App.capturePerformanceState()
    if App.Runtime.PerformanceState then
        return App.Runtime.PerformanceState
    end
    local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local state = {
        Lighting = {},
        Terrain = nil,
        Instances = {},
    }
    pcall(function()
        state.Lighting.GlobalShadows = lighting.GlobalShadows
        state.Lighting.FogEnd = lighting.FogEnd
        state.Lighting.Brightness = lighting.Brightness
        state.Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
        state.Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale
    end)
    local terrain = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if terrain then
        pcall(function()
            state.Terrain = {
                Object = terrain,
                Decoration = terrain.Decoration,
                WaterWaveSize = terrain.WaterWaveSize,
                WaterWaveSpeed = terrain.WaterWaveSpeed,
                WaterReflectance = terrain.WaterReflectance,
                WaterTransparency = terrain.WaterTransparency,
            }
        end)
    end
    for _, object in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
        if object:IsA("PostEffect") or object:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or object:IsA("Trail") or object:IsA("Beam") then
            local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return object.Enabled end)
            if ok then
                table.insert(state.Instances, {Object = object, Property = "Enabled", Value = enabled})
            end
        elseif object:IsA("BasePart") then
            local okShadow, castShadow = pcall(function() return object.CastShadow end)
            if okShadow then
                table.insert(state.Instances, {Object = object, Property = "CastShadow", Value = castShadow})
            end
            local okMaterial, material = pcall(function() return object.Material end)
            if okMaterial then
                table.insert(state.Instances, {Object = object, Property = "Material", Value = material})
            end
            if object:IsA("MeshPart") then
                local okFidelity, fidelity = pcall(function() return object.RenderFidelity end)
                if okFidelity then
                    table.insert(state.Instances, {Object = object, Property = "RenderFidelity", Value = fidelity})
                end
            end
        end
    end
    App.Runtime.PerformanceState = state
    return state
end

function App.applyPerformanceMode(mode)
    mode = tostring(mode or "Performance")
    local state = App.capturePerformanceState()
    local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local reduceGraphics = mode == "Reduce Graphics" or mode == "Performance"
    local reduceEffects = mode == "Reduce Effects" or mode == "Performance"
    local reduceQuality = mode == "Reduce Quality" or mode == "Performance"
    local plastic = mode == "Plastic Models" or mode == "Performance"
    if reduceGraphics then
        pcall(function()
            lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            lighting.FogEnd = 1000000
            lighting.Brightness = math.max(tonumber(lighting.Brightness) or 1, 1)
            lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
            lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
        end)
        if state.Terrain and state.Terrain.Object and state.Terrain.Object.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                state.Terrain.Object.Decoration = false
                state.Terrain.Object.WaterWaveSize = 0
                state.Terrain.Object.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
                state.Terrain.Object.WaterReflectance = 0
            end)
        end
    end
    for _, entry in ipairs(state.Instances) do
        local object = entry.Object
        if object and object.Parent then
            if reduceEffects and entry.Property == "Enabled" then
                pcall(function() object.Enabled = false end)
            elseif reduceQuality and entry.Property == "CastShadow" then
                pcall(function() object.CastShadow = false end)
            elseif plastic and entry.Property == "Material" then
                pcall(function() object.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic end)
            elseif reduceQuality and entry.Property == "RenderFidelity" then
                pcall(function() object.RenderFidelity = Enum.RenderFidelity.Performance end)
            end
        end
    end
    notify("Performance", mode .. " applied", 2)
    return true
end

function App.restorePerformanceMode()
    local state = App.Runtime.PerformanceState
    App.Runtime.PerformanceState = nil
    if not state then
        notify("Performance", "No saved graphics state", 2)
        return true
    end
    for _, entry in ipairs(state.Instances or {}) do
        if entry.Object and entry.Object.Parent then
            pcall(function() entry.Object[entry.Property] = entry.Value end)
        end
    end
    local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    for property, value in pairs(state.Lighting or {}) do
        pcall(function() lighting[property] = value end)
    end
    if state.Terrain and state.Terrain.Object and state.Terrain.Object.Parent then
        for property, value in pairs(state.Terrain) do
            if property ~= "Object" then
                pcall(function() state.Terrain.Object[property] = value end)
            end
        end
    end
    notify("Performance", "Original graphics restored", 2)
    return true
end

function App.normalizeBatLookup(value)
    local textValue = string.lower(tostring(value or ""))
    textValue = textValue:gsub("rbxassetid://", "")
    textValue = textValue:gsub("%s+", "")
    textValue = textValue:gsub("[^%w]", "")
    return textValue
end
function App.normalizeBatIcon(value)
    if type(value) == "number" then
        return "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(value)
    end
    if type(value) ~= "string" then return nil end
    if value == "" or value == "N/A" then return nil end
    if value:match("^%d+$") then return "rbxassetid://" .. value end
    return value
end
function App.readBatModuleData(moduleScript)
    if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then return nil end
    local ok, value = pcall(require, moduleScript)
    if not ok or type(value) ~= "table" then value = {} end
    local function read(key)
        local result = value[key]
        if result == nil then result = moduleScript:GetAttribute(key) end
        return result
    end
    local icon = read("Icon") or read("Image") or read("TextureId") or read("AssetId")
    if type(icon) == "table" then
        icon = icon.Image or icon.Icon or icon.AssetId or icon.TextureId
    end
    local displayName = read("DisplayName") or read("Name") or moduleScript.Name
    local aliases = {moduleScript.Name, displayName, read("Id"), read("ItemId"), read("GearId"), read("InternalName")}
    return {
        Name = tostring(displayName or moduleScript.Name),
        Icon = App.normalizeBatIcon(icon),
        Aliases = aliases,
    }
end
function App.scanBatGearModules()
    local map = {}
    local other = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Directory")
    other = other and other:FindFirstChild("Gears")
    other = other and other:FindFirstChild("_Index")
    other = other and other:FindFirstChild("Other")
    if not other then
        App.Runtime.UserBatGearMap = map
        return map
    end
    for _, moduleScript in ipairs(other:GetDescendants()) do
        if moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
            local data = App.readBatModuleData(moduleScript)
            local nameText = string.lower(moduleScript.Name .. " " .. tostring(data and data.Name or ""))
            if data and string.find(nameText, "bat", 1, true) then
                for _, alias in ipairs(data.Aliases or {}) do
                    local key = App.normalizeBatLookup(alias)
                    if key ~= "" then map[key] = data end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    App.Runtime.UserBatGearMap = map
    return map
end
function App.getEquippedBatTool(player)
    local character = player and player.Character
    if not character then return nil end
    for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Tool") then
            local values = {
                child.Name,
                child.ToolTip,
                child:GetAttribute("DisplayName"),
                child:GetAttribute("ItemName"),
                child:GetAttribute("GearId"),
                child:GetAttribute("ItemId"),
                child:GetAttribute("Id"),
                child:GetAttribute("ID"),
            }
            local joined = string.lower(table.concat((function()
                local result = {}
                for _, value in ipairs(values) do
                    if value ~= nil then table.insert(result, tostring(value)) end
                end
                return result
            end)(), " "))
            if string.find(joined, "bat", 1, true) then
                return child, values
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function App.resolveEquippedBat(player, map)
    local tool, values = App.getEquippedBatTool(player)
    if not tool then return nil end
    map = map or App.Runtime.UserBatGearMap or App.scanBatGearModules()
    local fallback = {
        Name = tostring(tool:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or tool:GetAttribute("ItemName") or tool.Name),
        Icon = nil,
    }
    for _, value in ipairs(values or {}) do
        local data = map[App.normalizeBatLookup(value)]
        if data then
            return {Tool = tool, Name = data.Name, Icon = data.Icon}
        end
    end
    return {Tool = tool, Name = fallback.Name, Icon = fallback.Icon}
end
function App.destroyUserBatESPEntry(player)
    local entry = App.Runtime.UserBatESPEntries[player]
    if entry then
        if entry.Gui then pcall(function() entry.Gui:Destroy() end) end
        App.Runtime.UserBatESPEntries[player] = nil
    end
end
function App.updateUserBatESPEntry(player, batInfo)
    if not player or not batInfo or not batInfo.Tool then return end
    local character = player.Character
    local root = character and (character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character.PrimaryPart)
    if not root then return end
    local entry = App.Runtime.UserBatESPEntries[player]
    if not entry or not entry.Gui or not entry.Gui.Parent then
        local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        gui.Name = "SAEUserBatESP"
        gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
        gui.LightInfluence = 0
        gui.MaxDistance = 1000
        gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(250, 52)
        gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
        gui.Adornee = root
        local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame.Name = "UserBatESPFrame"
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        frame.Parent = gui
        local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        icon.Name = "BatIcon"
        icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        icon.BorderSizePixel = 0
        icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 8)
        icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 36)
        icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        icon.Parent = frame
        local userLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        userLabel.Name = "Username"
        userLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        userLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 2)
        userLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 0, 22)
        userLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        userLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        userLabel.TextSize = 13
        userLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        userLabel.Parent = frame
        local equippedLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        equippedLabel.Name = "EquippedWith"
        equippedLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        equippedLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 25)
        equippedLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 0, 22)
        equippedLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        equippedLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
        equippedLabel.TextSize = 11
        equippedLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        equippedLabel.Parent = frame
        gui.Parent = App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or App.LocalPlayer
        entry = {Gui = gui, Icon = icon, Username = userLabel, EquippedWith = equippedLabel}
        App.Runtime.UserBatESPEntries[player] = entry
    end
    entry.Gui.Adornee = root
    entry.Username.Text = player.Name
    entry.EquippedWith.Text = "Is Equipped with the - " .. tostring(batInfo.Name or "Bat")
    entry.Icon.Image = batInfo.Icon or "rbxassetid://6031075931"
end
function App.stopUserBatESP()
    App.Runtime.Enabled.ESPUserBat = false
    local thread = App.Runtime.UserBatESPThread
    App.Runtime.UserBatESPThread = nil
    if thread and task and type(task.cancel) == "function" then pcall(task.cancel, thread) end
    for player, connection in pairs(App.Runtime.UserBatESPConnections or {}) do
        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
        App.Runtime.UserBatESPConnections[player] = nil
    end
    for player in pairs(App.Runtime.UserBatESPEntries or {}) do App.destroyUserBatESPEntry(player) end
end
function App.startUserBatESP()
    App.stopUserBatESP()
    App.Runtime.Enabled.ESPUserBat = true
    App.scanBatGearModules()
    App.Runtime.UserBatESPThread = task.spawn(function()
        while App.Runtime.Enabled.ESPUserBat and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
            local map = App.Runtime.UserBatGearMap
            for _, player in ipairs(App.Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= App.LocalPlayer then
                    local batInfo = App.resolveEquippedBat(player, map)
                    if batInfo then
                        App.updateUserBatESPEntry(player, batInfo)
                    else
                        App.destroyUserBatESPEntry(player)
                    end
                end
            end
            for player in pairs(App.Runtime.UserBatESPEntries) do
                if not player.Parent then App.destroyUserBatESPEntry(player) end
            end
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end)
    App.Runtime.UserBatESPConnections.PlayerRemoving = App.Services.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
        App.destroyUserBatESPEntry(player)
    end)
end

function App.getGearModule(name)
    if App.Runtime.GearModules[name] ~= nil then
        return App.Runtime.GearModules[name]
    end
    local ok, module = App.requirePath({"Directory", "Gears", "_Index", "Other", name}, name .. " gear")
    App.Runtime.GearModules[name] = ok and module or false
    return ok and module or nil
end

function App.getGearCooldown(name, fallback)
    local module = App.getGearModule(name)
    local cooldown = module and tonumber(module.COOLDOWN)
    return cooldown and math.max(cooldown, 0.1) or fallback
end

function App.fireGear(groupName, actionName, ...)
    local network = App.Game.Modules.Network
    local group = App.getNetworkGroup(groupName)
    local remote = group and group[actionName]
    if type(network) ~= "table" or type(network.Fire) ~= "function" or type(remote) ~= "string" then
        return false, "The " .. tostring(groupName) .. " network is unavailable"
    end
    local result = table.pack(pcall(network.Fire, remote, ...))
    if result[1] ~= true then
        return false, result[2]
    end
    return true
end

function App.findClosestBodySwapTarget()
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character.PrimaryPart
    if not root then
        return nil
    end
    local gear = App.getGearModule("BodySwap")
    local maxRange = gear and tonumber(gear.MAX_RANGE) or 20
    local closestPlayer = nil
    local closestDistance = maxRange
    for _, player in ipairs(App.Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= App.LocalPlayer then
            local targetCharacter = player.Character
            local targetRoot = targetCharacter and targetCharacter.PrimaryPart
            if targetRoot then
                local distance = (targetRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if distance <= closestDistance then
                    closestPlayer = player
                    closestDistance = distance
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closestPlayer
end

function App.stepAutoBodySwapNearby(feature)
    local target = App.findClosestBodySwapTarget()
    if target then
        local ok, err = App.fireGear("BodySwap", "REQUEST_SWAP", target)
        if not ok then
            App.reportFeatureError(feature, err)
        else
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Swapping with the nearest player")
        end
    else
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for a nearby player")
    end
    return App.getGearCooldown("BodySwap", 2)
end

        App.reportFeatureError(feature, err)

        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Using Boogie")
    end
    return App.getGearCooldown("Boogie", 5)

function App.stepAutoLaserCap(feature)
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head or not head:IsA("BasePart") then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for the character head")
        return 1
    end
    local mouse = App.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
    local targetPosition = mouse and mouse.Hit and mouse.Hit.Position
    if typeof(targetPosition) ~= "Vector3" then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for a laser target")
        return 1
    end
    local direction = targetPosition - head.Position
    if direction.Magnitude <= 0 or direction.Magnitude ~= direction.Magnitude then
        return 1
    end
    local ok, err = App.fireGear("LaserCap", "REQUEST_FIRE", head.Position, direction.Unit)
    if not ok then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, err)
    else
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Firing Laser Cap")
    end
    return App.getGearCooldown("LaserCap", 2)
end

function App.getCarryUpgradeNetworkNames()
    local names = {}
    local seen = {}
    local function add(value, context)
        if type(value) ~= "string" then
            return
        end
        local text = string.lower(tostring(context or "") .. " " .. value)
        if string.find(text, "carry", 1, true)
            and string.find(text, "upgrade", 1, true)
            and not string.find(text, "robux", 1, true)
            and not seen[value]
        then
            seen[value] = true
            table.insert(names, value)
        end
    end
    local function walk(node, context, depth)
        if depth > 5 or type(node) ~= "table" then
            return
        end
        for key, value in pairs(node) do
            local keyText = tostring(key)
            if type(value) == "string" then
                add(value, tostring(context or "") .. " " .. keyText)
            elseif type(value) == "table" then
                walk(value, tostring(context or "") .. " " .. keyText, depth + 1)
            end
        end
    end
    local constants = App.Game.Constants
    if type(constants) == "table" then
        walk(constants.NETWORK_MAP, "NETWORK_MAP", 0)
    end
    local network = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
    if network then
        for _, remote in ipairs(network:GetDescendants()) do
            local remoteText = string.lower(remote.Name)
            if (remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") or remote:IsA("RemoteFunction"))
                and string.find(remoteText, "carry", 1, true)
                and string.find(remoteText, "upgrade", 1, true)
            then
                add(remote.Name, "Network")
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(names, function(left, right)
        local leftRequest = string.find(string.lower(left), "request", 1, true) and 0 or 1
        local rightRequest = string.find(string.lower(right), "request", 1, true) and 0 or 1
        if leftRequest == rightRequest then
            return left < right
        end
        return leftRequest < rightRequest
    end)
    return names
end
function App.stepAutoCarryUpgrades(feature)
    local now = os.clock()
    if now < (App.Runtime.CarryUpgradeLastAttemptAt or 0) then
        return 0.35
    end
    local names = App.getCarryUpgradeNetworkNames()
    if #names == 0 then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Carry upgrade network endpoint not found")
        App.Runtime.CarryUpgradeLastAttemptAt = now + 3
        return 3
    end
    local data = App.getSaveData()
    local currentLevel = 0
    if type(data) == "table" then
        for _, key in ipairs({"CarryUpgradeLevel", "CarryLevel", "CarryCapacityLevel", "MaxCarryLevel", "CarryUpgrade"}) do
            local candidate = tonumber(data[key])
            if candidate then
                currentLevel = math.max(currentLevel, candidate)
            end
        end
    end
    local nextLevel = currentLevel + 1
    if nextLevel > 6 then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Maximum carry level reached")
        App.Runtime.CarryUpgradeLastAttemptAt = now + 10
        return 10
    end
    local costs = {100, 1000000, 500000000, 100000000000, 100000000000000, 7000000000000000}
    local cost = costs[nextLevel]
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Requesting carry upgrade " .. tostring(nextLevel) .. (cost and (" ($" .. App.formatMoney(cost) .. ")") or ""))
    local endpoint = App.Runtime.CarryUpgradeEndpoint
    local orderedNames = {}
    if endpoint then
        table.insert(orderedNames, endpoint)
    end
    for _, name in ipairs(names) do
        if name ~= endpoint then
            table.insert(orderedNames, name)
        end
    end
    local ok, result = App.callReplicatedNetwork(orderedNames)
    local usedEndpoint = orderedNames[1]
    if not ok then
        ok, result = App.invokeNetwork(usedEndpoint)
    end
    if not ok then
        ok, result = App.callReplicatedNetwork(orderedNames, nextLevel)
        if not ok then
            ok, result = App.invokeNetwork(usedEndpoint, nextLevel)
        end
    end
    App.Runtime.CarryUpgradeLastAttemptAt = now + 1.5
    if ok and result ~= false then
        App.Runtime.CarryUpgradeEndpoint = usedEndpoint
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Carry upgrade request sent")
    else
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, tostring(result or "Carry upgrade request rejected"))
    end
    return 1.5
end
function App.findAutoHitBat()
    local containers = {
        App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack"),
        App.LocalPlayer.Character,
    }
    for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
        if container then
            for _, item in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if item:IsA("Tool") then
                    local text = string.lower(table.concat({
                        tostring(item.Name),
                        tostring(item.ToolTip or ""),
                        tostring(item:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or ""),
                        tostring(item:GetAttribute("ItemName") or ""),
                    }, " "))
                    if string.find(text, "bat", 1, true) then
                        return item
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function App.findAutoHitBatTarget(range)
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and (character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character.PrimaryPart)
    if not root then
        return nil
    end
    local closest, closestDistance = nil, tonumber(range) or 25
    for _, player in ipairs(App.Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= App.LocalPlayer then
            local targetCharacter = player.Character
            local targetRoot = targetCharacter and (targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or targetCharacter.PrimaryPart)
            local humanoid = targetCharacter and targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if targetRoot and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                local distance = (targetRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if distance <= closestDistance then
                    closest, closestDistance = player, distance
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end
function App.stepAutoHitBat(feature)
    local configuredRange = math.clamp(tonumber(App.Runtime.AutoHitBatRange) or 25, 1, 100)
    local target = App.findAutoHitBatTarget(configuredRange)
    if not target then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for a nearby player")
        return 0.2
    end
    local bat = App.findAutoHitBat()
    if not bat then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Bat not found in backpack")
        return 1
    end
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid and bat.Parent ~= character then
        pcall(humanoid.EquipTool, humanoid, bat)
        task.wait(0.08)
    end
    if bat.Parent ~= character then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for bat equip")
        return 0.3
    end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now < (App.Runtime.AutoHitBatNextSwingAt or 0) then
        return 0.08
    end
    local activated = pcall(bat.Activate, bat)
    if not activated then
        local ok = App.callReplicatedNetwork({"Bat:Activate", "Bat.Activate"})
        if not ok then
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Bat swing failed")
            return 0.5
        end
    end
    local swingDelay = math.clamp(tonumber(App.Runtime.AutoHitBatDelay) or 0.35, 0.1, 1)
    App.Runtime.AutoHitBatNextSwingAt = now + swingDelay
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Swinging Bat near " .. tostring(target.Name))
    return 0.08
end
function App.isIncomePopupNode(instance)
    if not instance then
        return false
    end
    if instance.Name == "SyncedIncomeCashAttachment" or instance.Name == "SyncedIncomeCash" then
        return true
    end
    local attachment = instance:FindFirstAncestor("SyncedIncomeCashAttachment")
    local popup = instance:FindFirstAncestor("SyncedIncomeCash")
    return attachment ~= nil or popup ~= nil
end
function App.hideIncomePopupTree(root)
    if not root then
        return
    end
    local function hide(instance)
        if not App.isIncomePopupNode(instance) then
            return
        end
        local hidden = App.Runtime.IncomePopupHiddenInstances
        if instance:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            if hidden[instance] == nil then
                hidden[instance] = {Kind = "Enabled", Value = instance.Enabled}
            end
            instance.Enabled = false
        elseif instance:IsA("GuiObject") then
            if hidden[instance] == nil then
                hidden[instance] = {Kind = "Visible", Value = instance.Visible}
            end
            instance.Visible = false
        end
    end
    hide(root)
    for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        hide(descendant)
    end
end
function App.sweepIncomePopups()
    local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if not terrain then
        return
    end
    for _, descendant in ipairs(terrain:GetDescendants()) do
        if App.isIncomePopupNode(descendant) then
            App.hideIncomePopupTree(descendant)
        end
    end
end
function App.stepHideIncomePopup(feature)
    local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if not terrain then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for popup container")
        return 1
    end
    if not App.Runtime.IncomePopupConnection then
        App.Runtime.IncomePopupConnection = terrain.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            if App.Runtime.Enabled.HideIncomePopup then
                App.hideIncomePopupTree(descendant)
            end
        end)
    end
    App.sweepIncomePopups()
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Income popups hidden")
    return 0.35
end
function App.stopHideIncomePopup()
    if App.Runtime.IncomePopupConnection then
        pcall(function() App.Runtime.IncomePopupConnection:Disconnect() end)
        App.Runtime.IncomePopupConnection = nil
    end
    for instance, state in pairs(App.Runtime.IncomePopupHiddenInstances or {}) do
        if instance and instance.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                if state.Kind == "Enabled" then
                    instance.Enabled = state.Value
                elseif state.Kind == "Visible" then
                    instance.Visible = state.Value
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    App.Runtime.IncomePopupHiddenInstances = {}
end
function App.serverHop()
    local ok, err = pcall(
        App.Services.TeleportService.Teleport,
        App.Services.TeleportService,
        game.PlaceId,
        App.LocalPlayer
    )
    return ok, err
end

function App.stepAutoServerHop(feature, version)
    if not App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoServerHop then return App.Config.ServerHopDelay end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Rejoining server")
    App.serverHop()
    return App.Config.ServerHopDelay
end

function App.startAutoServerHop()
    App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoServerHop = true
    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoServerHop = (App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoServerHop or 0) + 1
    local version = App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoServerHop
    local thread = task.spawn(function()
        while App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoServerHop
            and App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoServerHop == version
            and not App.Runtime.Unloading
        do
            task.wait(App.Config.ServerHopDelay)
            if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoServerHop then
                App.serverHop()
            end
        end
    end)
    App.Runtime.ServerHopThread = thread
end

function App.stopAutoServerHop()
    App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoServerHop = false
    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoServerHop = (App.Runtime.WorkerVersion.AutoServerHop or 0) + 1
    if type(App.Runtime.ServerHopThread) == "thread" and task.cancel then
        pcall(task.cancel, App.Runtime.ServerHopThread)
    end
    App.Runtime.ServerHopThread = nil
end

function App.stepManualCarryReturn(feature, version)
    if not App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying or App.Runtime.StealTripPhase ~= nil then
        return 0.15
    end
    local uid = App.Runtime.CarryState.Uid
    local home, homeError = App.getPlotReturnPosition()
    if typeof(home) ~= "Vector3" then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, homeError or "Your base is unavailable")
        return 0.5
    end
    App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = "ManualReturning"
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Returning carried egg to base")
    local returned, returnError = App.directTravelTo(feature, version, home, true)
    if returned then
        local deadline = os.clock() + 1.5
        while App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying and os.clock() < deadline do
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        if App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying and uid then
            App.ensureAreaEggReleased(uid)
        end
    else
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, returnError or "Could not return to base")
    end
    App.Runtime.StealTripPhase = nil
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Monitoring manual carries")
    return 0.15
end

function App.getReplicatedNetworkRemote(names)
    local network = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
    if not network then
        return nil
    end
    if type(names) == "string" then
        names = {names}
    end
    if type(names) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    for _, name in ipairs(names) do
        local remote = network:FindFirstChild(tostring(name), true)
        if remote and (remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") or remote:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then
            return remote
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function App.callReplicatedNetwork(names, ...)
    local remote = App.getReplicatedNetworkRemote(names)
    if not remote then
        return false, "Network remote was not found"
    end
    local args = table.pack(...)
    if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        local ok, result = pcall(remote.FireServer, remote, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
        return ok, result
    end
    local ok, result = pcall(remote.InvokeServer, remote, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
    if not ok then
        return false, result
    end
    if result == false then
        return false, "Network request was rejected"
    end
    return true, result
end
function App.callTradingNetwork(key, ...)
    local constants = App.Game.Constants
    local map = constants and constants.NETWORK_MAP and constants.NETWORK_MAP.Trading
    local fallback = {
        REQUEST = "Server: Trading: Request",
        REJECT = "Server: Trading: Reject",
        DECLINE = "Server: Trading: Decline",
        SET_READY = "Server: Trading: Set Ready",
        SET_ITEM = "Server: Trading: Set Item",
        SET_CONFIRMED = "Server: Trading: Set Confirmed",
    }
    local names = {}
    if type(map) == "table" and type(map[key]) == "string" then
        table.insert(names, map[key])
    end
    if fallback[key] then
        table.insert(names, fallback[key])
    end
    return App.callReplicatedNetwork(names, ...)
end
function App.stepAutoSkipHatchAnimation(feature, version)
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    if type(eggCmds) ~= "table" then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Egg commands are unavailable")
        return 0.5
    end
    local recordsOk, records = App.callModuleMethod(
        eggCmds,
        "GetOwnerRuntimeRecords",
        App.LocalPlayer.UserId
    )
    if not recordsOk or type(records) ~= "table" then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for placed eggs")
        return 0.5
    end
    local attempted = 0
    local completed = 0
    for uid, record in pairs(records) do
        if type(record) == "table" and record.Placement ~= nil then
            local uidString = tostring(uid)
            local isHatching = App.Runtime.Hatching[uidString] == true
            local ready = false
            local readyOk, readyResult = App.callModuleMethod(eggCmds, "IsLocalEggReady", uidString)
            if readyOk and readyResult == true then
                ready = true
            end
            if isHatching or ready then
                attempted = attempted + 1
                local renderer = App.Game.Modules.PlacedEggRenderer
                if not renderer then
                    local rendererOk, rendererModule = App.requirePath({"Library", "Client", "Eggs", "PlacedEggRenderer"}, "PlacedEggRenderer")
                    if rendererOk then
                        renderer = rendererModule
                        App.Game.Modules.PlacedEggRenderer = rendererModule
                    end
                end
                if renderer then
                    App.callModuleMethod(renderer, "RequestSkipGrowthForLocalEgg", uidString)
                    App.callModuleMethod(renderer, "ActivateLocalEgg", uidString)
                end
                local completeOk, completeResult = App.callModuleMethod(
                    eggCmds,
                    "RequestCompleteHatchEgg",
                    uidString
                )
                if completeOk and completeResult == true then
                    completed = completed + 1
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if attempted == 0 then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "No active hatch animation")
    else
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Hatch skip requested " .. tostring(completed) .. "/" .. tostring(attempted))
    end
    return 0.15
end

        "GuardTutorial: RequestSyncProgress",

    if not ok then

    end
    if ok then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Tutorial progress synchronized")
    else
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, result or "Tutorial remote unavailable")
    end
    return 3
end

        "GroupReward: ClaimReward",

end
function App.getInventoryRecordsForAutomation()
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then
        return nil, dataError
    end
    local inventory = data.Inventory
    if type(inventory) ~= "table" then
        return nil, "Backpack inventory is unavailable"
    end
    return inventory
end
function App.isInventoryRecordFavorite(record)
    return type(record) == "table"
        and (record.IsFavorite == true or record.Favorite == true or record["Jadikan Favorit"] == true)
end
function App.getLiveBackpackAssetRecords()
    local records = {}
    local containers = {
        App.LocalPlayer and App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack"),
        App.LocalPlayer and App.LocalPlayer.Character,
    }
    for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
        if container then
            for _, tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                    local uid = tool:GetAttribute("UID")
                    local category = tool:GetAttribute("Category") or tool:GetAttribute("AssetCategory")
                    local itemType = tool:GetAttribute("ItemType")
                    if type(uid) == "string" and uid ~= "" and (itemType == "Asset" or category ~= nil) then
                        local record = {}
                        local itemData = tool:GetAttribute("ItemData")
                        if type(itemData) == "table" then
                            for key, value in pairs(itemData) do record[key] = value end
                        end
                        record.Category = record.Category or category
                        record.AssetCategory = record.AssetCategory or category
                        record.Rarity = record.Rarity or tool:GetAttribute("Rarity")
                        record.IsFavorite = tool:GetAttribute("Favorite") == true
                        records[uid] = record
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return records
end
function App.getFavoriteInventory()
    local live = App.getLiveBackpackAssetRecords()
    if App.countMap(live) > 0 then return live end
    return App.getInventoryRecordsForAutomation()
end
function App.getBackpackEggRarityRows()
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then return nil, dataError end
    local inventory = data.EggInventory
    if type(inventory) ~= "table" then return {}, nil end
    local areas = App.getIndexAreaDirectory()
    local rows = {}
    for uid, record in pairs(inventory) do
        if type(record) == "table" and record.Placement == nil then
            local category = record.AssetCategory or record.Category or record.ItemId
            local asset = App.getAssetDirectoryEntry(category)
            if asset and asset.Egg then
                local areaId
                for candidateAreaId, areaConfig in pairs(areas or {}) do
                    for _, dropEntry in ipairs(type(areaConfig) == "table" and areaConfig.DropTable or {}) do
                        if type(dropEntry) == "table" and tostring(dropEntry[1]) == tostring(category) then
                            areaId = candidateAreaId
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if areaId then break end
                end
                local areaConfig = areaId and areas[areaId]
                local eggName = asset.Egg.DisplayName
                    or (areaConfig and (tostring(areaConfig.DisplayName) .. " Egg"))
                    or "Egg"
                local rarity = App.getInventoryAssetRarity(record)
                local icon = asset.Egg.Icon or asset.Icon
                if type(icon) == "number" then icon = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(icon) end
                table.insert(rows, {
                    Uid = tostring(uid),
                    Name = tostring(eggName),
                    Rarity = tostring(rarity or "Unknown"),
                    Icon = icon,
                })
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(rows, function(left, right)
        if left.Rarity == right.Rarity then return left.Name < right.Name end
        return left.Rarity < right.Rarity
    end)
    return rows
end
function App.getSakuraState()
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then return nil, dataError end
    local state = type(data.Sakura) == "table" and data.Sakura or {}
    return state, nil
end
function App.getSakuraCrystalCount()
    local data = App.getSaveData()
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return 0 end
    local sources = {data.Currency, data.Currencies, data.Resources, data}
    for _, source in ipairs(sources) do
        if type(source) == "table" then
            local value = source.SakuraCrystals
            if type(value) == "table" then
                value = value.Amount or value.Value or value.Count
            end
            value = tonumber(value)
            if value then return math.max(0, value) end
        end
    end
    return 0
end
function App.getSakuraEggOptions()
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if not data then return {}, dataError end
    local inventory = type(data.EggInventory) == "table" and data.EggInventory or {}
    local areas = App.getIndexAreaDirectory and App.getIndexAreaDirectory() or {}
    local options = {}
    local lookup = {}
    local function getWeight(record)
        local value = record.Weight or record.WeightKg or record.KG or record.SizeKg or record.EggWeight
        local nestedEgg = type(record.Egg) == "table" and record.Egg or nil
        value = value or (nestedEgg and (nestedEgg.Weight or nestedEgg.WeightKg or nestedEgg.KG or nestedEgg.SizeKg))
        if type(value) == "table" then value = value.Value or value.Amount end
        value = tonumber(value)
        return value and math.max(0, value) or nil
    end
    local function getAreaName(category)
        for areaId, areaConfig in pairs(areas) do
            if type(areaConfig) == "table" then
                for _, drop in ipairs(areaConfig.DropTable or {}) do
                    local dropCategory = type(drop) == "table" and (drop[1] or drop.Category or drop.AssetCategory)
                    if tostring(dropCategory or "") == tostring(category) then
                        local display = tostring(areaConfig.DisplayName or areaId)
                        if not display:lower():find("egg", 1, true) then display = display .. " Egg" end
                        return display, tostring(areaId)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return tostring(category or "Unknown"), nil
    end
    for uid, record in pairs(inventory) do
        if type(record) == "table" and record.Placement == nil then
            local category = record.AssetCategory or record.Category or record.ItemId or record.Id
            if category then
                local areaName, areaId = getAreaName(category)
                local weight = getWeight(record)
                local label = string.format("%s - %s kg", areaName, weight and string.format("%.1f", weight) or "?")
                local uniqueLabel = label
                local serial = 2
                while lookup[uniqueLabel] do
                    uniqueLabel = label .. " (" .. tostring(serial) .. ")"
                    serial = serial + 1
                end
                lookup[uniqueLabel] = {
                    Uid = tostring(uid),
                    AreaId = areaId,
                    AreaName = areaName,
                    Weight = weight,
                    Record = record,
                    Category = tostring(category),
                }
                table.insert(options, uniqueLabel)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(options)
    return options, lookup
end
function App.getSakuraPredictionText()
    local state = App.getSakuraState()
    if type(state) ~= "table" then return "Sakura prediction unavailable" end
    local sakura = App.Game.Modules.Sakura
    local deposited = tonumber(state.Deposited) or 0
    local cost = sakura and tonumber(sakura.Incubator and sakura.Incubator.Cost) or 1000
    local maxCharge = sakura and tonumber(sakura.Incubator and sakura.Incubator.MaxChargePercent) or 150
    local charge = math.clamp(deposited / math.max(cost, 1) * 100, 0, maxCharge)
    local luckBoost = tonumber(state.LuckBoost) or 0
    local specialChance = 0
    if sakura and type(sakura.GetSpecialChance) == "function" then
        local ok, value = pcall(sakura.GetSpecialChance, charge, luckBoost)
        if ok then specialChance = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 0, 0, 100) end
    end
    local egg = type(state.Egg) == "table" and state.Egg.Egg or nil
    local selected = App.Runtime.Sakura.OptionLookup[App.Runtime.Sakura.SelectedLabel or ""]
    local eggName = type(egg) == "table" and (egg.DisplayName or egg.Name or egg.AssetCategory)
        or (selected and App.Runtime.Sakura.SelectedLabel)
        or "No egg inserted"
    return string.format(
        "%s\nSakura: %.2f%% | GreatBloom: %.2f%%\nCharge: %.1f%% | Luck: %d",
        tostring(eggName),
        100 - specialChance,
        specialChance,
        charge,
        luckBoost
    )
end
function App.refreshSakuraDisplay()
    local runtime = App.Runtime.Sakura
    if runtime.CrystalLabel then
        pcall(function()
            runtime.CrystalLabel:SetText("Sakura Crystal\nAmount remaining - " .. App.formatIncome(App.getSakuraCrystalCount()))
        end)
    end
    if runtime.PredictionLabel then
        pcall(function() runtime.PredictionLabel:SetText(App.getSakuraPredictionText()) end)
    end
    local options, lookup = App.getSakuraEggOptions()
    runtime.Options = options
    runtime.OptionLookup = lookup
    if runtime.EggDropdown and type(runtime.EggDropdown.SetValues) == "function" then
        pcall(runtime.EggDropdown.SetValues, runtime.EggDropdown, #options > 0 and options or {"No backpack eggs"})
    end
    return options
end
function App.setGuardVisualHidden(hidden)
    hidden = hidden == true
    local runtime = App.Runtime
    runtime.GuardVisualHidden = hidden
    if not hidden then
        for object, backup in pairs(runtime.GuardVisualBackup or {}) do
            if object and object.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    if backup.Kind == "Part" then object.LocalTransparencyModifier = backup.Value end
                    if backup.Kind == "Decal" then object.Transparency = backup.Value end
                    if backup.Kind == "Gui" then object.Enabled = backup.Value end
                end)
            end
            runtime.GuardVisualBackup[object] = nil
        end
        if runtime.GuardVisualThread and task.cancel then pcall(task.cancel, runtime.GuardVisualThread) end
        runtime.GuardVisualThread = nil
        return true
    end
    if runtime.GuardVisualThread then return true end
    local function hideGuard(guard)
        if not guard or not guard.Parent then return end
        for _, object in ipairs(guard:GetDescendants()) do
            local backup
            if object:IsA("BasePart") then
                backup = {Kind = "Part", Value = object.LocalTransparencyModifier}
                object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
                backup = {Kind = "Decal", Value = object.Transparency}
                object.Transparency = 1
            elseif object:IsA("BillboardGui") or object:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                backup = {Kind = "Gui", Value = object.Enabled}
                object.Enabled = false
            end
            if backup and runtime.GuardVisualBackup[object] == nil then
                runtime.GuardVisualBackup[object] = backup
            end
        end
    end
    runtime.GuardVisualThread = task.spawn(function()
        while runtime.GuardVisualHidden and not runtime.Unloading do
            local objects = App.Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
            local areas = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Areas")
            local guardAreas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
            if guardAreas then
                for _, area in ipairs(guardAreas:GetChildren()) do hideGuard(area:FindFirstChild("Guard")) end
            end
            task.wait(1)
        end
        runtime.GuardVisualThread = nil
    end)
    return true
end
function App.stepAutoSakuraIncubator(feature, version)
    local state, stateError = App.getSakuraState()
    local sakura = App.Game.Modules.Sakura
    local runtime = App.Runtime.Sakura
    if not state then return 2 end
    if state.Unlocked ~= true then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Sakura Incubator is sealed")
        return 2
    end
    if runtime.Busy then return 0.5 end
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    if nowClock - (runtime.LastUpdateAt or 0) >= 1.5 then
        pcall(App.refreshSakuraDisplay)
    end
    if nowClock - (runtime.LastActionAt or 0) < (tonumber(App.Config.SakuraActionCooldown) or 0.8) then
        return 0.5
    end
    local selected = runtime.OptionLookup[runtime.SelectedLabel or ""]
    if not selected or not selected.Uid then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Select a backpack egg")
        return 2
    end
    runtime.Busy = true
    runtime.LastActionAt = nowClock
    local function finish()
        runtime.Busy = false
        runtime.LastUpdateAt = os.clock()
    end
    local currentEgg = state.Egg
    local ok, result
    if currentEgg == false or currentEgg == nil then
        ok, result = App.invokeNetwork("Sakura: InsertEgg", selected.Uid)
        finish()
        if ok then
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Inserted " .. tostring(runtime.SelectedLabel))
            return 1
        end
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, result or "Sakura egg insertion failed")
        return 2
    end
    local cost = sakura and sakura.Incubator and tonumber(sakura.Incubator.Cost) or 1000
    local deposited = tonumber(state.Deposited) or 0
    if deposited < cost then
        ok, result = App.invokeNetwork("Sakura: Deposit")
        finish()
        if ok then
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Charging Sakura Incubator")
            return 1
        end
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, result or "Sakura deposit failed")
        return 2
    end
    local charge = math.clamp(deposited / math.max(cost, 1) * 100, 0, 150)
    if charge >= 100 then
        ok, result = App.invokeNetwork("Sakura: Mutate")
        finish()
        if ok then
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Sakura mutation requested")
            return 2
        end
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, result or "Sakura mutation failed")
        return 2
    end
    finish()
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Charging " .. string.format("%.1f%%", charge))
    return 1
end
function App.destroyBackpackEggRarityGui()
    if App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityGui then
        pcall(function() App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityGui:Destroy() end)
    end
    App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityGui = nil
    App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityRows = nil
end
