function App.updateBackpackEggRarityGui()
    local gui = App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityGui
    local rows = App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityRows
    if not gui or not gui.Parent or type(rows) ~= "table" then return end
    local entries = App.getBackpackEggRarityRows()
    if type(entries) ~= "table" then entries = {} end
    for index, row in ipairs(rows) do
        local entry = entries[index]
        row.Root.Visible = entry ~= nil
        if entry then
            row.Name.Text = entry.Name
            row.Rarity.Text = entry.Rarity
            row.Rarity.TextColor3 = App.getRarityColour(entry.Rarity) or Color3.fromRGB(245, 220, 145)
            row.Image.Image = entry.Icon and tostring(entry.Icon) or "rbxassetid://6031075931"
        end
    end
end
function App.stepAutoESPEggBackpack(feature, version)
    if not App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityGui or not App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityGui.Parent then
        if not App.createBackpackEggRarityGui() then
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "PlayerGui is unavailable")
            return 1
        end
    end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now >= (App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityNextUpdateAt or 0) then
        App.Runtime.BackpackEggRarityNextUpdateAt = now + 1
        App.updateBackpackEggRarityGui()
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Showing egg rarity only")
    return 0.25
end
function App.stepAutoFavoriteSpecificRarity(feature, version)
    local inventory, inventoryError = App.getFavoriteInventory()
    if not inventory then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, inventoryError)
        return 2
    end
    local selected = App.Runtime.SelectedFavoriteRarities
    local matched, favorited = 0, 0
    for uid, record in pairs(inventory) do
        if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
            return 0
        end
        if type(record) == "table" then
            local rarity = App.getInventoryAssetRarity(record)
            if App.raritySelected(selected, rarity) and not App.isInventoryRecordFavorite(record) then
                matched = matched + 1
                local ok = App.callReplicatedNetwork({
                    "AssetInventory: SetFavorite",
                    "AssetInventory.SetFavorite",
                }, tostring(uid), true)
                if ok then
                    favorited = favorited + 1
                end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, string.format("Favorited %d of %d matching item(s)", favorited, matched))
    return 3
end
function App.unfavoriteAllNow()
    local inventory, inventoryError = App.getFavoriteInventory()
    if not inventory then
        return false, inventoryError
    end
    local changed = 0
    for uid, record in pairs(inventory) do
        if App.isInventoryRecordFavorite(record) then
            local ok = App.callReplicatedNetwork({
                "AssetInventory: SetFavorite",
                "AssetInventory.SetFavorite",
            }, tostring(uid), false)
            if ok then
                changed = changed + 1
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end
    return true, "Unfavorited " .. tostring(changed) .. " item(s)"
end
function App.resolveTradeTarget()
    local wanted = tostring(App.Runtime.TradeTargetName or ""):lower()
    if wanted == "" then
        return nil
    end
    for _, player in ipairs(App.Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= App.LocalPlayer
            and (player.Name:lower() == wanted or player.DisplayName:lower() == wanted)
        then
            return player
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function App.ensureTradeRequestListener()
    local existing = App.Runtime.TradeRequestConnection
    if existing then
        return true
    end
    local remote = App.getReplicatedNetworkRemote({"Trading: Request"})
    if not remote or not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        return false
    end
    App.Runtime.TradeRequestConnection = remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(App.guard(function(...)
        local arguments = table.pack(...)
        local sender = arguments[1]
        if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoDeclineTrade then
            App.callTradingNetwork("REJECT", sender)
        elseif App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoAcceptTrade then
            App.callTradingNetwork("REQUEST", sender)
        end
    end))
    return true
end
function App.stepAutoAcceptTrade(feature, version)
    if not App.ensureTradeRequestListener() then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Trade request remote unavailable")
    else
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for trade requests")
    end
    return 1
end
function App.stepAutoDeclineTrade(feature, version)
    if not App.ensureTradeRequestListener() then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Trade request remote unavailable")
    else
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Declining trade requests")
    end
    return 1
end
function App.stepAutoChooseTradePlayer(feature, version)
    local target = App.resolveTradeTarget()
    if not target then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Select a valid trade player")
        return 1
    end
    local ok, result = App.callTradingNetwork("REQUEST", target)
    if not ok then
        ok, result = App.callTradingNetwork("REQUEST", target.UserId)
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, ok and ("Trading with " .. target.Name) or tostring(result or "Trade request failed"))
    return 3
end
function App.getTradingCmds()
    if App.Game.Modules.TradingCmds ~= nil then
        return App.Game.Modules.TradingCmds
    end
    local ok, module = App.requirePath({"Library", "Client", "TradingCmds"}, "TradingCmds")
    App.Game.Modules.TradingCmds = ok and module or false
    return ok and module or nil
end
function App.callTradingCommand(method, ...)
    local commands = App.getTradingCmds()
    local callback = commands and commands[method]
    if type(callback) ~= "function" then
        return false, "Trading command is unavailable: " .. tostring(method)
    end
    local args = table.pack(...)
    local result = table.pack(pcall(callback, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)))
    if result[1] ~= true then
        return false, result[2]
    end
    if result[2] == false then
        return false, result[3] or "Trading request was rejected"
    end
    return true, result[2], result[3]
end
function App.stepAutoChooseTradeItem(feature, version)
    local category = App.Runtime.TradeSelectedCategory
    local itemId = App.Runtime.TradeSelectedItemId
    local quantity = math.max(1, tonumber(App.Runtime.TradeSelectedQuantity) or 1)
    if not category or itemId == nil then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Select a trade item")
        return 1
    end
    local ok, result = App.callTradingCommand("SetItem", category, itemId, quantity)
    if not ok then
        ok, result = App.callTradingNetwork("SET_ITEM", category, itemId, quantity)
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, ok and ("Selected " .. tostring(App.Runtime.TradeSelectedName or itemId)) or tostring(result or "Trade item selection failed"))
    return 1
end
function App.getIndexAreaDirectory()
    local module = App.Game.Modules.Areas
    if type(module) == "table" and type(module.Directory) == "table" then return module.Directory end
    local ok, required = pcall(function() return require(App.Services.ReplicatedStorage.Directory.Areas) end)
    if ok and type(required) == "table" then return required.Directory or required.Areas or required end
    return nil
end
function App.getIndexAssetDirectory()
    local module = App.Game.Modules.Assets
    if type(module) == "table" and type(module.Directory) == "table" then return module.Directory end
    local ok, required = pcall(function() return require(App.Services.ReplicatedStorage.Directory.Assets) end)
    if ok and type(required) == "table" then return required.Directory or required.Assets or required end
    return nil
end
function App.getIndexAreaEggInfo(areaId, areaConfig)
    local areas = App.getIndexAreaDirectory()
    local assets = App.getIndexAssetDirectory() or {}
    areaConfig = areaConfig or (type(areas) == "table" and areas[areaId] or nil)
    local areaName = type(areaConfig) == "table" and tostring(areaConfig.DisplayName or areaId) or tostring(areaId or "")
    local candidates = {}
    local seenCandidates = {}
    local function addCandidate(value)
        if type(value) == "string" or type(value) == "number" then
            local text = tostring(value)
            if text ~= "" and not seenCandidates[text] then
                seenCandidates[text] = true
                table.insert(candidates, text)
            end
        elseif type(value) == "table" then
            for _, key in ipairs({"AssetCategory", "Category", "AssetId", "Id", "Name", "DisplayName", "EggName", "EggId", "EggAssetId"}) do
                addCandidate(value[key])
            end
        end
    end
    if type(areaConfig) == "table" then
        addCandidate(areaConfig.EggId)
        addCandidate(areaConfig.EggAssetId)
        addCandidate(areaConfig.EggName)
        addCandidate(areaConfig.EggCategory)
        addCandidate(areaConfig.Egg)
    end
    local function compact(value)
        return string.lower(tostring(value or "")):gsub("[^%w]", "")
    end
    local areaCompact = compact(areaId)
    local nameCompact = compact(areaName)
    local function areaReferenceMatches(value)
        if type(value) == "string" or type(value) == "number" then
            local compactValue = compact(value)
            return compactValue == areaCompact or compactValue == nameCompact
        end
        if type(value) == "table" then
            for _, key in ipairs({"AreaId", "Area", "AreaName", "AreaIdName"}) do
                if areaReferenceMatches(value[key]) then return true end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local category
    local displayName
    local icon
    for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
        local config = assets[candidate]
        if type(config) == "table" then
            category = tostring(candidate)
            displayName = tostring(config.DisplayName or candidate)
            icon = App.getIndexAssetIcon(config)
            break
        end
    end
    if not category and type(assets) == "table" then
        for assetId, config in pairs(assets) do
            if type(config) == "table" then
                local display = tostring(config.DisplayName or assetId)
                local assetEgg = config.Egg
                local candidateMatch = false
                for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
                    if compact(assetId) == compact(candidate) or compact(display) == compact(candidate) then
                        candidateMatch = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                local areaMatch = areaReferenceMatches(assetEgg)
                    or areaReferenceMatches(config.AreaId)
                    or areaReferenceMatches(config.Area)
                local displayMatch = compact(display):find(nameCompact, 1, true) ~= nil
                    and compact(display):find("egg", 1, true) ~= nil
                if candidateMatch or areaMatch or displayMatch then
                    category = tostring(assetId)
                    displayName = display
                    icon = App.getIndexAssetIcon(config) or App.getIndexAssetIcon(assetEgg)
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not category then
        category = candidates[1] or (areaName ~= "" and areaName .. " Egg" or nil)
    end
    if not displayName then
        displayName = candidates[1] or (areaName ~= "" and areaName .. " Egg" or category)
    end
    return {
        AreaId = areaId and tostring(areaId) or nil,
        Name = tostring(displayName or category or ""),
        Category = category and tostring(category) or nil,
        Icon = icon,
    }
end
function App.getAreaEggOptions()
    local directory = App.getIndexAreaDirectory()
    local options = {}
    local lookup = {}
    if type(directory) == "table" then
        for areaId, config in pairs(directory) do
            if type(config) == "table" then
                local info = App.getIndexAreaEggInfo(areaId, config)
                local label = tostring(info.Name or areaId)
                if label ~= "" and not lookup[label] then
                    lookup[label] = info
                    table.insert(options, label)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(options)
    App.Runtime.SelectedEggOptions = options
    App.Runtime.SelectedEggOptionLookup = lookup
    return options, lookup
end
function App.setSelectedAreaEgg(info)
    if type(info) ~= "table" then return false end
    App.Runtime.SelectedEggName = tostring(info.Name or info.Category or "")
    App.Runtime.SelectedEggAreaId = info.AreaId and tostring(info.AreaId) or nil
    App.Runtime.SelectedEggCategory = info.Category and tostring(info.Category) or nil
    return App.Runtime.SelectedEggName ~= ""
end
function App.getSelectedAreaEggInfo()
    local runtime = App.Runtime
    if runtime.SelectedEggAreaId then
        return App.getIndexAreaEggInfo(runtime.SelectedEggAreaId)
    end
    if runtime.SelectedEggName or runtime.SelectedEggCategory then
        return {
            AreaId = runtime.SelectedEggAreaId,
            Name = runtime.SelectedEggName,
            Category = runtime.SelectedEggCategory,
        }
    end
    return nil
end
function App.recordMatchesAreaEgg(record, info)
    if type(info) ~= "table" then return true end
    if type(record) ~= "table" then return false end
    local assets = App.getIndexAssetDirectory() or {}
    local values = {
        record.AssetCategory,
        record.Category,
        record.AssetId,
        record.Name,
        record.EggName,
    }
    local function compact(value)
        return string.lower(tostring(value or "")):gsub("[^%w]", "")
    end
    local targetCategory = compact(info.Category)
    local targetName = compact(info.Name)
    for _, value in ipairs(values) do
        local current = compact(value)
        if current ~= "" and (current == targetCategory or current == targetName) then
            return true
        end
    end
    local category = tostring(record.AssetCategory or record.Category or record.AssetId or record.Name or "")
    local config = assets[category]
    if type(config) == "table" then
        if compact(config.DisplayName) == targetCategory or compact(config.DisplayName) == targetName then
            return true
        end
        local eggReference = config.Egg
        if type(eggReference) == "string" or type(eggReference) == "number" then
            local current = compact(eggReference)
            if current == targetCategory or current == targetName then return true end
        elseif type(eggReference) == "table" then
            for _, key in ipairs({"AssetCategory", "Category", "AssetId", "Id", "Name", "DisplayName", "EggName"}) do
                local current = compact(eggReference[key])
                if current ~= "" and (current == targetCategory or current == targetName) then return true end
            end
            for _, key in ipairs({"AreaId", "Area", "AreaName"}) do
                if tostring(eggReference[key] or "") == tostring(info.AreaId or "") then return true end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
function App.findAreaEggUid(info, preferredUid, unplacedOnly)
    local data, dataError = App.getSaveData()
    if type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.EggInventory) ~= "table" then
        return nil, dataError or "Egg inventory is unavailable"
    end
    local function matches(uid, record)
        if type(record) ~= "table" then return false end
        if unplacedOnly and record.Placement ~= nil then return false end
        return App.recordMatchesAreaEgg(record, info)
    end
    if preferredUid and matches(tostring(preferredUid), data.EggInventory[tostring(preferredUid)]) then
        return tostring(preferredUid)
    end
    local uids = {}
    for uid, record in pairs(data.EggInventory) do
        if matches(uid, record) then table.insert(uids, tostring(uid)) end
    end
    table.sort(uids)
    return uids[1]
end
function App.getIndexAreaOptions()
    local directory = App.getIndexAreaDirectory()
    local options = {}
    local lookup = {}
    if type(directory) == "table" then
        for areaId, config in pairs(directory) do
            if type(config) == "table" then
                local label = tostring(config.DisplayName or areaId)
                lookup[label] = tostring(areaId)
                table.insert(options, label)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(options)
    App.Runtime.IndexSystem.AreaOptions = lookup
    return options
end
function App.getIndexAssetIcon(config)
    if type(config) ~= "table" then return nil end
    local icon = config.Icon or config.AssetId or config.Model or config.TextureId
    if type(icon) == "table" then icon = icon.Image or icon.Icon or icon.AssetId or icon.TextureId end
    if type(icon) == "number" then icon = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(icon) end
    if type(icon) ~= "string" or icon == "" or icon == "N/A" then return nil end
    if icon:match("^%d+$") then icon = "rbxassetid://" .. icon end
    return icon
end
function App.getIndexAreaEggIcon(areaId, areaConfig)
    local assets = App.getIndexAssetDirectory()
    if type(assets) == "table" and type(areaConfig) == "table" then
        local explicit = {
            areaConfig.EggId,
            areaConfig.EggAssetId,
            type(areaConfig.Egg) == "string" and areaConfig.Egg or nil,
        }
        for _, assetId in ipairs(explicit) do
            local config = assetId and assets[assetId]
            local icon = App.getIndexAssetIcon(config)
            if icon then return icon end
        end
    end
    local areaName = type(areaConfig) == "table" and tostring(areaConfig.DisplayName or areaId) or tostring(areaId)
    local areaText = string.lower(tostring(areaId))
    local nameText = string.lower(areaName)
    if type(assets) == "table" then
        for assetId, config in pairs(assets) do
            if type(config) == "table" then
                local display = string.lower(tostring(config.DisplayName or assetId))
                local egg = config.Egg
                local eggArea = type(egg) == "table" and (egg.AreaId or egg.Area or egg.AreaName)
                if tostring(eggArea or "") == tostring(areaId)
                    or (display:find(nameText, 1, true) and display:find("egg", 1, true))
                    or (string.lower(tostring(assetId)):find(areaText, 1, true) and string.lower(tostring(assetId)):find("egg", 1, true))
                then
                    local icon = App.getIndexAssetIcon(config) or App.getIndexAssetIcon(egg)
                    if icon then return icon end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local areaIcon = App.getIndexAssetIcon(areaConfig)
    return areaIcon
end
function App.getIndexAreaEntries(areaId)
    local areas = App.getIndexAreaDirectory()
    local assets = App.getIndexAssetDirectory()
    local areaConfig = type(areas) == "table" and areas[areaId] or nil
    local save = App.getSaveData()
    local index = type(save) == "table" and type(save.Index) == "table" and save.Index or {}
    local entries = {}
    if type(areaConfig) ~= "table" or type(assets) ~= "table" then return entries, areaConfig end
    for _, drop in ipairs(areaConfig.DropTable or {}) do
        local category = type(drop) == "table" and (drop[1] or drop.Category or drop.AssetId) or nil
        local weight = type(drop) == "table" and tonumber(drop[2] or drop.DropWeight or drop.Weight) or 0
        local config = category and assets[category] or nil
        if category and weight > 0 and type(config) == "table" and config.DontRoll ~= true then
            local rarity = type(config.Rarity) == "table" and config.Rarity or {}
            table.insert(entries, {
                Category = tostring(category),
                Name = tostring(config.DisplayName or category),
                Rarity = tostring(rarity._id or rarity.DisplayName or "Unknown"),
                RarityNumber = tonumber(rarity.RarityNumber) or 0,
                DropWeight = weight,
                Icon = App.getIndexAssetIcon(config),
                Discovered = index[category] == true,
            })
        end
    end
    table.sort(entries, function(a, b)
        if a.RarityNumber == b.RarityNumber then
            if a.DropWeight == b.DropWeight then return a.Category < b.Category end
            return a.DropWeight > b.DropWeight
        end
        return a.RarityNumber < b.RarityNumber
    end)
    return entries, areaConfig
end
function App.getIndexEggUid(areaId, areaConfig)
    local info = App.getIndexAreaEggInfo(areaId, areaConfig)
    local preferredUid = App.Runtime.IndexSystem and App.Runtime.IndexSystem.PlacementUid
    local uid = App.findAreaEggUid(info, preferredUid, true)
    return uid, info and (info.Category or info.Name) or nil
end
function App.getIndexUndiscovered(entries)
    local result = {}
    for _, entry in ipairs(entries or {}) do
        if not entry.Discovered then table.insert(result, entry) end
    end
    return result
end
function App.findIndexTargetEggUid(areaId, areaConfig)
    local uid, category = App.getIndexEggUid(areaId, areaConfig)
    return uid, category
end
function App.hatchIndexEgg(feature, version, uid)
    local eggCmds = App.Game.Modules.EggCmds
    if not eggCmds then return false, "Egg commands unavailable" end
    local renderer = App.Game.Modules.PlacedEggRenderer
    if not renderer then
        local rendererOk, rendererModule = App.requirePath({"Library", "Client", "Eggs", "PlacedEggRenderer"}, "PlacedEggRenderer")
        if rendererOk then
            renderer = rendererModule
            App.Game.Modules.PlacedEggRenderer = rendererModule
        end
    end
    if renderer then
        App.callModuleMethod(renderer, "RequestSkipGrowthForLocalEgg", uid)
        App.callModuleMethod(renderer, "ActivateLocalEgg", uid)
    end
    local ready = false
    local deadline = os.clock() + 180
    while os.clock() < deadline and App.featureAlive(feature, version) do
        local readyOk, readyResult = App.callModuleMethod(eggCmds, "IsLocalEggReady", uid)
        if readyOk and readyResult == true then ready = true break end
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
    if not ready then return false, "Egg did not become ready" end
    if renderer then App.callModuleMethod(renderer, "ActivateLocalEgg", uid) end
    local hatchOk, hatchResult = App.callModuleMethod(eggCmds, "RequestHatchEgg", uid)
    if not hatchOk or hatchResult ~= true then return false, "Hatch request failed" end
    task.wait(0.1)
    local completeOk, completeResult = App.callModuleMethod(eggCmds, "RequestCompleteHatchEgg", uid)
    if not completeOk or completeResult ~= true then return false, "Hatch completion failed" end
    if renderer then App.callModuleMethod(renderer, "RequestSkipGrowthForLocalEgg", uid) end
    return true
end
function App.stepIndexSystem(feature, version)
    local state = App.Runtime.IndexSystem
    local areaId = state.SelectedArea
    if not areaId then App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Choose an area"); return 1 end
    local entries, areaConfig = App.getIndexAreaEntries(areaId)
    local eggInfo = App.getIndexAreaEggInfo(areaId, areaConfig)
    App.setSelectedAreaEgg(eggInfo)
    state.Entries = entries
    if type(state.RefreshCallback) == "function" and os.clock() - state.LastRenderAt >= 1 then
        state.LastRenderAt = os.clock()
        pcall(state.RefreshCallback, areaId, entries, areaConfig)
    end
    local undiscovered = App.getIndexUndiscovered(entries)
    if not state.CompletionActive then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, tostring(#undiscovered) .. " undiscovered")
        return 1
    end
    if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoStealEgg or App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoPlaceEggs or App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoHatchEggs then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Waiting for other egg automation")
        return 1
    end
    if #undiscovered == 0 then
        state.CompletionActive = false
        state.CompletionTarget = nil
        state.PlacementUid = nil
        if type(state.NotifyCallback) == "function" and os.clock() - state.LastNotifyAt >= 2 then
            state.LastNotifyAt = os.clock()
            pcall(state.NotifyCallback, "Index System", "All Completed")
        end
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "All Completed")
        return 2
    end
    if state.CompletionCooldownAt and os.clock() < state.CompletionCooldownAt then return 0.5 end
    local target = undiscovered[1]
    state.CompletionTarget = target.Category
    local uid = App.findIndexTargetEggUid(areaId, areaConfig)
    if not uid then
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "No " .. tostring(areaConfig and areaConfig.DisplayName or areaId) .. " Egg in backpack")
        return 2
    end
    local data = App.getSaveData()
    local record = type(data) == "table" and data.EggInventory and data.EggInventory[uid] or nil
    if type(record) == "table" and record.Placement == nil then
        state.PlacementUid = uid
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Placing " .. tostring(areaConfig and areaConfig.DisplayName or areaId) .. " Egg")
        App.stepAutoPlaceEggs(feature, version)
        state.PlacementUid = nil
        local placedData = App.getSaveData()
        local placedRecord = type(placedData) == "table" and placedData.EggInventory and placedData.EggInventory[uid] or nil
        if type(placedRecord) ~= "table" or placedRecord.Placement == nil then return 1 end
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Hatching for " .. tostring(target.Name))
    local ok, result = App.hatchIndexEgg(feature, version, uid)
    if not ok then App.setFeatureDescription(feature, tostring(result or "Index hatch failed")); return 2 end
    state.CompletionCooldownAt = os.clock() + 5
    local after = App.getSaveData()
    local discovered = type(after) == "table" and type(after.Index) == "table" and after.Index[target.Category] == true
    if discovered and type(state.NotifyCallback) == "function" then pcall(state.NotifyCallback, "Index System", tostring(target.Name) .. " Discovered") end
    return 0.5
end

function App.clearServerAutoSell()
    if not App.Game.Ready then
        return false, "Not ready yet"
    end
    return App.syncAutoSellMap(nil, nil, {})
end

function App.stopFeature(feature, suppressServerCleanup)
    if feature == "AntiAFK" then
        return App.stopAntiAfk()
    end
    if feature == "ESP" then
        return App.stopEsp()
    end
    local thread = App.Runtime.Threads[feature]
    App.Runtime.PendingStarts[feature] = nil
    App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] = false
    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] = (App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] or 0) + 1
    local stoppedVersion = App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature]
    local cooperativeOnly = feature == "AutoHatchEggs"
        or feature == "AutoHatchAllEggs"
        or feature == "AutoStealEgg"
    local finishingSafeReturn = feature == "AutoStealEgg"
        and App.Runtime.StealTripPhase == "Returning"
    if not cooperativeOnly
        and not finishingSafeReturn
        and type(thread) == "thread"
        and task
        and type(task.cancel) == "function"
    then
        pcall(task.cancel, thread)
    end
    if not cooperativeOnly
        and not finishingSafeReturn
        and App.Runtime.Threads[feature] == thread
    then
        App.Runtime.Threads[feature] = nil
    end
    App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Disabled")
    return true
end

function App.startFeature(feature)
    if feature == "AntiAFK" then
        return App.startAntiAfk()
    end
    if feature == "ESP" then
        return App.startEsp()
    end
    if type(App.FeatureSteps[feature]) ~= "function" then
        App.reportFeatureError(feature, "Feature worker is unavailable")
        return nil
    end
    local existingThread = App.Runtime.Threads[feature]
    local cooperativeOnly = feature == "AutoHatchEggs"
        or feature == "AutoHatchAllEggs"
        or feature == "AutoStealEgg"
    if cooperativeOnly and type(existingThread) == "thread" then
        App.stopFeature(feature, true)
        App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] = true
        App.Runtime.PendingStarts[feature] = true
        App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Finishing the last action")
        return existingThread
    end
    App.stopFeature(feature, true)
    App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] = true
    App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] = (App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature] or 0) + 1

    local version = App.Runtime.WorkerVersion[feature]
    local thread



    thread = task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            App.setFeatureDescription(feature, "Starting up")
            while App.featureAlive(feature, version) do
                local delay = App.Config.WorkerIdleDelay
                local ready, initError = App.initGame()
                if not ready then
                    App.reportFeatureError(feature, initError)
                    delay = 2
                else
                    if not App.featureAlive(feature, version) then
                        break
                    end
                    local step = App.FeatureSteps[feature]
                    local stepOk, stepDelay = pcall(step, feature, version)
                    if not stepOk then
                        App.reportFeatureError(feature, stepDelay)
                        delay = 2
                    else
                        App.Runtime.FeatureErrorStreak[feature] = 0
                        if type(stepDelay) == "number" then
                            delay = math.max(0, stepDelay)
                        end
                    end
                end
                if not App.waitFeature(feature, version, delay) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if App.Runtime.Threads[feature] == thread then
                App.Runtime.Threads[feature] = nil
            end
            local shouldRestart = App.Runtime.PendingStarts[feature] == true
                and App.Runtime.Enabled[feature] == true
                and not App.Runtime.Unloading
            App.Runtime.PendingStarts[feature] = nil
            if shouldRestart then
                App.deferGuarded(App.startFeature, feature)
            end
        end)
    end)
    App.Runtime.Threads[feature] = thread
    return thread
end

    App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoSellAllEggs = false
    return App.stopFeature("AutoSellAllEggs")
end

function App.destroyScreenOverlay()
    local gui = App.Runtime.ScreenOverlayGui
    App.Runtime.ScreenOverlayGui = nil
    App.Runtime.ScreenOverlayMode = nil
    if gui then pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end) end
end
function App.setScreenOverlay(mode, enabled)
    mode = mode == "White" and "White" or "Black"
    if not enabled then
        if App.Runtime.ScreenOverlayMode == mode then
            App.destroyScreenOverlay()
        end
        return
    end
    App.destroyScreenOverlay()
    local playerGui = App.LocalPlayer and App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not playerGui then return end
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "SAEAFKScreenOverlay"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 10000
    local cover = Instance.new("Frame")
    cover.Name = "Cover"
    cover.BorderSizePixel = 0
    cover.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    cover.BackgroundColor3 = mode == "White" and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    cover.Parent = gui
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "SAEPremiumLabel"
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.Text = "SAE Premium " .. utf8.char(0xE000)
    label.TextColor3 = mode == "White" and Color3.new(0, 0, 0) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    label.TextSize = 24
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.Parent = cover
    gui.Parent = playerGui
    App.Runtime.ScreenOverlayGui = gui
    App.Runtime.ScreenOverlayMode = mode
end
function App.unloadAutomation()
    if App.Runtime.Unloading then return true end
    App.Runtime.Unloading = true
    App.stopRejoinMenu()
    for feature in pairs(App.FeatureSteps) do
        App.stopFeature(feature, true)
    end
    App.stopAntiAfk()
    if App.Runtime.DraggableStatusConnection then
        pcall(function() App.Runtime.DraggableStatusConnection:Disconnect() end)
        App.Runtime.DraggableStatusConnection = nil
    end
    App.setClientRenderedPetsHidden(false)
    App.Runtime.AutoRecarryDroppedEgg = false
    App.Runtime.AutoRecarryState.Uid = nil
    App.stopAntiGuard()
    App.stopAntiHitProtection()
    App.stopAntiRagdoll()
    App.stopAntiFling()
    App.stopUserBatESP()
    stopAutoSakuraIncubator()
    App.setGuardVisualHidden(false)
    App.stopEsp()
    App.stopIncomeOverlay()
    stopHideIncomePopup()
    stopAutoSellAllPets()
    stopAutoSellAllEggs()
    App.destroyBackpackEggRarityGui()
    App.stopTrapESP()
    App.stopAutoServerHop()
    App.stopMutationRefresh()
    App.Runtime.InstantPrompt = false
    App.Runtime.RemoveGameplayPrompt = false
    App.applyPromptSettings()
    if App.Runtime.TradeRequestConnection then
        pcall(function() App.Runtime.TradeRequestConnection:Disconnect() end)
        App.Runtime.TradeRequestConnection = nil
    end
    for root, connection in pairs(App.Runtime.PromptConnections) do
        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
        App.Runtime.PromptConnections[root] = nil
    end
    App.setFpsBooster(false)
    App.restorePerformanceMode()
    App.setStealWallsDisabled(false)
    App.destroyMediaPlayer()
    App.destroyScreenOverlay()
    App.Runtime.Enabled.BlackScreen = false
    App.Runtime.Enabled.WhiteScreen = false
    for _, connection in ipairs(App.Game.Connections) do
        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    App.Game.Connections = {}
    return true
end
local function stopAll() return App.unloadAutomation() end

function App.destroyMediaPlayer()
    local state = App.Runtime.MediaPlayer
    state.Enabled = false
    state.Token = (state.Token or 0) + 1
    if state.ProgressConnection then
        pcall(function() state.ProgressConnection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    state.ProgressConnection = nil
    if state.LoadConnection then
        pcall(function() state.LoadConnection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    state.LoadConnection = nil
    if state.LoadingTween then
        pcall(function() state.LoadingTween:Cancel() end)
    end
    state.LoadingTween = nil
    if state.StopPlayTween then
        pcall(function() state.StopPlayTween:Cancel() end)
    end
    state.StopPlayTween = nil
    if state.Gui then
        pcall(function() state.Gui:Destroy() end)
    end
    state.Gui = nil
    state.Video = nil
end

function App.setMediaPlaylist(value)
    local sources = {}
    for source in string.gmatch(tostring(value or ""), "[^,\n\r]+") do
        source = string.gsub(string.gsub(source, "^%s+", ""), "%s+$", "")
        if source ~= "" then
            table.insert(sources, string.match(source, "^%d+$") and "rbxassetid://" .. source or source)
        end
    end
    local state = App.Runtime.MediaPlayer
    state.Sources = sources
    state.Index = math.clamp(state.Index or 1, 1, math.max(#sources, 1))
end

-- Discover video entries from the game's authorized media module.
function App.scanGameMedia()
    local state = App.Runtime.MediaPlayer
    local sources = {}
    local seen = {}

    local function addSource(value)
        if typeof(value) ~= "string" then
            return
        end
        local source = value:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if source == "" then
            return
        end
        if source:match("^%d+$") then
            source = "rbxassetid://" .. source
        end
        if not seen[source] then
            seen[source] = true
            table.insert(sources, source)
        end
    end

    local function scanEntry(entry, depth)
        if depth > 4 or type(entry) ~= "table" then
            return
        end
        if entry.Kind == "Video" then
            addSource(entry.Video or entry.Asset or entry.AssetId or entry.Id or entry.Source)
        end
        for key, value in pairs(entry) do
            if type(value) == "table" then
                scanEntry(value, depth + 1)
            elseif key == "Video" or key == "VideoId" or key == "AssetId" or key == "Source" then
                addSource(value)
            end
        end
    end

    local replicatedStorage = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage
    local library = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Library")
    local client = library and library:FindFirstChild("Client")
    local controller = client and client:FindFirstChild("TreadmillVideoController")
    local mediaModule = controller and controller:FindFirstChild("Media")

    if mediaModule and mediaModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
        local ok, mediaEntries = pcall(require, mediaModule)
        if ok then
            scanEntry(mediaEntries, 0)
        end
    end

    state.Sources = sources
    state.Index = math.clamp(state.Index or 1, 1, math.max(#sources, 1))
    return #sources, sources
end

    gui.Name = "StealAnEggMediaPlayer"

    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.Parent = playerGui

    stroke.Parent = frame

    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
    header.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    header.Text = "   Media Player"
    header.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(238, 240, 246)
    header.TextSize = 14
    header.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    header.Parent = frame

    local close = Instance.new("TextButton")

    close.Position = UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 0)

    close.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

    close.TextSize = 22
    close.Parent = header
    local video = Instance.new("VideoFrame")
    video.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 9, 12)
    video.BorderSizePixel = 0
    video.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 48)
    video.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 427)
    local videoAspect = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
    videoAspect.AspectRatio = 9 / 16
    videoAspect.DominantAxis = Enum.DominantAxis.Width
    videoAspect.Parent = video
    video.Volume = 0.6
    video.Parent = frame
    Instance.new("UICorner", video).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
    local loading = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    loading.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 9, 12)
    loading.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    loading.BorderSizePixel = 0
    loading.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    loading.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    loading.Text = "Loading media"
    loading.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(197, 204, 222)
    loading.TextSize = 13
    loading.Visible = false
    loading.Parent = video
    Instance.new("UICorner", loading).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
    local loadingSpin = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    loadingSpin.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    loadingSpin.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    loadingSpin.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -20)
    loadingSpin.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32)
    loadingSpin.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    loadingSpin.Text = "◌"
    loadingSpin.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(116, 156, 255)
    loadingSpin.TextSize = 30
    loadingSpin.Parent = loading
    local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    empty.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    empty.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    empty.Text = "Scan the game media in Progression\nthen use the player controls."
    empty.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(145, 151, 168)
    empty.TextSize = 13
    empty.TextWrapped = true
    empty.Parent = video

    progressBack.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(49, 53, 65)
    progressBack.BorderSizePixel = 0
    progressBack.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 484)

    progressBack.Parent = frame

    progress.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(116, 156, 255)
    progress.BorderSizePixel = 0

    progress.Parent = progressBack

        item.BorderSizePixel = 0

    local play = button("Play", 66, 104)

    local indexLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    indexLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    indexLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(232, 500)
    indexLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -244, 0, 34)
    indexLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    indexLabel.Text = "No media"
    indexLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(157, 163, 181)
    indexLabel.TextSize = 11
    indexLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    indexLabel.Parent = frame
    local stopPlay = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    stopPlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    stopPlay.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    stopPlay.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    stopPlay.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 58)
    stopPlay.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    stopPlay.Text = "▶"
    stopPlay.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 247, 255)
    stopPlay.TextSize = 36
    stopPlay.TextTransparency = 1
    stopPlay.ZIndex = 5
    stopPlay.Parent = video

        if state.StopPlayTween then
            pcall(function() state.StopPlayTween:Cancel() end)
        end
        stopPlay.Text = playing and "Ⅱ" or "▶"
        stopPlay.TextTransparency = 0
        stopPlay.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(playing and 68 or 44, playing and 68 or 44)
        local enterTween = App.Services.TweenService:Create(stopPlay, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 58),
        })
        state.StopPlayTween = enterTween
        enterTween:Play()
        task.delay(0.32, function()
            if state.StopPlayTween == enterTween and stopPlay.Parent then
                local exitTween = App.Services.TweenService:Create(stopPlay, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    TextTransparency = 1,
                })
                state.StopPlayTween = exitTween
                exitTween:Play()
            end
        end)
    end
    local function setControlsEnabled(enabled)
        for _, control in ipairs({previous, play, nextButton}) do
            control.Active = enabled
            control.AutoButtonColor = enabled
            control.TextTransparency = enabled and 0 or 0.55
        end
    end
    local function stopLoading()
        if state.LoadingTween then
            pcall(function() state.LoadingTween:Cancel() end)
        end
        state.LoadingTween = nil
        loadingSpin.Rotation = 0
        loading.Visible = false
        setControlsEnabled(true)
    end
    local function showLoading()
        if state.LoadingTween then
            pcall(function() state.LoadingTween:Cancel() end)
        end
        loading.Visible = true
        loadingSpin.Rotation = 0
        local spinTween = App.Services.TweenService:Create(loadingSpin, TweenInfo.new(0.75, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1), {
            Rotation = 360,
        })
        state.LoadingTween = spinTween
        spinTween:Play()
        setControlsEnabled(false)
    end
    local function setPlaying(playing, animate)
        if #state.Sources == 0 then return end
        if playing then
            pcall(function() video:Play() end)
        else
            pcall(function() video:Pause() end)
        end
        play.Text = playing and "Pause" or "Play"
        if animate then
            showStopPlay(playing)
        end
    end
    local function loadCurrent(autoplay, direction)
        if #state.Sources == 0 then
            video:Pause()
            video.Video = ""
            empty.Visible = true
            indexLabel.Text = "No media"
            play.Text = "Play"
            stopLoading()
            return
        end
        state.Token = (state.Token or 0) + 1
        local token = state.Token
        if state.LoadConnection then
            pcall(function() state.LoadConnection:Disconnect() end)
        end
        state.LoadConnection = nil
        state.Index = math.clamp(state.Index, 1, #state.Sources)
        video:Pause()
        video.Video = state.Sources[state.Index]
        empty.Visible = false
        indexLabel.Text = tostring(state.Index) .. " / " .. tostring(#state.Sources)
        play.Text = "Play"
        progress.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
        showLoading()
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                App.Services.ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({video})
            end)
        end)
        local function finishLoad()
            if token ~= state.Token or not video.Parent then return end
            stopLoading()
            if autoplay then
                setPlaying(true, false)
            end
        end
        if video.IsLoaded then
            finishLoad()
        else
            state.LoadConnection = video.Loaded:Connect(function()
                if state.LoadConnection then
                    pcall(function() state.LoadConnection:Disconnect() end)
                end
                state.LoadConnection = nil
                finishLoad()
            end)
        end
        task.delay(6, finishLoad)
        if direction then
            local preloadIndex = state.Index + direction
            if preloadIndex < 1 then preloadIndex = #state.Sources end
            if preloadIndex > #state.Sources then preloadIndex = 1 end
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    App.Services.ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({state.Sources[preloadIndex]})
                end)
            end)
        end
    end
    local function selectPrevious()
        if #state.Sources == 0 then return end
        state.Index = state.Index <= 1 and #state.Sources or state.Index - 1
        loadCurrent(true, -1)
    end
    local function selectNext()
        if #state.Sources == 0 then return end
        state.Index = state.Index >= #state.Sources and 1 or state.Index + 1
        loadCurrent(true, 1)
    end
    previous.MouseButton1Click:Connect(selectPrevious)
    nextButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(selectNext)
    play.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if #state.Sources == 0 then return end
        if video.IsPlaying then
            setPlaying(false, true)
        else
            setPlaying(true, true)
        end
    end)
    video.Ended:Connect(selectNext)
    close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(App.destroyMediaPlayer)
    local input = App.Services.UserInputService
    local dragging, dragStart, frameStart = false, nil, nil
    header.InputBegan:Connect(function(event)
        if event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging, dragStart, frameStart = true, event.Position, frame.Position
        end
    end)
    input.InputChanged:Connect(function(event)
        if dragging and (event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = event.Position - dragStart
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(frameStart.X.Scale, frameStart.X.Offset + delta.X, frameStart.Y.Scale, frameStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
        end
    end)
    input.InputEnded:Connect(function(event)
        if event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end
    end)
    state.ProgressConnection = App.Services.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local length = video.TimeLength
        progress.Size = UDim2.new(length > 0 and math.clamp(video.TimePosition / length, 0, 1) or 0, 0, 1, 0)
    end)
    state.Gui, state.Video, state.Enabled = gui, video, true
    loadCurrent(false)
    return true
end

local Gift = {
    TargetUserId = nil,
    TargetName = "",
    SelectedTool = nil,
    FilterItem = "All Items",
    FilterMutation = "All Mutations",
    FilterRarity = "All Rarities",
    MinWeight = nil,
    MaxWeight = nil,
    IgnoreFavorites = true,
    OnlyFavorites = false,
    Delay = 0.35,
    AutoAll = false,
    AutoFiltered = false,
    AutoSelected = false,
    AutoAccept = false,
    OnlyAcceptTarget = false,
    LastRequest = nil,
    LoopVersion = 0,
    RequestConnection = nil,
    NetworkConnection = nil,
}
local function getGiftRemote(name)
    local network = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
    return network and network:FindFirstChild(name)
end
local function findGiftPlayer(value)
    local text = tostring(value or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if text == "" then return nil end
    local lowered = text:lower()
    for _, player in ipairs(App.Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player.Name:lower() == lowered or player.DisplayName:lower() == lowered then
            return player
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function resolveGiftTarget(value)
    local text = tostring(value or Gift.TargetName or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if text == "" then
        return nil, "Target username is empty"
    end
    local numeric = tonumber(text)
    if numeric then
        Gift.TargetUserId = numeric
        Gift.TargetName = text
        return numeric
    end
    local player = findGiftPlayer(text)
    if player then
        Gift.TargetUserId = player.UserId
        Gift.TargetName = player.Name
        return player.UserId
    end
    local ok, userId = pcall(App.Services.Players.GetUserIdFromNameAsync, App.Services.Players, text)
    if not ok or not userId then
        return nil, "Player could not be resolved"
    end
    Gift.TargetUserId = userId
    Gift.TargetName = text
    return userId
end
local function getGiftTools()
    local tools = {}
    local backpack = App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    for _, container in ipairs({backpack, character}) do
        if container then
            for _, item in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if item:IsA("Tool") then
                    table.insert(tools, item)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return tools
end
local function getToolValue(tool, names)
    for _, name in ipairs(names) do
        local attribute = tool:GetAttribute(name)
        if attribute ~= nil and tostring(attribute) ~= "" then return attribute end
        local child = tool:FindFirstChild(name)
        if child and child:IsA("ValueBase") then return child.Value end
    end
    return nil
end
local function getGiftRarity(tool, displayName)
    local rarity = getToolValue(tool, {"Rarity", "Tier", "ItemRarity", "RarityName"})
    if rarity ~= nil and tostring(rarity) ~= "" then return tostring(rarity) end
    local category = getToolValue(tool, {"Category", "AssetCategory"})
    local resolved = category and App.getAssetRarity(tostring(category))
    if resolved then return tostring(resolved) end
    local lower = tostring(displayName or tool.Name):lower()
    for _, candidate in ipairs(App.Runtime.RarityOrder) do
        if lower:find(tostring(candidate):lower(), 1, true) then return candidate end
    end
    return "Normal"
end
local function getGiftInfo(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return nil end
    local displayName = tostring(getToolValue(tool, {"DisplayName", "ItemName"}) or tool.Name)
    local mutation = getToolValue(tool, {"BaseMutation", "Mutations", "Mutation"}) or "Normal"
    local weight = tonumber(getToolValue(tool, {"Weight", "WeightKg", "KG"}) or 0) or 0
    return {
        Instance = tool,
        Name = tool.Name,
        DisplayName = displayName,
        Mutation = tostring(mutation),
        Rarity = getGiftRarity(tool, displayName),
        Weight = weight,
        Favorite = tool:GetAttribute("Favorite") == true,
    }
end
local function giftMatches(tool)
    local info = getGiftInfo(tool)
    if not info then return false end
    if Gift.IgnoreFavorites and info.Favorite then return false end
    if Gift.OnlyFavorites and not info.Favorite then return false end
    if Gift.FilterItem ~= "All Items" and Gift.FilterItem ~= "" then
        local itemFilter = tostring(Gift.FilterItem):lower()
        local itemName = tostring(info.Name):lower()
        local displayName = tostring(info.DisplayName):lower()
        if not itemName:find(itemFilter, 1, true) and not displayName:find(itemFilter, 1, true) then
            return false
        end
    end
    if Gift.FilterMutation ~= "All Mutations"
        and info.Mutation:lower() ~= tostring(Gift.FilterMutation):lower()
    then
        return false
    end
    if Gift.FilterRarity ~= "All Rarities" then
        local matchedRarity = false
        for part in tostring(Gift.FilterRarity):gmatch("[^,]+") do
            if info.Rarity:lower() == part:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""):lower() then
                matchedRarity = true
                break
            end
        end
        if not matchedRarity then return false end
    end
    if Gift.MinWeight and info.Weight < Gift.MinWeight then return false end
    if Gift.MaxWeight and info.Weight > Gift.MaxWeight then return false end
    return true, info
end
local function sendGift(tool)
    local targetId, targetError = resolveGiftTarget()
    if not targetId then return false, targetError end
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return false, "No valid tool selected" end
    local character = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid and tool.Parent ~= character then
        humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
        task.wait(0.08)
    end
    local remote = getGiftRemote("Gifting: Send Request")
    if not remote or not remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
        return false, "Gifting send remote not found"
    end
    local ok, result = pcall(remote.InvokeServer, remote, targetId)
    if (not ok or result == false) then
        local targetPlayer = App.Services.Players:GetPlayerByUserId(targetId) or findGiftPlayer(Gift.TargetName)
        if targetPlayer then
            ok, result = pcall(remote.InvokeServer, remote, targetPlayer)
        end
    end
    return ok and result ~= false, ok and result or "Gift request failed"
end
local function stopGiftLoops()
    Gift.AutoAll = false
    Gift.AutoFiltered = false
    Gift.AutoSelected = false
    Gift.LoopVersion = Gift.LoopVersion + 1
end
local function updateGiftLoops()
    if Gift.AutoAll or Gift.AutoFiltered or Gift.AutoSelected then
        Gift.LoopVersion = Gift.LoopVersion + 1
        local version = Gift.LoopVersion
        task.spawn(function()
            while not App.Runtime.Unloading and version == Gift.LoopVersion
                and (Gift.AutoAll or Gift.AutoFiltered or Gift.AutoSelected)
            do
                local tools = getGiftTools()
                for _, tool in ipairs(tools) do
                    if version ~= Gift.LoopVersion then break end
                    local shouldSend = Gift.AutoAll
                    if Gift.AutoFiltered then shouldSend = giftMatches(tool) end
                    if Gift.AutoSelected then shouldSend = tool == Gift.SelectedTool end
                    if shouldSend then
                        sendGift(tool)
                        task.wait(math.max(0.05, Gift.Delay))
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)
    else
        stopGiftLoops()
    end
end
local function attachGiftRequestListener()
    local network = App.Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
    if not network then return end
    local function connectRequestRemote()
        local remote = network:FindFirstChild("Gifting: Request")
        if not remote or not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return end
        if Gift.RequestConnection then Gift.RequestConnection:Disconnect() end
        Gift.RequestConnection = remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(senderName, senderId, itemName, requestUid)
            Gift.LastRequest = {
                SenderName = tostring(senderName or "Unknown"),
                SenderId = tonumber(senderId),
                ItemName = tostring(itemName or "Unknown item"),
                RequestUid = tostring(requestUid or ""),
            }
            if giftRequestLabel and type(giftRequestLabel.SetText) == "function" then
                pcall(giftRequestLabel.SetText, giftRequestLabel, string.format("Item: %s\nFrom: %s\nStatus: Waiting", Gift.LastRequest.ItemName, Gift.LastRequest.SenderName))
            end
            if Gift.AutoAccept
                and (not Gift.OnlyAcceptTarget or tonumber(senderId) == tonumber(Gift.TargetUserId))
            then
                local response = getGiftRemote("Gifting: Response")
                if response and response:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    local accepted, result = pcall(response.InvokeServer, response, senderId, tostring(requestUid or ""), true)
                    if giftRequestLabel and type(giftRequestLabel.SetText) == "function" then
                        pcall(giftRequestLabel.SetText, giftRequestLabel, string.format("Item: %s\nFrom: %s\nStatus: %s", Gift.LastRequest.ItemName, Gift.LastRequest.SenderName, accepted and tostring(result or "Accepted") or "Accept failed"))
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    connectRequestRemote()
    if not Gift.NetworkConnection then
        Gift.NetworkConnection = network.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "Gifting: Request" then
                connectRequestRemote()
            end
        end)
    end
end
local rarityValues = {
    "Basic",
    "Common",
    "Uncommon",
    "Rare",
    "SuperRare",
    "Epic",
    "Legendary",
    "Mythic",
    "Mythical",
    "Cosmic",
    "Secret",
    "Eternal",
    "Divine",
    "Celestial",
    "Exotic",
    "Superior",
    "BrainrotGod",
    "Rainbow",
    "Prismatic",
    "Transcendent",
    "Exclusive",
    "Limited",
    "Admin",
}
do
    local seenRarities = {}
    local orderedRarities = {}
    for _, rarity in ipairs(rarityValues) do
        if not seenRarities[rarity] then
            seenRarities[rarity] = true
            table.insert(orderedRarities, rarity)
        end
    end
    local ok, directoryModule = pcall(function()
        return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Directory.Rarity)
    end)
    local directory = ok and type(directoryModule) == "table" and directoryModule.Rarities or nil
    if type(directory) == "table" then
        for rarity in pairs(directory) do
            rarity = tostring(rarity)
            if not seenRarities[rarity] then
                seenRarities[rarity] = true
                table.insert(orderedRarities, rarity)
            end
        end
    end
    rarityValues = orderedRarities
end

local function simulateSlotEgg(periodIndex, areaName, slotName, areasDir, assetsDir, lotteryFn, resetTimeUtil)
    local areaConfig = areasDir[areaName]
    if not areaConfig or not areaConfig.DropTable then return nil end

    local seed = resetTimeUtil.GetSlotSeed(periodIndex, areaName, slotName)
    local rng = Random.new(seed)
    local petName = lotteryFn(rng, areaConfig.DropTable)
    if not petName then return nil end

    local assetData = assetsDir[petName]
    local rarityObj = assetData and assetData.Rarity
    local rarityName = (rarityObj and rarityObj.DisplayName) or "Common"
    local rarityNumber = (rarityObj and rarityObj.RarityNumber) or 1

    return {
        petName = petName,
        area = areaName,
        slot = slotName,
        rarity = rarityName,
        rarityNumber = rarityNumber
    }
end

local function formatPredictDuration(totalSeconds)
    totalSeconds = math.max(0, math.floor(totalSeconds))
    local days = math.floor(totalSeconds / 86400)
    local hours = math.floor((totalSeconds % 86400) / 3600)
    local mins = math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60)
    if days > 0 then
        return string.format("%dd %dh %dm", days, hours, mins)
    elseif hours > 0 then
        return string.format("%dh %dm", hours, mins)
    else
        return string.format("%dm %02ds", mins, totalSeconds % 60)
    end
end

local EggPanelReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local EggPanelAssets = require(EggPanelReplicatedStorage.Directory.Assets).Directory
local EggPanelAreas = require(EggPanelReplicatedStorage.Directory.Areas).Directory
local EggPanelResetTimeUtil = require(EggPanelReplicatedStorage.Library.Util.AreaEggResetTimeUtil)
local EggPanelLotteryCustom = require(EggPanelReplicatedStorage.Library.Functions.LotteryCustom)
local EggPanelEggCmds = require(EggPanelReplicatedStorage.Library.Client.EggCmds)

local PREDICT_AREAS = {
    "Forest", "Lake", "Desert", "Jungle", "Snow", "Volcano", "Abyss Ocean", "Prehistoric", "Cosmic"
}



local PREDICT_SLOTS = { "Slot_001", "Slot_002", "Slot_003", "Slot_004", "Slot_005" }
local MAX_PREDICT_PERIODS_AHEAD = 2016

local function eggPanelSimulateSlot(periodIndex, areaName, slotName)
    return simulateSlotEgg(periodIndex, areaName, slotName, EggPanelAreas, EggPanelAssets, EggPanelLotteryCustom, EggPanelResetTimeUtil)
end




local function buildRarityCatalog()
    local seen = {}
    local list = {}
    for _, assetData in pairs(EggPanelAssets) do
        local r = assetData.Rarity
        if r and r.DisplayName and not seen[r.DisplayName] then
            seen[r.DisplayName] = true
            table.insert(list, { name = r.DisplayName, color = r.Color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), order = r.RarityNumber or 0 })
        end
    end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.order > b.order end)
    return list
end
local RarityCatalog = buildRarityCatalog()
local RarityColorByName = { Any = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) }
for _, r in ipairs(RarityCatalog) do RarityColorByName[r.name] = r.color end

local MUTATION_COLORS = {
    Rainbow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 255),
    Golden = Color3.fromRGB(255, 234, 0),
    Silver = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220),
}

local function getEggLogEntries()
    local entries = {}
    local snapshot = EggPanelEggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
    if snapshot and snapshot.Records then
        for _, record in pairs(snapshot.Records) do
            if record.State == "Slot" or record.State == "Dropped" then
                local assetData = EggPanelAssets[record.AssetCategory]
                if assetData and assetData.Egg then
                    local areaData = EggPanelAreas[record.AreaId]
                    local mutationText = nil
                    if record.Mutations and #record.Mutations > 0 then
                        mutationText = table.concat(record.Mutations, " + ")
                    end
                    table.insert(entries, {
                        uid = record.Uid,
                        petName = assetData.DisplayName,
                        eggName = assetData.Egg.DisplayName,
                        eggIcon = assetData.Egg.Icon,
                        weightKg = assetData.Egg.WeightKg,
                        rarityName = (assetData.Rarity and assetData.Rarity.DisplayName) or "Common",
                        rarityOrder = (assetData.Rarity and assetData.Rarity.RarityNumber) or 0,
                        mutationText = mutationText,
                        mutationColor = MUTATION_COLORS[record.BaseMutation] or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                        area = (areaData and areaData.DisplayName) or record.AreaId,
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(entries, function(a, b)
        if a.rarityOrder ~= b.rarityOrder then
            return a.rarityOrder > b.rarityOrder
        end
        return a.petName < b.petName
    end)
    return entries
end




local function findUpcomingRarityAppearances(rarityFilter, searchText, startPeriod, maxResults, maxPeriodsAhead)
    local results = {}
    local searchLower = string.lower(searchText or "")
    for i = 1, maxPeriodsAhead do
        if i % 50 == 0 then task.wait() end
        local period = startPeriod + i
        for _, areaName in ipairs(PREDICT_AREAS) do
            for _, slotName in ipairs(PREDICT_SLOTS) do
                local egg = eggPanelSimulateSlot(period, areaName, slotName)
                if egg then
                    local rarityOk = (rarityFilter == "Any") or (egg.rarity == rarityFilter)
                    local searchOk = (searchLower == "") or string.find(string.lower(egg.petName), searchLower, 1, true)
                    if rarityOk and searchOk then
                        table.insert(results, {
                            petName = egg.petName,
                            area = egg.area,
                            slot = egg.slot,
                            rarity = egg.rarity,
                            releaseAt = EggPanelResetTimeUtil.GetPeriodStartsAt(period),
                        })
                        if #results >= maxResults then return results end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return results
end

local function setImage(imageObject, icon)
    local image = icon and tostring(icon) or "rbxassetid://6031075931"
    if image:match("^%d+$") then image = "rbxassetid://" .. image end
    imageObject.Image = image
end

local predictionEntries = {}

local function updateEggPredictionTimers()
    local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    for index = 1, 8 do
        local entry = predictionEntries[index]
        local card = predictionCards[index]
        if entry and card then
            card.Status.Text = string.format("%s  |  %s  |  in %s", tostring(entry.rarity), tostring(entry.area), formatPredictDuration(math.max(0, entry.releaseAt - now)))
        end
    end
end

function refreshEggPredictionDisplay()
    local ok, entries, now = pcall(function()
        local currentNow = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
        local period = EggPanelResetTimeUtil.GetPeriodIndex(currentNow)
        return findUpcomingRarityAppearances(selectedPredictionRarity, "", period, 8, MAX_PREDICT_PERIODS_AHEAD), currentNow
    end)
    if not ok or type(entries) ~= "table" then return end
    predictionEntries = entries
    for index = 1, 8 do
        local card = predictionCards[index]
        local entry = entries[index]
        if entry then
            local asset = EggPanelAssets[entry.petName]
            setImage(card.Image, asset and asset.Egg and asset.Egg.Icon or entry.eggIcon)
            card.Name.Text = tostring(entry.petName)
            card.Status.Text = string.format("%s  |  %s  |  in %s", tostring(entry.rarity), tostring(entry.area), formatPredictDuration(math.max(0, entry.releaseAt - now)))
            card.Name.TextColor3 = RarityColorByName[entry.rarity] or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        else
            card.Image.Image = "rbxassetid://6031075931"
            card.Name.Text = "No prediction"
            card.Status.Text = ""
        end
    end
end

local function refreshEggLogsDisplay()
    local ok, entries = pcall(getEggLogEntries)
    if not ok or type(entries) ~= "table" then return end
    for index = 1, 8 do
        local card = logCards[index]
        local entry = entries[index]
        if entry then
            setImage(card.Image, entry.eggIcon)
            card.Name.Text = tostring(entry.eggName)
            card.Status.Text = string.format("%s  |  %s  |  %s", tostring(entry.rarityName), tostring(entry.area), tostring(entry.petName))
            card.Name.TextColor3 = RarityColorByName[entry.rarityName] or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        else
            card.Image.Image = "rbxassetid://6031075931"
            card.Name.Text = "No egg log"
            card.Status.Text = ""
        end
    end
end

refreshEggPredictionDisplay()
refreshEggLogsDisplay()
task.spawn(function()
    local refreshClock = 0
    while not App.Runtime.Unloading do
        task.wait(1)
        if App.Runtime.Unloading then break end
        updateEggPredictionTimers()
        refreshClock = refreshClock + 1
        if refreshClock >= 10 then
            refreshClock = 0
            refreshEggPredictionDisplay()
            refreshEggLogsDisplay()
        end
    end
end)

    Text = "Gift Item Filter",
    Values = {"All Items"},
    Default = "All Items",
    Callback = function(value) Gift.FilterItem = value or "All Items" end,

local function refreshGiftTools()
    local values = {}
    local itemValues = {"All Items"}
    local seenItems = {}
    for _, tool in ipairs(getGiftTools()) do
        local info = getGiftInfo(tool)
        if info then
            table.insert(values, string.format("%s [%s] (%s) - %.1f KG%s", info.DisplayName, info.Mutation, info.Rarity, info.Weight, info.Favorite and " [Favorite]" or ""))
            local itemKey = tostring(info.DisplayName)
            if not seenItems[itemKey] then
                seenItems[itemKey] = true
                table.insert(itemValues, itemKey)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(itemValues, function(left, right)
        if left == "All Items" then return true end
        if right == "All Items" then return false end
        return left:lower() < right:lower()
    end)
    if #values == 0 then values = {"No giftable tools found"} end
    if giftToolDropdown and type(giftToolDropdown.SetValues) == "function" then
        pcall(giftToolDropdown.SetValues, giftToolDropdown, values)
    end
    if giftItemDropdown and type(giftItemDropdown.SetValues) == "function" then
        pcall(giftItemDropdown.SetValues, giftItemDropdown, itemValues)
    end
    return values
end
local function selectedGiftTool(value)
    for _, tool in ipairs(getGiftTools()) do
        local info = getGiftInfo(tool)
        if info then
            local label = string.format("%s [%s] (%s) - %.1f KG%s", info.DisplayName, info.Mutation, info.Rarity, info.Weight, info.Favorite and " [Favorite]" or "")
            if label == value then return tool end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

    Text = "Gift Item",
    Values = {"No giftable tools found"},
    Default = "No giftable tools found",
    Callback = function(value)
        Gift.SelectedTool = selectedGiftTool(value)
    end,
})

    Text = "Only Accept Target",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(value) Gift.OnlyAcceptTarget = value == true end,

local function respondToLastGift(accepted)
    local request = Gift.LastRequest
    local response = request and getGiftRemote("Gifting: Response")
    if not request or not response or not response:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
        notify("Gift", "No response request is available", 2)
        return
    end
    local ok, result = pcall(response.InvokeServer, response, request.SenderId, request.RequestUid, accepted == true)
    if giftRequestLabel and type(giftRequestLabel.SetText) == "function" then
        pcall(giftRequestLabel.SetText, giftRequestLabel, string.format("Item: %s\nFrom: %s\nStatus: %s", request.ItemName, request.SenderName, ok and tostring(result or (accepted and "Accepted" or "Declined")) or "Request failed"))
    end
    notify("Gift", ok and (accepted and "Gift accepted" or "Gift declined") or tostring(result or "Response failed"), 2)
end
GiftReceiveBox:AddButton({
    Text = "Accept Last Gift",
    Func = function() respondToLastGift(true) end,
})
GiftReceiveBox:AddButton({
    Text = "Decline Last Gift",
    Func = function() respondToLastGift(false) end,
})
GiftReceiveBox:AddButton({
    Text = "Refresh Gift Filters",
    Func = function()
        local mutations, rarities = {}, {}
        local seenMutations, seenRarities = {}, {}
        for _, tool in ipairs(getGiftTools()) do
            local info = getGiftInfo(tool)
            if info then
                if not seenMutations[info.Mutation] then seenMutations[info.Mutation] = true; table.insert(mutations, info.Mutation) end
                if not seenRarities[info.Rarity] then seenRarities[info.Rarity] = true; table.insert(rarities, info.Rarity) end
            end
        end
        table.sort(mutations); table.sort(rarities)
        table.insert(mutations, 1, "All Mutations")
        table.insert(rarities, 1, "All Rarities")
        if giftMutationDropdown and type(giftMutationDropdown.SetValues) == "function" then pcall(giftMutationDropdown.SetValues, giftMutationDropdown, mutations) end
        if giftRarityDropdown and type(giftRarityDropdown.SetValues) == "function" then pcall(giftRarityDropdown.SetValues, giftRarityDropdown, rarities) end
        refreshGiftTools()
        notify("Gift", "Gift filters refreshed", 2)
    end,
})
local TradeBox = GiftTab:AddRightGroupbox("Trading", "repeat", true, true, false)
local TradeTabs = TradeBox:AddTabbox1({Name = "Trade Modes", Side = 1})
local TradeSelectionTab = TradeTabs:AddTab("Trade Selection", "users")
local TradeResponseTab = TradeTabs:AddTab("Auto Response", "shield")
local tradePlayerDropdown
local tradeItemDropdown
local function getTradePlayerOptions()
    local values = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(App.Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= App.LocalPlayer then
            table.insert(values, player.Name)
        end
    end
    table.sort(values)
    if #values == 0 then values[1] = "No players found" end
    return values
end
local function getTradeItemOptions()
    local values = {}
    for _, tool in ipairs(getGiftTools()) do
        local info = getGiftInfo(tool)
        if info then
            local category = tostring(getToolValue(tool, {"Category", "AssetCategory"}) or info.Name)
            local itemId = getToolValue(tool, {"UID", "Uid", "ID", "Id", "AssetId", "UniqueId"}) or info.Name
            local label = string.format("%s [%s] - %s", info.DisplayName, info.Rarity, tostring(itemId))
            table.insert(values, label)
        end
    end
    if #values == 0 then values[1] = "No tradeable items found" end
    return values
end
local function selectTradeItem(value)
    for _, tool in ipairs(getGiftTools()) do
        local info = getGiftInfo(tool)
        if info then
            local category = tostring(getToolValue(tool, {"Category", "AssetCategory"}) or info.Name)
            local itemId = getToolValue(tool, {"UID", "Uid", "ID", "Id", "AssetId", "UniqueId"}) or info.Name
            local label = string.format("%s [%s] - %s", info.DisplayName, info.Rarity, tostring(itemId))
            if label == value then
                App.Runtime.TradeSelectedName = info.DisplayName
                App.Runtime.TradeSelectedCategory = category
                App.Runtime.TradeSelectedItemId = itemId
                App.Runtime.TradeSelectedUid = tostring(itemId)
                return
            end
        end
    end
    App.Runtime.TradeSelectedCategory = nil
    App.Runtime.TradeSelectedItemId = nil
    App.Runtime.TradeSelectedUid = nil
end
tradePlayerDropdown = TradeSelectionTab:AddDropdown("TradePlayerSelection", {
    Text = "Specific Player",
    Values = getTradePlayerOptions(),
    Default = "No players found",
    Callback = function(value)
        local player = value and findGiftPlayer(value)
        App.Runtime.TradeTargetName = tostring(value or "")
        App.Runtime.TradeTargetUserId = player and player.UserId or nil
        if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoChooseTradePlayer then
            startAutoChooseTradePlayer()
        end
    end,
})

    Text = "Refresh Players",
    Func = function()
        local values = getTradePlayerOptions()
        if tradePlayerDropdown and type(tradePlayerDropdown.SetValues) == "function" then
            pcall(tradePlayerDropdown.SetValues, tradePlayerDropdown, values)
        end
    end,
})

local EggsBox = Tab:AddLeftGroupbox("Eggs", "egg", true, true, false)

    Text = "Area Egg Selection",
    Values = eggOptions,
    Default = eggOptions[1],
    Callback = function(value)
        local label = tostring(value or "")
        local info = App.Runtime.SelectedEggOptionLookup[label]
        if type(info) == "table" then
            App.setSelectedAreaEgg(info)
            notify("Egg Selection", tostring(info.Name))
        end
    end,
})
if type(eggOptionLookup[eggOptions[1]]) == "table" then
    App.setSelectedAreaEgg(eggOptionLookup[eggOptions[1]])
end
local function refreshEggOptions()
    local ready, initError = App.initGame()
    if not ready then
        notify("Eggs", tostring(initError or "Game modules are not ready."))
        return
    end
    local values, lookup = App.getAreaEggOptions()
    if #values == 0 then values = {"No eggs found"} end
    App.Runtime.SelectedEggOptions = values
    App.Runtime.SelectedEggOptionLookup = lookup
    if eggDropdown and type(eggDropdown.SetValues) == "function" then
        pcall(eggDropdown.SetValues, eggDropdown, values)
    end
    local selectedInfo = lookup[tostring(values[1] or "")]
    if type(selectedInfo) == "table" then App.setSelectedAreaEgg(selectedInfo) end
    notify("Eggs", #lookup > 0 and "Area egg list refreshed." or "No area eggs found.")
end
EggsBox:AddButton({
    Text = "Refresh Eggs",
    Func = refreshEggOptions,
})

local WeatherBox = Tab:AddLeftGroupbox("Weather Prediction", "cloud-sun", true, true, false)
local WeatherTabs = WeatherBox:AddTabbox1({Name = "Weather", Side = 1})
local WeatherTab = WeatherTabs:AddTab("Forecast", "cloud-sun")
local WeatherCardSerial = 0
local function addWeatherDisplayLabel(text, icon)
    WeatherCardSerial = WeatherCardSerial + 1
    local root = Instance.new("Frame")
    root.Name = "WeatherCard" .. tostring(WeatherCardSerial)
    root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    root.BorderSizePixel = 0
    root.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 62)
    local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    image.Name = "WeatherIcon"
    image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    image.BorderSizePixel = 0
    image.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34)
    image.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -17)
    image.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    image.Image = icon or ""
    image.Parent = root
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "WeatherText"
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 0)
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 1, 0)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    label.TextSize = 11
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
    label.TextWrapped = true
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.Text = tostring(text or "")
    label.Parent = root
    pcall(function()
        WeatherTab:AddUIPassthrough("WeatherCard" .. tostring(WeatherCardSerial), {Instance = root, Height = 62})
    end)
    return root
end
WeatherTab:AddToggle("WeatherPrediction", {
    Text = "Live Weather Prediction",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)
        App.Runtime.Enabled.WeatherPrediction = enabled == true
        if enabled then
            App.startWeatherPrediction(addWeatherDisplayLabel)
        else
            App.stopWeatherPrediction()
        end
    end,
})
WeatherTab:AddButton({
    Text = "Refresh Weather",
    Func = function()
        App.renderWeatherPrediction(addWeatherDisplayLabel)
    end,
})
