local StealingBox = Tab:AddRightGroupbox("Stealing", "swords", true, true, false)
local stealAreaOptions = App.getIndexAreaOptions()
table.insert(stealAreaOptions, 1, "Any Area")
local stealAreaDropdown
local function refreshStealAreaDropdown()
    local values = App.getIndexAreaOptions()
    table.insert(values, 1, "Any Area")
    stealAreaOptions = values
    if stealAreaDropdown and type(stealAreaDropdown.SetValues) == "function" then
        pcall(stealAreaDropdown.SetValues, stealAreaDropdown, values)
    end
end
stealAreaDropdown = StealingBox:AddDropdown("StealSpecificArea", {
    Text = "Specific Steal Area",
    Values = stealAreaOptions,
    Default = "Any Area",
    Callback = function(value)
        local label = tostring(value or "Any Area")
        if label == "Any Area" then
            App.Runtime.StealAreaEnabled = false
            App.Runtime.SelectedStealAreaId = nil
            App.Runtime.SelectedStealAreaName = nil
            return
        end
        local areaId = App.Runtime.IndexSystem.AreaOptions[label]
        if areaId then
            App.Runtime.SelectedStealAreaId = tostring(areaId)
            App.Runtime.SelectedStealAreaName = label
            App.Runtime.StealAreaEnabled = true
        end
    end,
})
task.defer(refreshStealAreaDropdown)
StealingBox:AddToggle("StealSpecificAreaEnabled", {
    Text = "Steal Specific Area Only",
    Description = "Uses the selected area instead of requiring a rarity choice.",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)
        App.Runtime.StealAreaEnabled = enabled == true
        if not enabled then
            App.Runtime.SelectedStealAreaId = nil
            App.Runtime.SelectedStealAreaName = nil
        end
    end,
})

TweenToPoint = function(targetPos, speed, arriveRadius, timeout)
    local chr = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    if not chr then return false, "no char" end
    local hrp = chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = chr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then return false, "no hum" end
    local sp = math.max(16, speed or 400)
    hum.WalkSpeed = sp
    local arrive = arriveRadius or 10
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while not App.Runtime.Unloading do
        local now = hrp.Position
        local dx, dz = targetPos.X - now.X, targetPos.Z - now.Z
        local dist = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz)
        if dist <= arrive then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            return true
        end
        if os.clock() - t0 > (timeout or 90) then return false, "timeout" end
        if hum.Health <= 0 then return false, "died" end
        local goal = CFrame.new(targetPos.X, now.Y, targetPos.Z) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
        local duration = math.clamp(dist / sp, 0.05, 60)
        local tween = App.Services.TweenService:Create(hrp, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), { CFrame = goal })
        local finished = false
        local interruptedByTrap = false
        local conn = tween.Completed:Connect(function() finished = true end)
        tween:Play()
        local tT0 = os.clock()
        while not finished and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
            if hum.Health <= 0 then
                tween:Cancel(); conn:Disconnect()
                return false, "died"
            end
            if not (chr.Parent and hrp.Parent) then
                tween:Cancel(); conn:Disconnect()
                return false, "no char"
            end
            if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoAvoidTrap then
                local trapPosition = App.isTrapOnPath(hrp.Position, targetPos)
                if trapPosition then
                    interruptedByTrap = true
                    tween:Cancel()
                    break
                end
            end
            if os.clock() - tT0 > duration + 5 then
                tween:Cancel(); conn:Disconnect()
                return false, "tween stuck"
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        conn:Disconnect()
        if interruptedByTrap then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            if App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoAvoidTrap then
                local trapPosition = App.isTrapOnPath(hrp.Position, targetPos)
                if trapPosition then
                    local safePoint = App.getSafeTrapPoint(hrp.Position, trapPosition)
                    App.moveHumanoidTo(
                        nil,
                        nil,
                        safePoint,
                        true
                    )
                    while App.Runtime.Enabled.AutoAvoidTrap and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
                        if not App.isTrapOnPath(hrp.Position, targetPos) then
                            break
                        end
                        task.wait(0.2)
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            if not finished then
                tween:Cancel()
                return false, "dead"
            end
        end
    end
    return false, "dead"
end

local function WaitForStanding(timeout)
    local chr = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    local hum = chr and chr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return false end
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while os.clock() - t0 < (timeout or 10) do
        local st = hum:GetState()
        if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
            return true
        end
        task.wait(0.15)
    end
    return false
end

local gateFlag = false
local gateFlagChar = nil
local function WalkThroughGate()
    local chr = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    if not chr then return false end
    local hrp = chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = chr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then return false end
    if hrp.Position.X > 570 then return true end
    local crossed = false
    WaitForStanding(10)
    if hrp.Position.X > 570 then
        crossed = true
    else
        hum.WalkSpeed = 300
        local gy = hrp.Position.Y
        hum:MoveTo(Vector3.new(546, gy, -370))
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while not App.Runtime.Unloading and os.clock() - t0 < 12 do
            if (hrp.Position - Vector3.new(546, gy, -370)).Magnitude < 6 then break end
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
        hum:MoveTo(Vector3.new(585, gy, -366))
        t0 = os.clock()
        while not App.Runtime.Unloading and os.clock() - t0 < 12 do
            if hrp.Position.X > 575 then break end
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
        hum.WalkSpeed = 16
        crossed = hrp.Position.X > 570
    end
    return crossed
end

EnsureGameplayFlag = function()
    local chr = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    if chr ~= gateFlagChar then
        gateFlagChar = chr
        gateFlag = false
    end
    if gateFlag then return true end
    local ok = WalkThroughGate()
    if ok then gateFlag = true end
    return ok
end

IsCarryingEgg = function()
    local p = App.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if p and p:FindFirstChild("DropHeldEgg") then return true end
    local chr = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    if chr then
        for _, c in ipairs(chr:GetChildren()) do
            if string.find(c.Name, "Egg", 1, true) and (c:IsA("Tool") or c:IsA("Model")) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    if App.Runtime.CarryState and App.Runtime.CarryState.IsCarrying then return true end
    return false
end

FindEggPrompt = function(pos)
    local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
    for _, v in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and v.Name == "CarryAreaEgg" then
            local pp = v.Parent
            local okp, p = pcall(function() return pp.Position end)
            if okp then
                local d = (p - pos).Magnitude
                if d < bestD then
                    best, bestD = v, d
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

HardStop = function()
    local chr = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    if chr then
        local hrp = chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end
    task.wait(0.2)
end

local function GetBasePos()
    -- use App.getPlotReturnPosition() if available, else fallback
    if App.getPlotReturnPosition then
        local pos, err = App.getPlotReturnPosition()
        if typeof(pos) == "Vector3" then return pos end
    end
    local chr = App.LocalPlayer.Character
    if chr and chr.PrimaryPart then return chr.PrimaryPart.Position end
    return Vector3.new(0,0,0)
end

local function SetStealTreadmillHidden(hidden)
    for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") then
            local isTm = false
            local anc = d
            while anc do
                if string.find(anc.Name:lower(), "treadmill") then
                    isTm = true
                    break
                end
                anc = anc.Parent
            end
            if isTm then
                if hidden then
                    d.CanCollide = false
                    d.CanQuery = false
                    d.CanTouch = false
                    d.Transparency = 1
                else
                    d.CanCollide = true
                    d.CanQuery = true
                    d.CanTouch = true
                    d.Transparency = 0
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local SettingsBox = Tab:AddLeftGroupbox("Settings", "settings", true, true, false)
local function setEspMode(mode, enabled)
    App.Runtime.EspModes[mode] = enabled == true
    local anyEnabled = false
    for mode, active in pairs(App.Runtime.EspModes) do
        if mode ~= "IncomeMoney" and active then
            anyEnabled = true
            break
        end
    end
    if anyEnabled then
        App.startEsp()
    else
        App.stopEsp()
    end
end
local EggMonitorEnabled = false
local EggMonitorRunning = false
local EggMonitorFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("EggMonitorESP") or Instance.new("Folder")
EggMonitorFolder.Name = "EggMonitorESP"
EggMonitorFolder.Parent = workspace
local EggMonitorRenderer = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Library.Client.Eggs.PlacedEggRenderer)
local EggMonitorCmds = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Library.Client.EggCmds)
local EggMonitorUtil = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Library.Util.EggItemUtil)
local EggMonitorEntries = {}
local EggMonitorModelCache = {}
local EggMonitorRendererFolder = nil
local EggMonitorRecordsCache = {}
local EggMonitorNextSnapshotAt = 0
local EggMonitorNextRecordAt = 0
local EggMonitorFolderScanAt = 0
local EggMonitorFolderModels = {}
local EggMonitorNextModelScanAt = {}
    EggMonitorRendererFolder = nil
    EggMonitorFolderModels = {}
    EggMonitorFolderScanAt = 0
local function eggMonitorTime(seconds)
    seconds = math.max(0, math.floor((tonumber(seconds) or 0) + 0.5))
    return string.format("%02d:%02d", math.floor(seconds / 60), seconds % 60)
end
local function eggMonitorMutationLabel(mutations)
    if type(mutations) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local names = {}
    for _, mutation in pairs(mutations) do
        if type(mutation) == "string" then
            table.insert(names, mutation)
        elseif type(mutation) == "table" then
            local name = mutation.Name or mutation.DisplayName or mutation._id
            if name then
                table.insert(names, tostring(name))
            end
        end
    end
    return #names > 0 and table.concat(names, ", ") or nil
end
local function eggMonitorRenderFolder()
    local now = os.clock()
    if EggMonitorRendererFolder and EggMonitorRendererFolder.Parent and now < EggMonitorFolderScanAt then
        return EggMonitorRendererFolder
    end
    if now < EggMonitorFolderScanAt then return EggMonitorRendererFolder end
    EggMonitorFolderScanAt = now + 3
    local ok, folder = pcall(function() return EggMonitorRenderer.GetRenderFolder() end)
    if ok and folder then
        EggMonitorRendererFolder = folder
        EggMonitorFolderModels = {}
        for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            EggMonitorFolderModels[tostring(child.Name)] = child
        end
        return folder
    end
    return EggMonitorRendererFolder
end
local function eggMonitorMatchesModel(model, uid)
    if not model then
        return false
    end
    local uidString = tostring(uid)
    local ownerString = tostring(App.LocalPlayer.UserId)
    local name = tostring(model.Name)
    if name == ownerString .. "_" .. uidString
        or name == ownerString .. ":" .. uidString
        or name == uidString
        or name:sub(-#("_" .. uidString)) == "_" .. uidString
        or name:sub(-#((":" .. uidString))) == ":" .. uidString
    then
        return true
    end
    local modelUid = model:GetAttribute("Uid") or model:GetAttribute("UID") or model:GetAttribute("EggUid")
    local modelOwner = model:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId") or model:GetAttribute("OwnerUserID") or model:GetAttribute("Owner")
    if modelUid ~= nil and tostring(modelUid) == uidString then
        return modelOwner == nil or tostring(modelOwner) == ownerString
    end
    return false
end
local function eggMonitorRenderModel(uid)
    local uidString = tostring(uid)
    local cached = EggMonitorModelCache[uidString]
    if cached and cached.Parent then return cached end
    local folder = eggMonitorRenderFolder()
    if folder then
        local ownerString = tostring(App.LocalPlayer.UserId)
        local directNames = { ownerString .. ":" .. uidString, ownerString .. "_" .. uidString, uidString }
        for _, name in ipairs(directNames) do
            local direct = EggMonitorFolderModels[name] or folder:FindFirstChild(name)
            if direct then
                EggMonitorModelCache[uidString] = direct
                return direct
            end
        end
        local now = os.clock()
        if now >= (EggMonitorNextModelScanAt[uidString] or 0) then
            EggMonitorNextModelScanAt[uidString] = now + 3
            for _, candidate in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                local candidateName = tostring(candidate.Name)
                EggMonitorFolderModels[candidateName] = candidate
                if eggMonitorMatchesModel(candidate, uidString) then
                    EggMonitorModelCache[uidString] = candidate
                    return candidate
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local exactModel = App.getAreaEggModel({ Uid = uidString })
    if exactModel then
        EggMonitorModelCache[uidString] = exactModel
        return exactModel
    end
    return nil
end
local function eggMonitorModelPart(model)
    if not model then return nil end
    if model:IsA("BasePart") then return model end
    if model:IsA("Model") then return model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) end
    return model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
local function eggMonitorGrowthInfo(record)
    local placement = record.Placement
    if placement and placement.ReadyAt ~= nil then
        return 0, true
    end
    local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    local speed = tonumber(record.GrowthSpeedMultiplier) or 1
    local creditOk, credit = App.callModuleMethod(
        EggMonitorUtil,
        "GetCurrentNightGrowthCreditSeconds",
        record,
        now,
        speed
    )
    if not creditOk then credit = 0 end
    local remainingOk, remaining = App.callModuleMethod(
        EggMonitorUtil,
        "GetRemainingGrowthSeconds",
        record,
        now,
        speed,
        credit,
        App.LocalPlayer
    )
    if not remainingOk then return 0, false end
    remaining = math.max(0, tonumber(remaining) or 0)
    return remaining, remaining <= 0
end
local function createEggMonitorEntry(id, part, record)
    local uidString = tostring(id)
    local state = EggMonitorEntries[uidString]
    local billboard = state and state.Billboard
    if not billboard or not billboard.Parent then
        billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        billboard.Name = "EggMonitorESP_" .. uidString
        billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        billboard.LightInfluence = 0
        billboard.MaxDistance = 250
        billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 55)
        billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
        billboard.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
        billboard.Parent = EggMonitorFolder
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Name = "EggMonitorText"
        label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.BorderSizePixel = 0
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        label.TextSize = 15
        label.TextWrapped = true
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        label.ZIndex = 100
        label.Parent = billboard
        state = { Billboard = billboard, LastText = nil, LastColor = nil, LastPart = nil }
        EggMonitorEntries[uidString] = state
    end
    if state.LastPart ~= part then
        billboard.Adornee = part
        state.LastPart = part
    end
    local remaining, ready = eggMonitorGrowthInfo(record)
    local species = tostring(record.AssetCategory or record.Name or "?")
    local mutation = eggMonitorMutationLabel(record.Mutations)
    local sourceMutation = App.getAreaEggMutationKey(record)
    if sourceMutation and sourceMutation ~= "None" then mutation = sourceMutation end
    local name = mutation and (species .. " [" .. tostring(mutation) .. "]") or species
    local label = billboard:FindFirstChild("EggMonitorText")
    if label then
        local color = ready and Color3.fromRGB(70, 255, 90) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 70)
        local display = ready and (name .. "\nREADY TO HATCH") or (name .. "\n" .. eggMonitorTime(remaining))
        if state.LastColor ~= color then
            label.TextColor3 = color
            state.LastColor = color
        end
        if state.LastText ~= display then
            label.Text = display
            state.LastText = display
        end
    end
end
local function clearEggMonitorEntry(uidString)
    local state = EggMonitorEntries[uidString]
    if state and state.Billboard then pcall(function() state.Billboard:Destroy() end) end
    EggMonitorEntries[uidString] = nil
    EggMonitorModelCache[uidString] = nil
    EggMonitorNextModelScanAt[uidString] = nil
end
local function clearEggMonitor()
    for uidString in pairs(EggMonitorEntries) do clearEggMonitorEntry(uidString) end
    for _, child in ipairs(EggMonitorFolder:GetChildren()) do pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end
    EggMonitorEntries = {}
    EggMonitorModelCache = {}
    EggMonitorRecordsCache = {}
    EggMonitorNextModelScanAt = {}
    EggMonitorRendererFolder = nil
    EggMonitorFolderModels = {}
    EggMonitorFolderScanAt = 0
end
local function refreshEggMonitor()
    if not EggMonitorEnabled then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now >= EggMonitorNextSnapshotAt then
        EggMonitorNextSnapshotAt = now + 3
        App.callModuleMethod(EggMonitorCmds, "RequestRuntimeSnapshot")
    end
    if now >= EggMonitorNextRecordAt then
        EggMonitorNextRecordAt = now + 0.75
        local recordsOk, records = App.callModuleMethod(EggMonitorCmds, "GetOwnerRuntimeRecords", App.LocalPlayer.UserId)
        if recordsOk and type(records) == "table" then EggMonitorRecordsCache = records end
    end
    if type(EggMonitorRecordsCache) ~= "table" then return end
    local active = {}
    for uid, record in pairs(EggMonitorRecordsCache) do
        if type(record) == "table" and record.Placement ~= nil then
            local uidString = tostring(uid)
            active[uidString] = true
            local model = eggMonitorRenderModel(uidString)
            local part = eggMonitorModelPart(model)
            if part then pcall(function() createEggMonitorEntry(uidString, part, record) end) end
        end
    end
    for uidString in pairs(EggMonitorEntries) do
        if not active[uidString] then clearEggMonitorEntry(uidString) end
    end
end

SettingsBox:AddToggle("ESPEggMonitor", {
    Text = "ESP Egg Monitor",
    Description = "Shows your placed eggs, mutations, and hatch status.",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(value)
        EggMonitorEnabled = value == true
        if not EggMonitorEnabled then
            clearEggMonitor()
        elseif not EggMonitorRunning then
            EggMonitorRunning = true
            task.spawn(function()
                while EggMonitorEnabled and not App.Runtime.Unloading do
                    pcall(refreshEggMonitor)
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
                EggMonitorRunning = false
            end)
        end
    end,
})

local SettingsTab = Tab
local SettingsLeftBox = SettingsBox
local SaveManager
local saveManagerOk, saveManagerResult = pcall(function()
    local addon = loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/Obsidian/main/addons/SaveManager.lua"))
    return addon and addon()
end)
if saveManagerOk and type(saveManagerResult) == "table" then
    SaveManager = saveManagerResult
    pcall(SaveManager.SetLibrary, SaveManager, Library)
    pcall(SaveManager.IgnoreThemeSettings, SaveManager)
    pcall(SaveManager.SetIgnoreIndexes, SaveManager, {"MenuKeybind"})
    pcall(SaveManager.SetFolder, SaveManager, "StealAnEgg")
    pcall(SaveManager.SetSubFolder, SaveManager, tostring(game.PlaceId))
    pcall(SaveManager.BuildConfigSection, SaveManager, SettingsTab, "save")
    pcall(SaveManager.LoadAutoloadConfig, SaveManager)
else
    SettingsLeftBox:AddLabel("SaveManager unavailable")
end
local ThemeManager
local themeManagerOk, themeManagerResult = pcall(function()
    local addon = loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/Obsidian/main/addons/ThemeManager.lua"))
    return addon and addon()
end)
if themeManagerOk and type(themeManagerResult) == "table" then
    ThemeManager = themeManagerResult
    pcall(ThemeManager.SetLibrary, ThemeManager, Library)
    pcall(ThemeManager.SetFolder, ThemeManager, "StealAnEgg")
    pcall(ThemeManager.ApplyToTab, ThemeManager, SettingsTab)
    pcall(ThemeManager.LoadDefault, ThemeManager)
else
    SettingsLeftBox:AddLabel("ThemeManager unavailable")
end
App.installRejoinMenu()
attachGiftRequestListener()
App.startFeature("ManualCarryReturn")
Window:SelectTab(Tab)
local DraggableLabel = Library:AddDraggableLabel("SAE Premium " .. utf8.char(0xE000) .. " | " .. App.LocalPlayer.Name)
DraggableLabel:SetVisible(true)
local FrameTimer = tick()
local FrameCounter = 0
local FPS = 60
local StatsService = game:GetService("Stats")
App.Runtime.DraggableStatusConnection = App.Services.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    FrameCounter = FrameCounter + 1
    if tick() - FrameTimer >= 1 then
        FPS = FrameCounter
        FrameTimer = tick()
        FrameCounter = 0
    end
    local ping = 0
    pcall(function()
        ping = math.floor(StatsService.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue())
    end)
    DraggableLabel:SetText(("SAE Premium " .. utf8.char(0xE000) .. " | %s | %s FPS | %sms"):format(App.LocalPlayer.Name, math.floor(FPS), ping))
end)
Library:Notify({
    Title = "Steal An Egg",
    Description = "UI ready",
    Duration = 3,
})
end)()
